Anyone who, even with authorization to carry a weapon, carries a weapon in a place open to the public and under conditions likely to disturb public peace and intimidate others, is punishable by imprisonment from two months to six months and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs.

#### TITRE II : CRIMES AND OFFENSES AGAINST THE CONSTITUTION AND

PUBLIC FREEDOMS

##### CHAPITRE 1 : HIGH TREASON AND ATTACK AGAINST THE

CONSTITUTION

###### Article 321-1 :

There is high treason when the President of Faso violates his oath, commits acts contrary to the dignity of his office, is author, co-author or accomplice of serious and characterized violations of human rights or of cession of part of the national territory.

High treason is punishable by life imprisonment.

###### Article 321-2 :

Any conduct violating the Constitution and its principles or any breach, in serious contravention of democratic and republican values, constitutes an attack against the Constitution.

Attack against the Constitution is punishable by imprisonment from twenty-one years to thirty years and a fine from one hundred million (100,000,000) to five hundred million (500,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 321-3 :

Prescription cannot be invoked by the person prosecuted when it is established that by virtue of the functions they held, they were able to influence the course of prosecutions or prevent any prosecution against them.

##### CHAPITRE 2 : OFFENSES OF A RACIAL, REGIONALIST,

RELIGIOUS, SEXIST OR CASTE NATURE

###### Article 322-1 :

Any act of discrimination, any manifestation contrary to freedom of conscience and freedom of worship likely to turn persons against each other, is punishable by imprisonment from one year to ten years and a fine from one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs and prohibition of residence for five years.

###### Article 322-2 :

Any distinction, exclusion, restriction, or preference based on race, ethnicity, color, sex, language, religion, political opinion or any other opinion, national or social origin, fortune, birth that has the purpose or effect of destroying or compromising the recognition, enjoyment or exercise, under conditions of equality, of human rights and fundamental freedoms in the political, economic, social and cultural spheres or in any other sphere of public life, is considered an act of discrimination.

###### Article 322-3 :

Discrimination is punishable by imprisonment from six months to three years and a fine from three hundred thousand (300,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, when it consists of:

˗ refusing to provide a good or service;

˗ hindering the normal exercise of any economic activity; ˗ refusing to hire, sanctioning or dismissing a person; ˗ making any service or advantage subject to a condition based on discrimination.

###### Article 322-4 :

Any public speech or writing that justifies or purports to justify any discrimination as referred to in Article 322-2 above, any hatred, intolerance or violence for any reason whatsoever against a person or group of persons, is punishable by imprisonment from six months to three years and a fine from three hundred thousand (300,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs.

If such speeches or writings have resulted in violence against persons and/or destruction of property, the penalty is from three years to ten years and a fine from five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

The court seized may, in addition, order the confiscation of the media containing such writings or speeches.

###### Article 322-5 :

Non-public incitement to discrimination as referred to in Article 322-2 above, to intolerance, hatred or violence for any reason whatsoever against a person or group of persons, is punishable by imprisonment from two months to one year and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs.

##### CHAPITRE 3 : CRIMES AND OFFENSES RELATING TO THE EXERCISE OF

CIVIC RIGHTS

###### Article 323-1 :

Anyone who, by unlawful assembly, assault or threat, prevents one or more persons from exercising their civic rights, is punishable by imprisonment from six months to two years and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 323-2 :

The penalty is imprisonment from five to ten years and a fine from five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, if the acts are committed pursuant to a concerted plan to be executed either throughout the national territory, or in one or more administrative entities.

##### CHAPITRE 4 : ATTACKS ON FREEDOM

###### Article 324-1 :

Any public official or any other representative of authority who orders or causes to be ordered any arbitrary act or attack either on individual freedom, or on the civic rights of one or more persons, or on the texts in force, is punishable by imprisonment from five years to ten years and a fine from one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs. If he justifies that he acted by legal order of his superiors and within the limits of competence for matters within their purview, he is exempt from penalty, which, in this case, is applied only to the superiors who gave the order.

###### Article 324-2 :

Any minister who orders or commits the acts mentioned in Article 324-1 above and who refuses or neglects to cease such acts, is punishable by imprisonment from eleven years to twenty-one years and a fine from five million (5,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 324-3 :

If the minister charged with having ordered or authorized the act contrary to the texts claims that the signature attributed to him was obtained by fraud, he is required, while ceasing the act, to denounce the person he declares to be the perpetrator of the fraud, otherwise, he is prosecuted personally and is liable to the penalty provided for in Article 324-2 above.

###### Article 324-4 :

The offenses provided for in Article 324-1 above may give rise to payment of damages that cannot be less than ten thousand (10,000) CFA francs per day of unlawful detention and per person.

###### Article 324-5 :

Are punished by a term of imprisonment from eleven years to twenty-one years and a fine of five million (5 000 000) to ten million (10 000 000) CFA francs, if the act contrary to the texts in force is done according to a false signature of the name of the minister or a public official, the perpetrators of the forgery and those who knowingly make use of it.

###### Article 324-6 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from one year to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500 000) to three million (3 000 000) CFA francs, any official or public agent charged with administrative or judicial police who, having knowledge of facts of illegal or arbitrary detention in any place, refuses or neglects to record them and to put an end to them.

###### Article 324-7 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from one year to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500 000) to three million (3 000 000) CFA francs, any person in charge of places of detention who:

˗ receives a person without warrant or judgment or without order of the government in case of expulsion or extradition;

˗ detains them or refuses to present them to the judicial police officer or to the bearer of their orders;

˗ refuses to exhibit the registers to any authority charged with controlling them.

###### Article 324-8 :

Are punished by a term of imprisonment from one year to five years:

˗ the prosecutors general, the prosecutors of Faso, their deputies, the judges or the judicial police officers who detain or cause to be detained a person outside the places and outside the conditions determined by law;

˗ the prosecutors general, the prosecutors of Faso, their deputies and the judges who bring or judge a person before a criminal court without them having been previously and legally prosecuted.

##### CHAPITRE 5 : COALITION OF PUBLIC AGENTS

###### Article 325-1 :

Are punished by a term of imprisonment from six months to five years, the depositaries of public authority who, either by meeting of individuals or bodies, or by delegation or correspondence between them, concert measures contrary to the law.

###### Article 325-2 :

If by one of the means expressed above, measures are concerted against the execution of laws or against the orders of the government, the author or authors are punished by a term of imprisonment from one year to five years.

The court seized may further pronounce a prohibition of residence for a duration that cannot exceed five years.

If this concertation takes place between the civil authorities and the military or security corps or their chiefs, the authors or instigators are punished by a term of imprisonment from five years to ten years.

If the concertation had as its object or result a plot attacking the security of the State, the authors are punished by imprisonment from eleven years to thirty years.

##### CHAPITRE 6 : MALFEASANCE AND ENCROACHMENT OF ADMINISTRATIVE,

LEGISLATIVE AND JUDICIAL AUTHORITIES

###### Article 326-1 :

Any crime committed by a public agent in the exercise or on the occasion of the exercise of their functions is malfeasance.

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from eleven years to twenty-one years and a fine of one million (1 000 000) to five million (5 000 000) CFA francs when the law has not provided for a higher penalty, any act of malfeasance. Misdemeanors and petty offenses committed by the public agent in the exercise or on the occasion of the exercise of their functions are not constitutive of malfeasance.

###### Article 326-2 :

Are punished for malfeasance by a term of imprisonment from five years to ten years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500 000) to five million (5 000 000) CFA francs:

˗ the counselors, the judges, the prosecutors general, the prosecutors of Faso, their deputies, the judicial police officers who, intentionally interfere in the exercise of legislative power either by regulations containing legislative provisions, or by stopping or suspending the execution of one or more laws, or by deliberating on the question of whether the laws will be published or executed;

˗ the counselors, the judges, the prosecutors general, the prosecutors of Faso, their deputies, the judicial police officers who, intentionally exceed their powers by interfering in matters attributed to administrative authorities, either by making regulations on these matters, or by prohibiting the execution of orders emanating from the administration or who, having permitted or ordered to summon administrators by reason of the exercise of their functions, persist in the execution of their judgments or orders notwithstanding the annulment that would have been pronounced or the conflict that would have been notified to them.

###### Article 326-3 :

Are punished by a term of imprisonment from five years to ten years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500 000) to five million (5 000 000) CFA francs, the ministers, the mayors and all administrative authorities acting in their capacity, who intentionally interfere in the exercise of legislative power or who take general texts tending to give any orders or prohibitions to courts and tribunals.

Are also punished with the same penalties the deputies or any other persons enjoying legislative power who, intentionally, interfere in the exercise of judicial power or who, intentionally, take general texts tending to give any orders or prohibitions to courts or tribunals.

###### Article 326-4 :

Are punished by a term of imprisonment from five years to ten years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500 000) to five million (5 000 000) francs, the administrative authorities who encroach on judicial functions or unduly attribute to themselves the knowledge of private rights and interests within the jurisdiction of the courts and who after the claim of the parties or one of them, nevertheless decide the matter before the authority charged with settling the conflict has ruled.

#### TITLE III: ON CORRUPTION AND RELATED OFFENSES

##### CHAPTER 1: ON CORRUPTION OF PUBLIC OFFICIALS

###### Article 331-1:

For the purposes of this title, the following terms mean:

˗ Public official:

 any civilian or military person who holds a legislative, executive, administrative, or judicial mandate, whether appointed or elected, on a permanent or temporary basis, whether remunerated or not and regardless of their hierarchical level or seniority;

 any other civilian or military person invested with a function or mandate, even temporary, remunerated or not, who contributes, in that capacity, to the service of a public body or public enterprise, or any other enterprise in which the State holds all or part of its capital, or any other enterprise that provides a public service;

 the agent of any legal person under private law responsible for the execution of a public service or public procurement regardless of the terms of the mission entrusted to them;

 any other civilian or military person defined as a public official or who is assimilated to one in accordance with the legislation and regulations in force;

˗ foreign public official: any civilian or military person who holds a legislative, executive, administrative or judicial mandate with a foreign country, whether appointed or elected; and any civilian or military person who exercises a public function for a foreign country, including for a public body or public enterprise;

˗ balance of enrichment: the statement of movable and immovable acquisitions during a given period balanced against the legal income obtained or the legal means mobilized that enabled these acquisitions;

˗ property: all types of assets, corporeal or incorporeal, movable or immovable, tangible or intangible, as well as legal instruments or documents attesting to ownership of these assets or the rights relating thereto; ˗ gift: a present, an object given to a person with the intention of pleasing them;

˗ confiscation: the permanent deprivation of property by decision of a judicial body or any other competent authority;

˗ conflict of interest: any situation of interference between a public interest and public or private interests that is likely to influence or appear to influence the independent, impartial and objective exercise of a function; ˗ Convention: the United Nations Convention against Corruption;

˗ donation: the act of voluntarily transferring something to a person without asking for anything in return;

˗ entity: an organized set of corporeal or incorporeal elements or natural or legal persons, pursuing a specific objective;

˗ official of a public international organization: any international official or any person authorized by such an organization to act on its behalf;

˗ freezing or seizure: the temporary prohibition of the transfer, conversion, disposition or movement of property, or the act of temporarily assuming custody or control of property by decision of a court or other competent authority;

˗ conventional hospitality: any disinterested act generally accepted in social relations and falling within the customs of society;

˗ predicate offense: any offense as a result of which proceeds are generated that may become the subject of money laundering in accordance with the applicable legislation relating thereto;

˗ controlled delivery: the method of allowing the exit from the national territory, passage through the territory or entry into the territory of illicit or suspected shipments, with the knowledge and under the control of the competent authorities, in order to investigate an offense and identify the persons involved in its commission;

˗ assets: all movable and immovable property, rights and receivables belonging to the declarant and related persons but also debts and financial commitments contracted by the latter;

˗ related person: any person related to the declarant by the bonds of marriage, common-law union, filiation or adoption, with the exception of adult children.

˗ proceeds of crime: any property derived, directly or indirectly, from the commission of an offense or obtained, directly or indirectly, by committing it.

˗ misuse of the sole-source or direct contracting procedure: exceeding the quota set by WAEMU regulations and the award of more than one contract by sole-source or direct contracting procedure either to a natural or legal person, or to close relatives up to the 4th degree in direct line and the 5th degree in collateral line in the same year.

###### Article 331-2:

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from two years to five years and a fine equal to twice the value of the promises accepted, undue advantages or things received or requested, such fine not being less than two million (2,000,000) CFA francs:

˗ anyone who promises, offers or grants to a public official, directly or indirectly, an undue advantage, either for themselves or for another person or entity, so that they perform or refrain from performing an act in the exercise of their functions;

˗ any public official who solicits or accepts, directly or indirectly, an undue advantage, for themselves or for another person or entity, in order to perform or refrain from performing an act within their functions.

##### CHAPTER 2: ON RELATED OFFENSES

###### Section 1: On unjustified advantages and corruption in

public procurement

###### Article 332-1:

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from five years to ten years and a fine from two million (2,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs: ˗ any public official who enters into, approves or reviews a contract, agreement, public procurement or amendment in violation of the legislative and regulatory provisions in force in order to procure an unjustified advantage for another;

˗ any merchant, industrialist, artist or artisan, private sector entrepreneur or in general, any natural or legal person who enters into, even on an occasional basis, a contract or public procurement with the State, local authorities, public institutions or bodies and State companies by taking advantage of the authority or influence of agents of the aforementioned bodies to increase the prices they normally and habitually practice or to modify, to their advantage, the quality of goods or services or delivery or supply deadlines.

###### Article 332-2:

Is punished by the penalties provided for in Article 332-1 above, anyone who compels or attempts to compel by acts of force or threats, corrupts or attempts to corrupt by promise, offer, donation or gift, a public official, whether or not the attempt had any effect.

###### Article 332-3:

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from two years to five years and a fine equivalent to three times the amount or value of the advantage received or to be received, any public official who, on the occasion of the preparation, negotiation, conclusion or execution of a public procurement, contract or amendment concluded in the name of the State or local authorities, public institutions of the State or State companies, receives or attempts to receive, directly or indirectly, for their benefit or for the benefit of a third party, remuneration or an advantage of any kind from a private contractor.

###### Article 332-4 :

Is punished by imprisonment from two years to five years and a fine equivalent to double the accepted promises, undue advantages, things received or requested of the value of a public order and a ban on bidding for public contracts for two years, any natural or legal person contracting with the State or local authorities, public establishments of the State or State companies who grants or proposes remuneration or any advantage by himself or through an intermediary to a public agent with a view to obtaining a public order.

###### Article 332-5 :

Is punished by imprisonment from one year to three years and a fine equivalent to double the accepted promises, undue advantages, things received or requested, of the value of the public order, any public agent who abusively resorts to the direct agreement procedure in a public order concluded in the name of the State or local authorities, public establishments of the State or State companies.

###### Section 2 : Of corruption of foreign public agents and

officials of public international organizations

###### Article 332-6 :

Is punished by imprisonment from five years to ten years and a fine from two million (2 000 000) to ten million (10 000 000) CFA francs: ˗ whoever promises, offers or grants to a foreign public agent or to an official of a public international organization, directly or indirectly, an undue advantage, for himself or for another person or entity, so that he performs or refrains from performing an act in the exercise of his functions, with a view to obtaining or retaining a public order or an undue advantage in connection with international trade or other;

˗ any foreign public agent or official of a public international organization who solicits or accepts, directly or indirectly, an undue advantage, for himself or for another person or entity, in order to perform or refrain from performing an act within his functions.

###### Section 3 : Of misappropriation of property by a public agent

###### Article 332-7 :

Is punished by imprisonment from five years to ten years and a fine from two million (2 000 000) to ten million (10 000 000) CFA francs any public agent who misappropriates, destroys or dissipates for his own benefit or for the benefit of another person or entity, any property, any funds or securities, public or private, or any thing of value which have been entrusted to him either by virtue of a contract or by reason of his functions.

###### Section 4 : Of illicit and abusive use and retention of public property

by a public agent

###### Article 332-8 :

Is punished by imprisonment from one year to five years and a fine from five hundred thousand (500 000) to one million five hundred thousand (1 500 000) CFA francs, any public agent who knowingly and unduly retains for his own benefit or for the benefit of another person or entity, any property, any funds or public securities, or any thing of value which have been entrusted to him either by virtue of a contract or by reason of his functions, or who makes illicit and abusive use of public property.

###### Section 5 : Of extortion

###### Article 332-9 :

Any public agent who solicits, receives, demands or orders to collect what he knows not to be due or exceeds what is due, either to himself, or to the administration, or to the parties for whom he collects, is punished:

˗ by imprisonment from one year to five years and a fine equal to double the proceeds of the offense if the value is less than or equal to five hundred thousand (500 000) francs, without this fine being less than two million (2 000 000) CFA francs;

˗ by imprisonment from five years to ten years and a fine equal to double the value of the proceeds of the offense if it exceeds five hundred thousand (500 000) CFA francs without this fine being less than ten million (10 000 000) CFA francs.

###### Section 6 : Of illegal exemptions and immunities

###### Article 332-10 :

Is punished by imprisonment from five years to ten years and a fine of two to ten times the value of the property or right compromised, any agent of the State who, for any reason whatsoever, in an illegal manner, grants exemptions or immunities from taxes, charges, fines, bonds and other rights or gives free of charge or sells at a low price, public property in violation of laws and regulations.

###### Section 7 : Of trading in influence and abuse of function

###### Article 332-11 :

Is punished by imprisonment from two years to five years and a fine from two million (2 000 000) to ten million (10 000 000) CFA francs: ˗ whoever promises, offers or grants to a public agent or to any other person, directly or indirectly, an undue advantage, so that said agent or said person uses his real or supposed influence with a view to obtaining from an administration or a public authority, an undue advantage for the initial instigator of the act or for any other person; ˗ any public agent or any other person who solicits, accepts directly or indirectly, an undue advantage for himself or for another person, in order to use his real or supposed influence with a view to obtaining from an administration or a public authority an undue advantage.

###### Article 332-12 :

Is punished by imprisonment from five years to ten years and a fine from two million (2 000 000) to ten million (10 000 000) CFA francs, the act, for a public agent, of intentionally abusing his functions or his position, by performing or refraining from performing, in the exercise of his functions, an act in violation of laws and regulations in order to obtain an undue advantage for himself or for another person or entity.

###### Section 8 : Of overbilling

###### Article 332-13 :

Is punished by imprisonment from three years to five years, confiscation of the amount received as a rebate and a fine equivalent to triple the value received without this fine being less than two million (2 000 000) CFA francs, without prejudice to the sanctions provided by the regulations on public procurement, any public agent who proceeds or causes to proceed with the invoicing, for an amount higher than its actual cost, of a good or service to be acquired by an entity of the national or local public administration.

###### Section 9 : Of nepotism and favoritism

###### Article 332-14 :

Is punished by imprisonment from one year to three years and prohibition from exercising a function in the national public administration for a period of five years, any political personality, any public agent, any member of the defense and security forces who uses his function or uses his influence, to procure directly or indirectly any undue material advantage, or employment, to a member of his family in direct line or in collateral line up to the third degree.

###### Article 332-15 :

Is punished by imprisonment from three months to one year and a fine from three hundred thousand (300 000) to one million five hundred thousand (1 500 000) CFA francs, any official of the administrative or judicial order, any military or similar person, any agent or representative of the administration, any person invested with an elective mandate, who procures or attempts to procure to another an unjustified advantage by an act contrary to legislative or regulatory provisions intended to guarantee equal access and equality of candidates in public employment and public procurement.

###### Section 10: Incompatible commerce

###### Article 332-16:

Is punished by imprisonment of five years to ten years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs, any public agent engaging in commercial or lucrative activities other than the commercialization of their non-industrial agro-pastoral, literary, scientific and artistic productions.

In addition, confiscation of the means of this commerce or lucrative activity is ordered.

###### Section 11: Misappropriation of public property

###### Article 332-17:

Whoever misappropriates or dissipates for personal purposes public funds, active assets in lieu thereof, payment instruments, securities, acts containing or operating obligations or discharge, materials or movable objects belonging, destined or entrusted to the State, to local authorities or public establishments, to organizations or companies benefiting from State participation, which they hold by reason of their functions, is guilty of misappropriation of public property:

˗ if the value of the misappropriation is less than or equal to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, the penalty is imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine equal to triple the value of the misappropriated property without this fine being less than one million (1,000,000) CFA francs;

˗ if the value is greater than one million (1,000,000) CFA francs and less than or equal to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, the penalty is imprisonment of five years to ten years and a fine equal to triple the value of the misappropriated property without this fine being less than five million (5,000,000) CFA francs;

˗ if the value is greater than ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, the penalty is imprisonment of eleven years to twenty-one years and a fine equal to triple the value of the misappropriated property without this fine being less than ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs.

In the cases provided for in bullets 2 and 3 of this article, the court may additionally order the prohibition from exercising civic rights for a duration that cannot exceed five years.

###### Section 12: Conflict of interest and illegal taking of interest

###### Article 332-18:

Is punished by imprisonment of six months to two years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, any public agent whose private interests coincide with the public interest and are likely to influence the normal exercise of their functions, who does not inform their hierarchical superior.

###### Article 332-19:

Is punished by imprisonment of five years to ten years and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, any public agent who, either directly, or indirectly or through simulated act, takes, receives or retains any interest whatsoever in acts, awards, bids, enterprises of which they have, at the time of the act in whole or in part, the administration or supervision or, who, having the mission to authorize payment or to liquidate a matter, has taken any interest therein.

###### Article 332-20:

Is punished by imprisonment of one year to ten years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, any public agent responsible by reason of their function for:

˗ the supervision and control of a private enterprise;

˗ the entering into, on behalf of the State or public authorities, of contracts or agreements of any nature or with a private enterprise;

˗ the expression of opinions on contracts or agreements of any nature with a private enterprise, who, during a period of five years from the cessation of the function, exercises a social mandate or remunerated activity, except with express authorization from the competent administrative authority, or takes or receives a capital participation, except through hereditary devolution, either in any of the enterprises mentioned above, or in any enterprise possessing with one of these at least thirty percent of common capital, or in any enterprise having concluded with one of these a contract involving exclusivity in law or in fact.

###### Article 332-21:

The provisions of articles 332-19 and 332-20 above apply equally to agents of public establishments, nationalized enterprises and mixed economy companies in which the State or public authorities hold directly or indirectly at least twenty-five percent of the capital.

###### Section 13: Illicit simulation

###### Article 332-22:

Is punished by imprisonment of six months to five years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs, any person from the private sector who agrees to represent a public agent, acquires property or exercises commercial or lucrative activities on their behalf pursuant to a written or verbal nominee agreement.

The public agent, party to this nominee agreement, is punished with the same penalties, notwithstanding the administrative and disciplinary sanctions to which they may be subject.

In addition, the court orders ex officio the confiscation of property and income subject to the offense.

###### Section 14: Offense of appearance

###### Article 332-23:

Is punished by imprisonment of two years to five years and a fine of five million (5,000,000) to twenty-five million (25,000,000) CFA francs, whoever cannot reasonably justify the increase in their lifestyle beyond a threshold set by regulation in relation to their lawful income. The trial court orders confiscation of the unjustified portion of the assets.

Incurs the same penalty enacted for the offense of receiving provided for by article 334-2 below, whoever has knowingly contributed by any means whatsoever, to concealing the illicit nature of the property at the origin of the lifestyle referred to in this article.

The offense of appearance, thus referred to, is a continuing offense characterized by the possession of illicit property or their use in a direct or indirect manner.

###### Section 15: Illicit enrichment

###### Article 332-24:

Is punished by the imprisonment penalties provided for in article 332-17, whoever has enriched themselves by using funds, materials, titles, acts, objects, effects or any other means belonging to the State.

###### Section 16: Insider trading offense

###### Article 332-25:

Is punished by imprisonment of two years to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs which may reach up to ten times the value of the profit realized, any agent from the public or private sector who exploits, in anticipation, knowingly, information not known to the public of a nature to break equality of opportunity or which would influence the course of any economic activity and of which they became aware by virtue of their situation or position.

The court orders ex officio the confiscation of property and income subject to the offense.

###### Section 17: Failure or false declaration of interest or

assets

###### Article 332-26:

Is punished by imprisonment of six months to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, any public agent legally subject to a declaration of interest and assets who, two months after a written formal notice from the institution in charge of the fight against corruption, knowingly, does not make a declaration of their assets or makes an incomplete, inaccurate or false declaration or knowingly formulates false observations or deliberately violates the obligations imposed on them by law.

###### Section 18: On the disclosure of information

###### Article 332-27:

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to three years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, any agent of the authority depositary of declarations of interest and assets or of the registry offices found guilty of having disclosed, subject to cases provided by law, in any manner whatsoever, totally or partially, declarations of interest and assets or observations received.

###### Section 19: On the offense of acceptance of undue gifts

###### Article 332-28:

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of six months to two years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, any public agent who accepts from a person a gift or any undue advantage likely to influence the handling of a procedure or transaction in progress related to his functions or having a connection with such handling or transaction.

The donor is punished by the same penalties.

###### Section 20: On the covert financing of political parties

###### Article 332-29:

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of two years to five years and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, without prejudice to the penal provisions in force relating to the financing of political parties, any political party leader who receives covert financing for the benefit of his party.

Is punished by the same penalties, any person who covertly finances a

### political party.

##### CHAPTER 3: ON CORRUPTION AND MISAPPROPRIATION OF ASSETS

IN THE PRIVATE SECTOR, ON PROHIBITED EMPLOYMENT

###### Article 333-1:

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of two years to five years and a fine equal to three times the value of the advantage or thing promised, offered or granted without being less than two million (2,000,000) CFA francs: ˗ anyone who promises, offers or grants, directly or indirectly, an undue advantage to any person who directs a private sector entity or works for such entity, in any capacity whatsoever, for herself or for another person, in order that she perform or refrain from performing an act in violation of her duties;

˗ any person directing a private sector entity or working for such entity, in any capacity whatsoever, who solicits or accepts, directly or indirectly, an undue advantage, for herself or for another person or entity in order that she perform or refrain from performing an act in violation of her duties.

###### Article 333-2:

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine equivalent to three times the value of the property or funds misappropriated without being less than two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, any person who directs a private sector entity or works for such entity, in any capacity whatsoever and who, intentionally, in the context of economic, financial or commercial activities, misappropriates any property or any private funds or securities or any other thing of value entrusted to him by reason of his functions.

###### Article 333-3:

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs, any public agent, entrusted by the State with a mission of control, supervision, administration or counsel of a private enterprise, who exercises less than five years after cessation of his functions, a corporate office or remunerated activity in such enterprise except with express authorization of the competent administrative authority.

##### CHAPTER 4: ON MONEY LAUNDERING OF PROCEEDS OF CRIME AND CONCEALMENT

OF PROCEEDS OF CORRUPTION AND RELATED OFFENSES

###### Article 334-1:

Money laundering of the proceeds of crimes provided for in this chapter is punished by the penalties provided for by the legislation on money laundering.

###### Article 334-2:

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of three years to five years and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, any person who, knowingly, conceals in whole or in part, the proceeds obtained by means of one of the offenses provided for in this title.

##### CHAPTER 5: MISCELLANEOUS PROVISIONS

###### Section 1: On rules relating to penalties and prescription

###### Article 335-1:

If the perpetrator of one or more offenses provided for in this title is a magistrate, civil servant exercising a senior function of the State, public officer, member of the superior authority responsible for the fight against corruption, officer, judicial police agent or having judicial police prerogatives or court clerk, he incurs a term of imprisonment of five years to ten years together with the fine provided for the offense committed.

###### Article 335-2:

Benefits from an exculpatory excuse under the conditions provided for by this penal code, anyone who is the perpetrator or accomplice of one or more offenses provided for in this chapter, who, before any prosecution, reveals an offense to the administrative or judicial authorities or to the relevant bodies and allows the identification of the persons implicated.

Apart from the case provided for in the preceding paragraph, the maximum penalty incurred by anyone who is the perpetrator or accomplice of one of the offenses provided for in this chapter, who, after the initiation of prosecution, facilitates the arrest of one or more other persons involved, is reduced by half.

###### Article 335-3:

In case of conviction for one or more offenses provided for in this title, the court may impose one or more of the following additional penalties:

˗ the prohibition from exercising civil rights for a period that cannot exceed five years;

˗ the prohibition from exercising public functions or employment for a period that cannot exceed five years;

˗ the prohibition from obtaining any distinction or decoration awarded by the State or its subdivisions.

###### Article 335-4:

Illicit revenues and assets deriving from one or more offenses provided for in chapters 1 to 4 of title III of book III of this code may be seized or frozen by court decision or order of the competent authority. In case of conviction, the court orders, subject to cases of restitution of assets or rights of third parties in good faith, the confiscation of illicit revenues and assets for the benefit of the Public Treasury.

The court further orders the confiscation of embezzled assets or of the value of the interest or gain obtained, even if such assets are already transferred to the ascendants, descendants, collaterals, spouse and relatives by marriage of the convicted person, whether they remained in their state or converted into any other value.

###### Article 335-5:

Public prosecution and penalties relating to offenses provided for in chapters 1 to 4 of title III of book III of this code are imprescriptible when the proceeds of crime are transferred outside the national territory.

###### Section 2: On provisions relating to denunciations

###### Article 335-6:

Is punished by imprisonment of six months to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, any person who, by virtue of his function or profession, permanent or temporary, has knowledge of one or more offenses provided for in chapters 1 to 4 of

#### title III of book III of this code, and does not inform in a timely manner the authorities

competent public authorities.

However, benefits from an exculpatory excuse anyone who brings the information to the knowledge of private anti-corruption organizations.

###### Article 335-7 :

Is punished by imprisonment from six months to five years and a fine from five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who knowingly, and by any means whatsoever, made a slanderous or abusive denunciation of the offenses provided for in chapters 1 to 4 of title III of book III of this code, to the competent authorities, against one or more persons.

###### Section 3 : Protection of witnesses, experts, whistleblowers

and victims

###### Article 335-8 :

No person may be excluded from a recruitment procedure or from access to an internship or training period in a company, no employee may be sanctioned, dismissed or subjected to discriminatory measures, direct or indirect, particularly regarding remuneration, training, reclassification, assignment, qualification, classification, professional promotion, transfer or contract renewal for having reported or testified, either to their employer or to judicial or administrative authorities, about one of the offenses provided for in chapters 1 to 4 of title III of book III of this code of which they became aware in the exercise or on the occasion of the exercise of their duties.

###### Article 335-9 :

Is punished by imprisonment from six months to five years and a fine from five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, any person who resorts to physical or moral violence, revenge, intimidation or threats in any form whatsoever and in any manner whatsoever, against the person of witnesses, experts, whistleblowers or victims or their relatives or other persons close to them.

Is punished by the same penalties, any person who reveals the identity or address of a witness who has benefited from the protection provisions provided for by article 335-8 above.

##### CHAPITRE 6 : ELECTORAL FRAUD

###### Article 336-1 :

Is punished by imprisonment from one month to one year and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs anyone who registers under a false name or false qualification or in registering has concealed an incapacity provided for by law or fraudulently registers on more than one list.

Is punished by the same penalties, anyone who obtains a false certificate of registration or removal from electoral lists. Is punished by the same penalties, anyone who has violated the provisions relating to sponsorship.

###### Article 336-2 :

Is punished by imprisonment from one to three months and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs anyone who, deprived of the right to vote, either following a judicial conviction or following a bankruptcy not followed by rehabilitation, has voted either by virtue of a registration on lists prior to their deprivation, or by virtue of a subsequent registration, made with their complicity.

###### Article 336-3 :

Is punished by imprisonment from six months to two years and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, anyone who has voted during an electoral consultation, either by virtue of a registration obtained in the cases provided for by article 336-1 above, or by falsely assuming the names and qualifications of a registered voter.

###### Article 336-4 :

Is punished by the penalties provided for in article 336-1 above, any citizen who has taken advantage of multiple registration to vote more than once.

The same penalties are applied to anyone who has prevented, by willful non-observance of the law, the registration on an electoral list of a citizen meeting the conditions set by the electoral law.

###### Article 336-5 :

Is punished by imprisonment from six months to two years, a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs and deprivation of the right to vote and to be eligible, for at least five years and at most ten years, anyone who, being responsible in a ballot for receiving, counting or examining the ballots containing the votes of citizens, has removed, added or altered ballots, or has deliberately read a name other than the one written.

Any failure to fulfill one of the obligations prescribed in articles 86 and 96 of the law establishing the electoral code by a member of the polling station is equivalent to a violation of the ballot and subject to the sanctions provided for in paragraph 1 of this article.

###### Article 336-6 :

Is punished by imprisonment from six months to two years, a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs and deprivation of the right to vote and to be eligible for at least five years and at most ten years, anyone who, by gatherings, clamors or threatening demonstrations, has disrupted the operations of an electoral consultation, infringed upon the exercise of the electoral right or the freedom of the vote.

###### Article 336-7 :

Is punished by a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, anyone who participates in an electoral consultation with a concealed weapon.

The penalty is imprisonment from one to three months and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, when the weapon is visible.

###### Article 336-8 :

Notwithstanding the provisions of this code regarding defamation and insult, any candidate or activist of political parties or formations who uses defamation, insults or any act of provocation that may taint the morality and serenity of the electoral campaign is punished by two or all of the following penalties:

˗ imprisonment from one month to one year;

˗ a fine from three hundred thousand (300,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs;

˗ deprivation of civil rights for at least two years and at most five years.

###### Article 336-9 :

Is punished by imprisonment from one year to five years and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, anyone who breaks into a polling station, when the break-in is carried out or attempted with violence.

If the perpetrators are carrying weapons, or if the ballot has been violated, the penalty is imprisonment from five years to ten years and a fine from five hundred thousand (500

,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 336-10 :

Is punished by imprisonment from one month to one year and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, anyone who, present at the voting location, has committed acts of violence, threats or behavior likely to disturb public order and tranquility, to delay or prevent electoral operations. If the ballot has been violated, the penalty of imprisonment is from one year to five years and a fine from three hundred thousand (300,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs. The penalty is imprisonment from five years to ten years and a fine from six hundred thousand (600,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, in cases where the offenses provided for in paragraph 1 were committed pursuant to a concerted plan to be executed in one or more electoral constituencies.

###### Article 336-11 :

Is punished by imprisonment from one year to five years and a fine from three hundred thousand (300,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who removes a ballot box containing votes cast and not yet counted. If this removal was carried out by a group, with or without violence, the penalty of imprisonment is from five years to ten years and the fine from six hundred thousand (600

,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 336-12 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of five years to ten years and a fine of six hundred thousand (600 000) to two million (2 000 000) CFA francs, the violation of the ballot, either by members of the polling station, or by agents of the authority, assigned to guard the ballots not yet counted.

###### Article 336-13 :

The conviction, if it is pronounced, cannot, in any case, have the effect of annulling the election declared valid by the competent authorities or that has become final, by the absence of any regular protest made within the time limits provided by the laws in force.

###### Article 336-14 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one month to two years, and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250 000) to six hundred thousand (600 000) CFA francs, anyone who, either by assault, violence or threats against a voter, or by making him fear losing his job or exposing his person, his family or his fortune to damage, has determined him or has attempted to determine him to abstain from voting or has influenced his vote.

###### Article 336-15 :

Apart from cases specifically provided for by the provisions of the laws and regulations in force, is punished by a term of imprisonment of one month to one year and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250 000) to six hundred thousand (600 000) CFA francs, anyone who, either in an administrative commission, or in a polling station or in the offices of town halls, prefectures or outside them, before during or after the ballot, has, by any fraudulent acts, violated or attempted to undermine the sincerity, prevented or attempted to prevent the ballot operations or who has changed or attempted to change the result.

The perpetrator may also be deprived of his civil rights for at least two years and at most five years.

###### Article 336-16 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one month to three months and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250 000) to six hundred thousand (600 000) CFA francs, anyone who has distributed or had distributed on the day of the ballot voting ballots and other electoral campaign documents.

The court seized, orders, in addition, the confiscation of voting ballots and all other electoral campaign documents.

###### Article 336-17 :

The public action and the civil action brought under articles 336-1 to 336- 19 of this law are prescribed after six months, from the day of the proclamation of the election result.

###### Article 336-18 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to two years, a fine of five million (5 000 000) to ten million (10 000 000) CFA francs and the deprivation of civil rights for five years anyone who is guilty of acts qualified as corruption or electoral fraud notably by: ˗ fraudulent registration on electoral lists;

˗ alteration of indelible ink in order to vote several times; ˗ transfer and transport of voters to register on an electoral list or to vote;

˗ transfer of populations from one polling station to another;

˗ lack of transparency in the use of resources that the State makes available to political parties for electoral campaigns;

˗ falsification of electoral results.

Is punished by the same penalties anyone who offers to electoral actors notably members of polling stations or scrutineers promises, gifts or advantages of any nature whatsoever.

###### Article 336-19 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to two years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500 000) to one million (1 000 000) CFA francs anyone who proceeds to:

˗ the use of attributes, goods or means of the State, like those of a public legal entity, an institution or a public body notably a company, an office, a State project and an international institution for electoral purposes;

˗ advertising practices of a political nature;

˗ media coverage of any disguised electoral campaign;

˗ the distribution of leaflets, the posting of posters or the accomplishment of any act of electoral propaganda outside the places and time reserved for this propaganda.

#### TITLE IV : ON ABUSE OF AUTHORITY, OBSTRUCTIONS TO THE EXERCISE OF

JUSTICE AND OFFENSES RELATING TO THE MAINTENANCE OF CIVIL STATUS

##### CHAPTER 1 : ATTACKS ON THE AUTHORITY OF JUSTICE

###### Section 1 : Obstruction of the proper functioning of justice

###### Article 341-1 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of six months to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500 000) to two million (2 000 000) CFA francs: ˗ anyone who resorts to physical force, threats or intimidation or promises, offers or grants an undue advantage to obtain false testimony or prevent testimony or the presentation of evidence in a proceeding related to the offenses established in accordance with chapters 1 to 4 of title III of book III of this code; ˗ anyone who resorts to physical force, threats or intimidation to obstruct the course of investigations related to the commission of offenses established in accordance with chapters 1 to 4 of title III of book III of this code.

###### Section 2 : Protection of witnesses, experts, whistleblowers

and victims

###### Article 341-2 :

No person may be excluded from a recruitment procedure or from access to an internship or training period in a company, no employee may be sanctioned, dismissed or subject to a discriminatory measure, direct or indirect, particularly regarding remuneration, training, reclassification, assignment, qualification, classification, professional promotion, transfer or contract renewal for having reported or testified, either to his employer, or to the judicial or administrative authorities, about facts qualified as an offense within the meaning of this chapter of which he would have had knowledge in the exercise or on the occasion of the exercise of his duties.

###### Article 341-3 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of six months to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500 000) to two million (2 000 000) CFA francs, anyone who resorts to physical or moral violence, revenge, intimidation or threat in any form whatsoever and in any manner whatsoever, against the person of witnesses, experts, whistleblowers or victims or their relatives or other persons close to them. Is punished by the same penalties, anyone who reveals the identity or address of a witness having benefited from the protection provisions provided for by this chapter.

###### Section 3 : False or abusive denunciation of corruption or

acts of corruption

###### Article 341-4 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of six months to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500 000) to two million (2 000 000) CFA francs, anyone who has knowingly, and by any means whatsoever, made a false or abusive denunciation of the offenses provided for by this chapter, to the competent authorities, against one or more persons.

###### Section 4 : Failure to report offenses

###### Article 341-5 :

Is punished by imprisonment of six months to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500 000) to two million (2 000 000) CFA francs, anyone who, by virtue of his function or profession, permanent or provisional, has knowledge of one or more offenses provided for in this chapter, and does not inform the competent public authorities in time.

However, benefits from an absolute excuse the person who, for any reasons, brings the information to the knowledge of private anti-corruption organizations.

###### Section 5 : Abuse of authority and other obstructions to the exercise

of justice

###### Article 341-6 :

Is punished by imprisonment from three months to two years and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, the act, by a person holding public authority or charged with a public service mission, acting in the exercise or on the occasion of the exercise of their functions or mission, of entering or attempting to enter into the home of another against their will outside the cases provided for by law.

###### Article 341-7 :

Any public official, public officer, administrator, agent or employee of the administration or police, any executor of judicial warrants or judgments, any chief or subordinate commander of the public force who, without legitimate reason, uses or causes to be used violence against persons in the exercise or on the occasion of the exercise of their functions, is punished according to the nature and severity of such violence following the rule set forth in article 344-1 of this law.

###### Article 341-8 :

Is punished by imprisonment from six months to ten years and a fine from three hundred thousand (300,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs, any suppression, any opening of letters or parcels entrusted to the postal service, committed or facilitated by a public official or agent assigned to the postal administration.

The court shall further pronounce against the convicted person a prohibition from any public employment or function for a period that may not exceed five years.

###### Article 341-9 :

Is punished by a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs and a prohibition from exercising public functions for a period from two months to five years, the act, by a magistrate, any other person sitting in a judicial body, of denying to render justice after being requested to do so and of persisting in their denial after warning or injunction from their superiors.

###### Article 341-10 :

Is punished by imprisonment from one month to three months, in addition to fines pronounced for non-appearance, the witness who alleges an excuse found to be false.

###### Article 341-11 :

Is punished by imprisonment from two years to ten years and a fine from three hundred thousand (300,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs the act, by anyone, of using threats, violence or committing any other act of intimidation to obtain from a magistrate, any person sitting in a judicial body or participating in the public service of justice, or an agent of detection or repression services of offenses in a foreign State or in an international court, that they perform or refrain from performing an act of their function, their mission or facilitated by their function or mission.

###### Article 341-12 :

Is punished by imprisonment from one year to ten years any public official, agent or employee of the administration who requisitions or orders, causes to requisition or order the action or use of public force against the execution of an order, a judicial warrant or any other order emanating from the legitimate authority.

###### Article 341-13 :

If as a result of said orders or requisitions, punishable acts occur subject to penalties stronger than those referred to in the preceding articles, these stronger penalties are applied to the public officials, agents or employees guilty of having given said orders or made said requisitions.

###### Article 341-14 :

Is punished by imprisonment from six months to two years, any commander of internal security forces legally seized with a requisition from the authorized authority who refuses their services or abstains from causing the forces under their orders to act.

###### Section 6 : Obstructions to judicial measures for the protection

of minors

###### Article 341-15 :

If an incident relating to supervised freedom reveals a characterized lack of supervision on the part of the parents or guardian or custodian or systematic obstructions to the exercise of the supervision mission, the children's judge establishes an incident report which they send to the procureur du Faso, who seizes the competent court.

Is punished by imprisonment from one month to two months and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs anyone who commits the offense provided for in paragraph 1 of this article.

###### Article 341-16 :

If on the occasion of the execution of a measure taken by a juvenile court in application of paragraphs 1, 2 and 3 of article 116 of law n°015-2014/AN of 13 May 2014 on the protection of children in conflict with the law or in danger, an incident reveals a characterized lack of supervision on the part of the parents, legal representatives of the child or systematic obstructions to the exercise of the social worker's mission, the children's judge establishes an incident report which they send to the procureur du Faso, who seizes the competent court.

Is punished by imprisonment from one month to two months and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs anyone who commits the offense provided for in the first paragraph of this article.

###### Article 341-17 :

If on the occasion of the execution of a measure taken by a juvenile court in application of article 120 of law n°015-2014/AN of 13 May 2014 on the protection of children in conflict with the law or in danger, an incident reveals a characterized lack of supervision on the part of the parents, legal representatives or systematic obstructions to the exercise of the mission of the social worker responsible for child protection, the children's judge establishes an incident report which they send to the procureur du Faso, who seizes the competent court.

Is punished by imprisonment from one month to two months and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs anyone who commits the offense provided for in paragraph 1 of this article.

##### CHAPITRE 2 : OFFENSES RELATING TO THE KEEPING OF CIVIL STATUS

RECORDS AND TO ASSOCIATIONS

###### Article 342-1 :

Is punished by imprisonment from six months to one year and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs, the civil status officer or person delegated by them by virtue of legal provisions, who records these acts elsewhere than in the register intended for that purpose or who omits to record them therein.

###### Article 342-2 :

Is punished by imprisonment from six months to one year and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs, the civil status officer or person delegated by them by virtue of legal provisions, who celebrates a marriage in violation of the conditions prescribed by law.

###### Article 342-3 :

The provisions of articles 342-1 and 342-2 above are applicable even if the nullity of the civil status records has not been requested or has been cured.

###### Article 342-4 :

Is punished by a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs and in case of recidivism by a double fine, anyone who contravenes the legislative and regulatory provisions on the conditions for formation and declaration of associations.

Are punished by imprisonment from three months to three years and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs, the founders or leaders of associations who maintain themselves or who reconstitute them illegally after publication of the regulatory text of dissolution, as well as persons who, by propaganda, speeches, writings or by any other means, perpetuate or attempt to perpetuate the dissolved association.

###### Article 342-5 :

Is punished with a penalty of imprisonment from one month to one year and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who in any capacity, assumes or continues to assume the administration of an association notwithstanding the refusal or withdrawal of the declaration receipt or public utility recognition or the finding of nullity.

###### Article 342-6 :

Is punished with imprisonment from six months to three years and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who does not comply with the injunctions of the competent authority tending to the recognition of an association or who gives false information, assumes or continues to assume the administration of foreign associations or establishments operating without authorization.

The same penalties are applicable to the leaders and participants in the activity of associations or establishments that operate without observing the conditions imposed by the authorization text or beyond the duration fixed by the latter.

##### CHAPITRE 3 : ILLEGAL EXERCISE OF PUBLIC AUTHORITY

###### Article 343-1 :

Is punished with a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, any official subject to oath who enters into the exercise of their functions without having taken the oath.

###### Article 343-2 :

Is punished with a penalty of imprisonment from one year to ten years and a fine of six hundred thousand (600,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, any person invested with an administrative mandate, or any public official revoked, dismissed, suspended, legally prohibited who, after having had official knowledge thereof, continues the exercise of their functions or who, invested with elective or temporary functions, exercises them after having been replaced or after their functions have ended.

The convicted person is furthermore prohibited from any public employment or function for a duration that cannot exceed five years, all without prejudice to the more severe penalties imposed against the commanders referred to in Article 313-5 of this law.

##### CHAPITRE 4 : SPECIAL PROVISION

###### Article 344-1 :

Outside the cases where the law specifically regulates the penalties incurred for crimes and offenses committed by public or military officials or officers, those among them who participate in other crimes or offenses that they were charged with monitoring or suppressing are punished as follows:

˗ with the maximum penalty, if it is an offense;

˗ with imprisonment from eleven to thirty years, if the crime carries against any other perpetrator the penalty of imprisonment of more than ten years.

#### TITRE V : CRIMES AND OFFENSES COMMITTED BY PRIVATE INDIVIDUALS

AGAINST PUBLIC ORDER

##### CHAPITRE 1 : OFFENSES AGAINST THE FLAG AND SYMBOLS

OF THE NATION

###### Article 351-1 :

Is punished with a penalty of imprisonment from two months to two years and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs, the act, during a demonstration organized or regulated by public authority, of publicly insulting the national anthem or the national flag.

The penalty is imprisonment from six months to three years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, when the insult is committed in a gathering.

###### Article 351-2 :

Outside the cases provided for by Article 351-1 above, is punished with a penalty of imprisonment from two months to two years and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs, the act, for anyone, when it is committed under conditions likely to disturb public order and with the intention of insulting the national flag: ˗ to destroy it, deteriorate it or use it in a degrading manner, in a public place or place open to the public;

˗ for the perpetrator of such acts, even committed in a private place, to disseminate or cause to be disseminated the recording of images relating to their commission. In case of recidivism, the penalty is imprisonment from four months to four years and a fine that cannot be less than double the fine pronounced upon the first conviction.

##### CHAPITRE 2 : INSULTS AGAINST HOLDERS OF PUBLIC

AUTHORITY

###### Article 352-1 :

Is punished with a penalty of imprisonment from three months to one year and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs the insult made by gestures, threats, sending of objects, drawings, writings, words or cries uttered against the head of State or a foreign head of State. When the insult is uttered against a president of an Institution, a member of the government, of a foreign government, a deputy or a diplomatic agent, the penalty is imprisonment from one month to one year and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 352-2 :

Is punished with a penalty of imprisonment from six months to three years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, the insult by words, gestures or threats, by writings or images of any nature not made public or by sending any objects addressed to a magistrate, an assessor or any person sitting in a judicial formation in the exercise of their functions or on the occasion of this exercise and tending to impair their honor, their dignity or the respect due to the function with which they are invested.

If the insult takes place at the hearing of a court, a tribunal or a judicial formation, the penalty of imprisonment is increased from one year to ten years and the fine from five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs. When the insult is perpetrated before the perpetrator had knowledge of the quality of the magistrate, the penalty of imprisonment is from three months to one year and the fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 352-3 :

Is punished with a penalty of imprisonment from six months to one year and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, the act of seeking to cast discredit, publicly by acts, words, writings or images of any nature, on a judicial act or decision, under conditions likely to impair the authority of justice or its independence.

The same penalties apply to the deliberate refusal to execute a judicial decision.

The provisions of paragraph 1 do not apply, neither to acts, words, writings or images of any nature tending to the reform, cassation or revision of a decision, nor to technical commentaries.

When the offenses provided for in paragraphs 1 and 2 of this article are committed by lawyers, notaries, bailiffs, court clerks or other auxiliaries of justice, the penalty of imprisonment is from six months to two years. When the offense is committed through the written or audiovisual press, the specific provisions of the laws governing these matters are applicable with regard to the determination of responsible persons.

###### Article 352-4 :

Is punished with a penalty of imprisonment from one month to one year and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, the insult made by words, gestures, threats, writings or drawings not made public or also by sending any objects, tending to impair their honor or delicacy and targeting any ministerial officer or any commander or agent of the public force any sworn agent in the exercise or on the occasion of the exercise of their functions.

###### Article 352-5 :

In all cases, the offender may be further sentenced to make reparation, either at the first hearing or in writing, and the period of imprisonment imposed against him is counted only from the day the reparation takes place. When the insult is publicly perpetrated, the maximum penalties provided for are imposed.

###### Article 352-6 :

Cannot give rise to prosecutions for insults:

˗ parliamentary debates;

˗ speeches during electoral campaigns without imputation of fact on strictly private life;

˗ judicial debates;

˗ pronouncements or writings produced before the courts;

˗ faithful and good faith reporting of these debates and speeches except for defamation proceedings;

˗ publication of judicial decisions including those rendered in defamation matters;

˗ official report made in good faith by a person regularly designated to conduct an investigation and within the framework of that investigation; ˗ imputation made in good faith by a superior or his subordinate; ˗ information given in good faith about a person or a third party who has a personal or official interest in knowing it or who has the power to remedy an alleged injustice;

˗ criticism of a work, a show, any opinion publicly expressed provided that said criticism does not constitute a personal attack.

###### Article 352-7 :

For the offenses provided for in this chapter, the public action is prescribed after three full months from the commission of the offense or from the day of the last act of prosecution or investigation.

##### CHAPITRE 3 : VIOLENCE AGAINST HOLDERS OF PUBLIC AUTHORITY

###### Article 353-1 :

Is punished by imprisonment from one year to ten years and a fine from three hundred thousand (300,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, whoever, even without weapons and without resulting in injuries, engages in violence or assault against a magistrate, an assessor, or any person sitting in a judicial body in the exercise or on the occasion of the exercise of his functions.

The maximum penalties are always imposed if the assault or violence takes place at the hearing or within the premises of a court or tribunal. The court seized may also impose the prohibition of exercising civil rights, the prohibition of residence as well as exercising any public employment for a period that cannot exceed five years.

###### Article 353-2 :

Are punished by imprisonment from six months to three years and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, violence or assault of the type provided for in Article 353-1 above, directed against a public service agent, if they take place in the exercise or on the occasion of the exercise of his functions.

###### Article 353-3 :

If the violence or assault exercised against the officials and agents designated in Articles 353-1 and 353-2 above have caused an incapacity for work equal to twenty-one days or more, the penalty is imprisonment from five years to ten years.

If death resulted from it, the penalty is imprisonment from eleven years to thirty years.

###### Article 353-4 :

In cases where this violence or assault has not caused bloodshed, injuries or illnesses, the perpetrators are punished by imprisonment from five years to ten years.

If the violence or assault was exercised with premeditation or ambush and caused bloodshed, injuries or illnesses, the perpetrators are punished by imprisonment from eleven years to twenty years.

###### Article 353-5 :

Is punished by life imprisonment, if the blows were struck or injuries made to officials or agents designated in Articles 353-1 and 353-2 above in the exercise or on the occasion of the exercise of their functions with the intention of causing death.

##### CHAPITRE 4 : DEGRADATION OF MONUMENTS, ACTS OF VANDALISM AND UNLAWFUL DEMONSTRATIONS

###### Section 1 : Degradation of monuments

###### Article 354-1 :

Is punished by imprisonment from two months to two years and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, whoever voluntarily destroys, mutilates or degrades:

˗ either monuments, statues, paintings or other objects intended for public utility or decoration and erected or placed by public authority or with its authorization;

˗ either monuments, statues, paintings or other objects of art of any kind placed in museums, places reserved for worship or other buildings open to the public;

˗ either monuments, sites, paintings or other natural objects registered or classified as national heritage.

###### Section 2 : Acts of vandalism

###### Article 354-2 :

The act of vandalism is the act of damaging, destroying, degrading or voluntarily deteriorating property belonging to another during demonstrations on public roads.

Vandalism is punished by imprisonment from one year to three years and a fine from three hundred thousand (300,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 354-3 :

The offense of act of vandalism applies:

˗ to acts of vandalism as provided for in the following article;

˗ to organizers of unlawful demonstrations on public roads, during which acts of vandalism have been committed;

˗ to perpetrators and accomplices of acts of vandalism;

˗ to persons who enter a demonstration even if lawful, with the intention of committing or having other participants commit acts of vandalism.

###### Article 354-4 :

The following acts committed during demonstrations on public roads constitute acts of vandalism:

˗ destruction or degradation caused to property, movable or immovable, private or public;

˗ destruction of registers, minutes or acts of public authority.

###### Section 3 : Lawful and unlawful demonstrations

###### Article 354-5 :

A demonstration is lawful when the organizers have declared it to the competent administrative authority under the conditions provided for by the laws and regulations in force relating to freedom of assembly and demonstration on public roads.

###### Article 354-6 :

Within the meaning of this section, a demonstration is unlawful when: ˗ the organizers have not taken the precaution of declaring it to the competent administrative authority under the conditions provided for by law;

˗ the demonstration has been prohibited by the competent administrative authority after the declaration was made to it;

˗ the organizers have made an incomplete or inaccurate declaration likely to mislead about the object or conditions of the planned demonstration.

###### Article 354-7 :

The organizers of an unlawful demonstration as defined in Article 354-6 above are liable to imprisonment from six months to two years and a fine from three hundred thousand (300,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.

The penalty is imprisonment from two months to one year and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs for persons who have voluntarily participated in it.

###### Article 354-8 :
