When as a result of an unlawful demonstration or one prohibited by the administrative police authority, acts of vandalism have been committed, are punished by a sentence of:

˗ imprisonment of one year to four years and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs for the organizers of this demonstration who have not given the order to disperse as soon as they become aware of such violence or acts of violence, destruction or damage;

˗ imprisonment of six months to three years and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, for persons who continue to actively participate in this demonstration, after the commencement and with knowledge of the violence, acts of violence, destruction or damage.

###### Article 354-9 :

Persons found guilty of any of the offenses referred to in this chapter are jointly and severally liable for civil damages.

##### CHAPTER 5: BREAKING OF SEALS AND REMOVAL OF DOCUMENTS

IN PUBLIC DEPOSITORIES

###### Article 355-1 :

Is punished by a sentence of imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs anyone who intentionally breaks or attempts to break seals or is an accomplice thereto. If it is the guardian himself who breaks or attempts to break seals or is an accomplice thereto, the sentence of imprisonment is two years to five years and the fine is one million (1,000,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 355-2 :

When seals affixed either by an administrative order or pursuant to a court order have been broken, the negligent guardian is punished by a sentence of imprisonment of two months to six months and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 355-3 :

Is punished by a sentence of imprisonment of five years to ten years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, any theft committed by means of breaking seals.

Is punished by the same sentence any theft of a seal.

###### Article 355-4 :

Is punished by a sentence of imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who removes, destroys or takes away, outside the cases provided for by law, procedural documents or other documents, registers, deeds and effects contained in public archives, registries or depositories or entrusted to a public depositary in that capacity.

###### Article 355-5 :

When the offense provided for in Article 355-4 above has been facilitated by the negligence of court clerks, archivists and other public depositaries, they are punished by a sentence of imprisonment of two months to one year and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 355-6 :

If the act is committed by the depositary himself, he is punished by a sentence of imprisonment of five years to ten years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 355-7 :

If the breaking of a seal, the removal, taking away or destruction of the seal or of the document kept in a public depository has been committed with violence against persons, the sentence is imprisonment of eleven years to twenty-one years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs without prejudice to other convictions on account of the violence.

##### CHAPTER 6: OFFENSES RELATING TO BURIALS, GRAVES

AND VIOLATION OF RESPECT DUE TO THE

DEAD

###### Article 356-1 :

Is punished by a sentence of imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs anyone who:

˗ violates the integrity of a corpse, by any means whatsoever; ˗ commits a violation or desecration, by any means whatsoever, of tombs, graves, cinerary urns or monuments erected in memory of the dead.

Is punished by a sentence of imprisonment of two months to six months and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, anyone who, without a permit issued by the civil status officer, has a deceased person buried.

The same sentences are imposed against those who contravene in any manner whatsoever the legislative and regulatory provisions relating to burials.

###### Article 356-2 :

The offenses defined in Article 356-1 above are punished by a sentence of imprisonment of three years to ten years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs when they have been committed on account of the membership or non-membership, true or supposed, of the deceased persons in a specific ethnic group, nation, race or religion.

###### Article 356-3 :

Is punished by a sentence of imprisonment of one year to three years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who conceals the corpse of a victim of homicide or who died as a result of assault and battery.

Is also punished by the same sentence anyone who retains without legitimate reason in his possession the corpse of a person.

###### Article 356-4 :

Is punished by a sentence of imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who is guilty of mutilation of a corpse.

If the mutilations committed were for the purpose of trafficking or trade in bones or any other part of the human body, the sentence of imprisonment is five years to ten years.

##### CHAPTER 7: OFFENSES AGAINST THE

ENVIRONMENT

###### Article 357-1 :

Is punished by a sentence of imprisonment of six months to five years and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who has, through inattention, recklessness or negligence harmed the health of man, animals, or plants by altering either the balance of the natural environment or the essential qualities of soil, water or air.

###### Article 357-2 :

Is punished by a sentence of imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who involuntarily destroys, degrades or damages woods, forests, heaths, scrubland, plantations, fields or reforestation by the effect of an explosion, a fire or any other means caused by failure to comply with an obligation of prudence or safety imposed by law or regulation. The same sentences are applicable when the offense described above has resulted in the death of wild animals.

The sentence is imprisonment of one year to six years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, if it involves fully protected wild animals.

In case of deliberate violation of an obligation of prudence or safety provided for by law or regulation, the sentences incurred are increased to one year to five years of imprisonment and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

In this latter case, the offense is punished by the same sentences when it has resulted in the death of wild animals.

The sentence is imprisonment of three years to seven years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs if it involves fully protected wild animals.

The attempt is punished by the same sentences.

###### Article 357-3 :

Is punished by imprisonment from one year to seven years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who voluntarily destroys, degrades or deteriorates woods, forests, heathlands, scrublands, plantations, fields or reforestation by means of an explosion, fire or any other means. The same penalties apply when the offense has resulted in the death of wild animals.

The imprisonment penalty is from five years to ten years and the fine from one million (1,000,000) to fifteen million (15,000,000) CFA francs, if the offense has caused irreversible damage to the environment.

The same penalties apply when the offense has resulted in the death of fully protected wild animals.

The attempt of the offenses provided for in this article is punishable.

##### CHAPITRE 8 : DES AUTRES ATTEINTES A L'ORDRE PUBLIC

###### Section 1 : Des infractions à la réglementation des maisons de jeux et des

loteries non autorisées par la loi

###### Article 358-1 :

Is punished by imprisonment from two months to two years and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who, without authorization and in a public place or place open to the public:

˗ operates a house of games of chance;

˗ operates devices whose operation is essentially based on chance and which are intended to provide a gain in exchange for a stake;

˗ organizes lotteries, betting or raffles.

In all cases, the funds or assets that are found displayed, the furniture, instruments, devices used and the movable objects with which the premises are furnished or decorated are confiscated for the benefit of the public treasury.

Raffles and games organized for charitable purposes on the occasion of regular declared events are authorized by right.

###### Article 358-2 :

Is punished by imprisonment from six months to three years and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs anyone who derives their subsistence from practicing or facilitating on public roads, in a public place or place open to the public, the exercise of illicit games.

###### Section 2 : Des délits des fournisseurs des forces armées

###### Article 358-3 :

Is punished by imprisonment from one year to five years and a fine of one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, all without prejudice to stronger penalties in case of intelligence with the enemy, anyone charged, as a member of a company, with supplies, enterprises or administrations for the account of the armed forces, who, without having been compelled by force majeure, does not fulfill their obligations.

The same penalties apply to agents of suppliers when the cessation of service results from their act.

###### Article 358-4 :

When public officials or agents appointed or employed by the government assist the perpetrators in causing the service to fail, the penalty is increased to the maximum without prejudice to stronger penalties in case of intelligence with the enemy.

###### Article 358-5 :

Is punished by imprisonment from one year to five years and a fine of one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, even though the service has not failed, anyone who through their negligence delays deliveries or works or who commits fraud on the nature, quality or quantity of works, labor or things supplied.

###### Article 358-6 :

The offenses provided for in this section may only be prosecuted upon denunciation by the government.

###### Section 3 : Des infractions relatives à l'industrie, au commerce et aux

enchères publiques

###### Article 358-7 :

Is punished by a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs and confiscation of goods, anyone who commits a violation of regulations relating to products intended for export and which aims to guarantee their good quality, their nature, their quantity and their dimensions.

###### Article 358-8 :

Is guilty of illicit speculation and punished by imprisonment from six months to three years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who, directly or through an intermediary, operates or attempts to operate the artificial increase or decrease of prices of foodstuffs, goods or public or private securities by:

˗ false or slanderous news or information knowingly spread among the public;

˗ offers thrown on the market with the intention of disturbing prices;

˗ offers of prices higher than what sellers are asking;

˗ any fraudulent ways or means;

˗ or by exercising or attempting to exercise, either individually or by meeting or coalition, an action on the market with the aim of obtaining a gain that would not be the result of the natural play of supply and demand.

###### Article 358-9 :

The perpetrator is punished by imprisonment from one year to five years and a fine of five million (5,000,000) to thirty million (30,000,000) CFA francs, when the increase or decrease was operated or attempted on grains, flour, farinaceous substances, foodstuffs, beverages, pharmaceutical products, fuels or commercial fertilizers.

The imprisonment penalty is ten years and the fine fifty million (50,000,000) CFA francs, if the speculation relates to foodstuffs or goods not falling within the usual exercise of the profession of the perpetrator of the offense.

###### Article 358-10 :

Is prohibited any advertising made in bad faith containing, in whatever form, allegations, indications or presentations that are false or likely to mislead, when these relate to one or more of the following elements:

˗ existence, nature, composition, substantial qualities, content of useful principles, species, origin, quantity, mode and date of manufacture, properties; ˗ price and conditions of sale of goods or services that are the subject of the advertising, conditions of their use, results that can be expected from their use, reasons or methods of sale or provision of services;

˗ scope of commitments made by the advertiser identity, quality or aptitudes of the manufacturer, resellers, promoters or service providers.

###### Article 358-11 :

The advertiser, on whose behalf the advertising is disseminated, is primarily responsible for the offense committed and is punished by imprisonment from two months to two years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs.

These penalties apply to advertising agents, owners of advertising media, resellers and service providers.

###### Article 358-12 :

The cessation of advertising may be ordered either on requisition of the public prosecutor, or ex officio by the examining judge or the court seized of the prosecution.

###### Article 358-13 :

In the cases provided for by Articles 358-9, 358-10, 358-11 and 358-12 above, the court seized may furthermore pronounce the prohibition from exercising civil rights and serving in public functions, positions or offices for a period that may not exceed five years and/or apply the provisions of Article 214-17 of this law.

###### Article 358-14 :

Is punished by imprisonment from three months to three years and a fine from one million (1,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs anyone who:

˗ counterfeits a trademark, service mark or trade mark, or fraudulently affixes a mark belonging to another;

˗ makes use of a mark without authorization of the owner, even with the addition of words such as "formula, manner, system, imitation, type". However, the use of a mark by manufacturers of accessories to indicate the destination of the product is not punishable;

˗ possesses without legitimate reason products that he knew bore a counterfeit or fraudulently affixed mark, or knowingly sells, offers for sale, supplies or offers to supply products or services under such a mark.

###### Article 358-15 :

Is punished by imprisonment from three months to two years and a fine from one million (1,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs anyone who diverts the clientele of another in commercial or industrial matters:

˗ by using inaccurate or misleading titles, distinctive signs, marks or professional designations to create belief in special qualities or capacities;

˗ by resorting to measures likely to create confusion with the goods, processes or products, activities or business of another; ˗ by disparaging the goods, processes, activities or business of another, or by giving inaccurate or misleading indications about one's own in order to derive advantage to the detriment of one's competitors.

###### Article 358-16 :

The maximum penalty is doubled if the diversion of clientele is carried out:

- by granting or offering to employees, agents or auxiliaries of another advantages that should not be due to them, in order to induce them to surprise or reveal a secret of manufacture, organization or operation;
- by disclosing or exploiting such secrets learned or surprised under the conditions referred to in the preceding point.

###### Article 358-17 :

Is punished by imprisonment from six months to one year and a fine from five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who in the auctions of the ownership, usufruct or lease of immovable or movable property of an enterprise, exploitation or any service, obstructs, disturbs, attempts to obstruct or disturb the freedom of bids or tenders by acts of force, violence or threats, either before or during the bids or tenders.

Is punished by the same penalties anyone who, by gift, promise, agreement or fraudulent maneuver, removes or attempts to remove bidders, limits or attempts to limit bids or tenders, as well as the person who receives such gifts or accepts such promises.

###### Article 358-18 :

Is punished by imprisonment from one year to ten years and a fine equal to at least four times the value of the goods or property, without this fine being less than ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who imports prohibited, expired or unfit for consumption goods or property.

The same penalty applies to anyone who produces or sells or in any manner whatsoever makes available to the population prohibited goods or property unfit for consumption.

The same penalty is also applicable to anyone who falsifies the expiration date of such goods or property.

#### TITRE VI : DES CRIMES ET DELITS CONTRE LA SECURITE PUBLIQUE

##### CHAPITRE 1 : DES INFRACTIONS EN MATIERE DE TERRORISME

###### Section 1 : Des actes de terrorisme

###### Article 361-1 :

The following offenses which, by their nature, aim to intimidate or terrorize a population or to compel a State or an international organization to perform or abstain from performing any act, constitute acts of terrorism:

˗ hostage-taking;

˗ offenses against civil aviation, ships, fixed platforms, and any other means of collective transport;

˗ offenses against persons enjoying international protection including diplomatic agents;

˗ offenses by use of dangerous materials.

###### Article 361-2 :

Also constitute acts of terrorism, the following offenses when by their context these acts aim to intimidate or terrorize a population or to compel a State or an international organization to perform or abstain from performing any act:

˗ intentional attacks on life, intentional attacks on the integrity of the person, kidnapping and sequestration as defined by this code;

˗ thefts, extortions, destructions, degradations and deteriorations, as well as offenses relating to information technology provided by law;

˗ criminal association;

˗ offenses relating to weapons and explosive products as defined by law.

###### Article 361-3 :

The maximum penalties provided for are pronounced for the offenses referred to in Article 361-2 above.

The court that pronounces a custodial sentence for terrorist acts must accompany it with a safety period at least equal to two-thirds of the sentence pronounced.

The safety period determines a maximum non-reducible detention period.

###### Section 2 : Des infractions contre l'aviation civile, les navires, les plates-

formes fixes et tout autre moyen de transport collectif

###### Article 361-4 :

Is punished by imprisonment from eleven years to twenty-one years, anyone who, being on board an aircraft in flight, a ship or any other means of collective transport, seizes such aircraft, ship or means of transport by violence, threat of violence or exercises control over it. Is punished by the same penalty anyone who seizes a fixed platform or exercises control over it by violence or threat of violence.

###### Article 361-5 :

Is punished by imprisonment from eleven years to twenty-one years, anyone who:

˗ destroys an aircraft in service or not in an airport serving civil aviation or causes to such an aircraft damage that renders it unfit for flight or that is likely to compromise its safety in flight;

˗ places or causes to be placed, by any means whatsoever, on an aircraft in service or not, parked, a device or substances capable of destroying said aircraft or causing it damage that renders it unfit for flight or that is likely to compromise its safety in flight;

˗ destroys or damages air navigation facilities or services of an airport or disrupts their operation, if any of these acts is likely to compromise the safety of an aircraft in service or of civil aviation;

˗ performs an act of violence against a person on board an aircraft, if this act is likely to compromise the safety of the aircraft or civil aviation.

The same penalties apply when the facts stated above concern a fixed platform, a ship or any other means of collective transport.

###### Article 361-6 :

If injuries or illnesses result from the acts provided for in Articles 361-4 and 361-5 above, the penalty is imprisonment from twenty-one to thirty years.

If death has resulted from it, the penalty is life imprisonment.

###### Article 361-7 :

An aircraft is considered to be in flight from the moment when, boarding having been completed, all its exterior doors have been closed until the moment when one of these doors is opened for the purpose of disembarkation. In the event of a forced landing, the flight is deemed to continue until the competent authority takes charge of the aircraft as well as the persons and property on board.

An aircraft is considered to be in service from the moment when ground personnel or the crew begins to prepare it for a specific flight until the expiry of a period of twenty-four hours following any landing. The period of service extends in any event to the entirety of the time during which the aircraft is in flight within the meaning of the preceding paragraph. The term ship means a vessel of any type whatsoever which is not permanently attached to the sea-bed and includes dynamically supported craft, submersible craft and all other floating craft. The expression fixed platform means an artificial island, installation or structure permanently attached to the sea-bed for the purposes of exploration or exploitation of resources or for other economic or scientific purposes.

###### Article 361-8 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of eleven years to twenty-one years and a fine of three million (3,000,000) to thirty million (30,000,000) CFA francs, any person who, by communicating information that he knew to be false, compromises the security of a fixed platform, an aircraft in service, a ship or any other means of collective transport in service.

###### Section 3 : Des infractions contre les personnes jouissant d'une

protection internationale

###### Article 361-9 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of eleven years to twenty-one years any person who:

˗ commits or threatens to commit a kidnapping or any other attack against a person or the liberty of a person enjoying international protection;

˗ commits or threatens to commit, by resorting to violence, against the official premises, private residence or means of transport of a person enjoying international protection, an attack likely to endanger his person or his liberty.

Is punished by the same penalty any person who threatens to commit a murder against a person enjoying international protection.

If the above acts result in injuries or illnesses, the penalty is imprisonment of twenty-one to thirty years.

If death has resulted therefrom, the penalty is life imprisonment.

###### Article 361-10 :

The expression "person enjoying international protection" means: ˗ any Head of State, including each member of a collegial body performing, under the constitution of the State concerned, the functions of Head of State;

˗ any head of government or any minister of foreign affairs, when such person is in a foreign State as well as members of his family who accompany him;

˗ any representative, official or official personality of a State and any official, official personality or other agent of an international organization, who at the date and place where an offence is committed against his person, his official premises, his private domicile or his means of transport, is entitled under international law to special protection against any attack on his person, his liberty or his dignity as well as members of his family who form part of his household.

###### Section 4 : De la prise d'otage

###### Article 361-11 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of eleven years to twenty-one years any person who seizes a person or detains him and threatens to kill him, injure him or continue to detain him in order to compel a third party, namely a State, an international organization, a population, a natural or legal person or a group of persons, to do or abstain from doing any act as an explicit or implicit condition for the release of the hostage.

If the above acts result in injuries or illnesses, the penalty is imprisonment of twenty-one years to thirty years.

If death has resulted therefrom, the penalty is life imprisonment.

###### Section 5 : Des infractions par utilisation de matières dangereuses

###### Article 361-12 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of twenty-one years to thirty years any person who:

˗ uses against a ship or a fixed platform or discharges from a ship, explosives, radioactive materials or biological, chemical or nuclear weapons, in a manner likely to cause death or serious bodily or material damage;

˗ uses on board a ship or a fixed platform explosives, radioactive materials or biological, chemical or nuclear weapons, in a manner likely to cause death or serious bodily or material damage;

˗ discharges from a ship or a fixed platform, hydrocarbons, liquefied natural gas or other harmful or potentially dangerous substances, which are not covered by the preceding paragraph, in quantity or concentration, likely to cause death or serious bodily or material damage;

˗ uses a ship in a manner that causes serious material damage;

˗ threatens to commit any one of the offences referred to in the preceding bullet points.

If the above acts have resulted in injuries or illnesses, the penalty is imprisonment of twenty-one years to thirty years.

If death has resulted therefrom, the penalty is life imprisonment.

###### Article 361-13 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of twenty-one to thirty years any person who transports on board a ship:

˗ explosives or radioactive materials, knowing that they are intended to cause or threaten to cause death, serious bodily or material damage;

˗ any biological, chemical or nuclear weapon knowingly; ˗ any raw materials or special fissionable products, equipment or materials specially designed or prepared for the processing, use or production of special fissionable products, knowing that such materials, products or equipment are intended for a nuclear explosive activity or any other illicit nuclear activity;

˗ any equipment, materials or software, or related technologies that contribute significantly to the design, manufacture or launch of a biological, chemical or nuclear weapon.

If the above acts have resulted in injuries or illnesses, the penalty is imprisonment of twenty-one to thirty years.

If death has resulted therefrom, the penalty is life imprisonment.

###### Article 361-14 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from twenty-one years to thirty years anyone who:

˗ holds, transfers, alters, surrenders, disperses, unlawfully uses or threatens to use nuclear or radioactive materials, causing or likely to cause death or serious injury to others or substantial damage to property or the environment;

˗ manufactures or possesses a device, with the intent to cause the death of a person or to cause serious bodily harm or with the intent to cause substantial damage to property or the environment;

˗ uses radioactive materials or devices in any manner whatsoever, uses or damages a nuclear facility, so as to release or risk releasing radioactive materials, with the intent to cause the death of a person or to cause serious bodily harm or with the intent to cause substantial damage to property or the environment or with the intent to compel a natural or legal person, an international organization or a government to perform an act or refrain from doing so;

˗ fraudulently removes or threatens to remove, diverts or unlawfully appropriates nuclear materials;

˗ unlawfully transports, sends or moves nuclear materials into or from a State;

˗ unlawfully commits or threatens to commit an act against a nuclear facility or disrupts its operation, an act by which the perpetrator knows that he may cause death or serious injury to others or substantial damage to property or the environment as a result of exposure to nuclear materials through threat, use of force or any other form of intimidation;

˗ demands the surrender of radioactive materials or devices or nuclear facilities by resorting to threat in circumstances that make it credible or to the use of force;

˗ introduces into the atmosphere, on the ground, in the subsoil, in food or food components, in water or in objects of use, a substance likely to endanger the health of humans or animals or the natural environment, where this act is intended to intimidate or terrorize a population or to compel a State or an international organization to perform or refrain from performing any act. If injuries or illnesses result from the above acts, the penalty is imprisonment from twenty-one years to thirty years.

If death has resulted therefrom, the penalty is life imprisonment.

###### Section 6 : Des actes préparatoires et d'appui

###### Article 361-15 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from eleven years to twenty-one years anyone who provides or facilitates the provision of weapons, knowing that they may be used for the commission of one of the offenses provided for in this chapter.

###### Article 361-16 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from eleven years to twenty-one years anyone who recruits, maintains or provides training to another person to commit or participate in the commission of one of the offenses provided for in this chapter or asks him to commit or participate in the commission of one of these offenses or to join an association or a group, with the intent or knowing that the purpose of this recruitment or this request is to participate in the commission of one or more offenses provided for in this chapter.

###### Article 361-17 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from eleven years to twenty-one years: ˗ the national who travels or attempts to travel to a State other than his State of residence or of which he is a national, or any person who leaves or attempts to leave the national territory to travel to a State other than his State of residence or nationality, with the intent to commit, organize or prepare acts of terrorism, or in order to participate therein or to provide or receive training in terrorism;

˗ any person who deliberately provides or collects, by any means whatsoever, directly or indirectly, funds that he intends to use or knows will be used to finance the travel of persons who travel to a State other than their State of residence or of which they are nationals, with the intent to commit, organize or prepare acts of terrorism, or in order to participate therein or to provide or receive training in terrorism;

˗ any person who, on the national territory, deliberately organizes the travel of persons who travel to a State other than their State of residence or of which they are nationals, with the intent to commit, organize or prepare acts of terrorism, or in order to participate therein or to provide or receive training in terrorism, or participation in other activities that facilitate these acts, including recruitment.

###### Article 361-18 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from five years to ten years and a fine from two million (2,000,000) to twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs, the fact for any person of being unable to justify resources corresponding to his lifestyle, while being in regular contact with one or more persons engaging in one or more of the terrorist acts defined in this chapter.

###### Article 361-19 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from one year to ten years and a fine from one million (1,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, the act of publicly glorifying terrorist acts.

When the acts were committed using an online public communication service or through written or audiovisual press, the term of imprisonment is from eleven years to twenty-one years and a fine from one million (1,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 361-20 :

Constitutes an act of terrorism punished by a term of imprisonment from eleven years to twenty-one years and a fine from one million (1,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, the act of training or being trained in the handling of weapons or in any form of combat, in the manufacture or use of explosive, incendiary, nuclear, radiological, biological or chemical substances or in the piloting of aircraft for terrorist purposes.

###### Article 361-21 :

Constitutes an act of terrorism punished by a term of imprisonment from three years to five years and a fine from five hundred thousand (500,000) francs to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, the act of habitually consulting, for terrorist purposes, one or more online public communication services or possessing documents directly inciting the commission of acts of terrorism or glorifying them.

###### Section 7 : Du financement du terrorisme

###### Article 361-22 :

Offenses relating to the financing of terrorism are governed by the legislative and regulatory provisions relating to the fight against the financing of terrorism in Burkina Faso.

###### Section 8 : De la disposition particulière

###### Article 361-23 :

For the offenses provided for in this chapter, the competent court may, in the event of conviction, order the freezing, confiscation of property and prohibition of residence or territory.

##### CHAPITRE 2 : DE L'ASSOCIATION DE MALFAITEURS ET DE L'ASSISTANCE

AUX CRIMINELS

###### Article 362-1 :

Any association or agreement whatever its duration and the number of its members, formed or established for the purpose of preparing or committing crimes against persons or property, constitutes the crime of criminal association which exists by the mere fact of the resolution to act jointly agreed upon.

###### Article 362-2 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from eleven years to twenty-one years and a fine from five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, any individual who is part of the association or understanding defined in Article 362-1 above.

The imprisonment is from eleven years to thirty years and a fine from one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, for the leaders of the association or understanding or for those who exercised any command therein.

###### Article 362-3 :

Benefit from an absolute excuse those of the perpetrators who, before any attempt at a crime that is the object of the association or understanding and before any prosecution, were the first to reveal to the authorities the established understanding or the existence of the association.

###### Article 362-4 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from one year to ten years and a fine from five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, whoever knowingly harbors a person whom he knows to have committed a crime or an offense and whom he knows to be sought by justice for that reason, or who removes or attempts to remove that person from searches or arrest, or helps them to hide or to flee.

The above provision does not apply to the spouse, to the parents or relatives of the perpetrator of the crime or offense up to and including the fourth degree.

##### CHAPTER 3: ON REBELLION

###### Article 363-1 :

Any attack or resistance practiced with violence or assault against public officials or representatives of public authority acting for the execution of orders or ordinances emanating from that authority or laws, regulations, judicial decisions or warrants of justice constitutes rebellion.

Threats of violence having a serious character are treated as violence itself.

###### Article 363-2 :

Is punished by imprisonment from two months to one year and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, rebellion committed by one or two persons.

The term of imprisonment is from one year to three years and the fine from five hundred thousand (500,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, when the rebellion is committed in an assembly of more than two persons.

If the rebellion is committed by more than twenty persons, the perpetrators are punished by a term of imprisonment from five to ten years and a fine from one million (1,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 363-3 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from three months to two years and a fine from five hundred thousand (500,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, any person who commits a rebellion while carrying a weapon. The term of imprisonment is from two years to five years and the fine from seven hundred thousand (700,000) to seven million (7,000,000) CFA francs, if in the assembly, more than two individuals are carrying weapons.

If the rebellion is committed by more than twenty persons and there is carrying of weapons, the term of imprisonment is from eleven years to twenty-one years and the fine from three million (3,000,000) to fifteen million (15,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 363-4 :

For the offenses provided for in this chapter, the court seized may pronounce the prohibition of the exercise of civic rights and of serving in public functions, employment or offices for a duration that cannot exceed five years.

###### Article 363-5 :

Persons found carrying concealed weapons, having been part of a group or assembly reputed to be unarmed, are individually punished as in the case of an armed troop or assembly.

###### Article 363-6 :

Whoever incites rebellion either by speeches given in public places or gatherings, or by placards, posters, leaflets or writings, is punished as an accomplice to rebellion.

###### Article 363-7 :

A residence ban for a duration that cannot exceed five years may be pronounced against the instigators, leaders of the rebellion.

###### Article 363-8 :

In the case of rebellion in a group, no penalty is pronounced against the rebels who have no role to play in the group, who withdrew at the first warning from the public authority, or were seized outside the place of rebellion without resistance and without weapons.

###### Article 363-9 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from two months to six months and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, whoever, by assault, opposes the execution of works ordered or authorized by the public authority.

Those who, by gathering, threats or violence oppose the execution of these works, are punished by a term of imprisonment from three months to two years and a fine from five hundred thousand (500,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.

##### CHAPTER 4: ON ESCAPE AND OTHER VIOLATIONS OF REGULATIONS CONCERNING PRISON ADMINISTRATION

###### Article 364-1 :

Constitutes an escape the act, by a detainee, of evading the custody to which he is subject.

Escape is punished by a term of imprisonment from six months to two years and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs.

When the escape is attempted or accomplished by violence, breaking and entering or corruption, even if these offenses were committed, in concert with the detainee, by a third party, the imprisonment is from three years to five years and the fine from five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs. For the application of this article, any person is considered detained:

˗ who is placed in police custody;

˗ who is pending or being presented to the judicial authority at the end of police custody or in execution of a summons warrant or arrest warrant;

˗ who has been notified of a committal warrant, an arrest warrant or a provisional custody order continuing to have effect;

˗ who is serving a custodial sentence or who has been arrested to serve that sentence;

˗ who is placed under extradition detention;

˗ who is the subject of a body attachment order;

˗ who is placed under community service order.

###### Article 364-2 :

The sentence pronounced for escape is cumulative with any temporary custodial sentence imposed for the offense that motivated the arrest or detention.

If the prosecution of this latter offense results in an order or decision of no case to answer, a decision of acquittal or discharge, the duration of the pre-trial detention served on that count is not deducted from the duration of the sentence pronounced for escape or attempted escape.

###### Article 364-3 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from two months to three years and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, any person serving as escort or manning posts who, through negligence, permits or facilitates an escape.

###### Article 364-4 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from three years to five years and a fine from five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs the act, by any person in charge of his supervision, of facilitating or preparing, even by voluntary abstention, the escape of a detainee.

These provisions are also applicable to any person authorized by their functions to enter a prison establishment or to approach, in whatever capacity, detainees.

The perpetrator is subject to a ban from any public employment or any public function for a duration that cannot exceed five years.

###### Article 364-5 :

Is punished by imprisonment from six months to three years and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs the act of providing a detainee with any means to escape from the custody to which he was subject.

If the assistance thus provided is accompanied by violence, breaking and entering or corruption, the imprisonment is from three to five years and the fine from three hundred thousand (300,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs.

If this assistance consists of providing or using a weapon or an explosive, incendiary or toxic substance, the imprisonment is from five to ten years and the fine from three million (3,000,000) to ten million five hundred thousand (10,500,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 364-6 :

All those who knowingly procure or facilitate an escape are jointly and severally condemned to pay damages to the victim or his successors in title in reparation for the harm caused by the offense for which the escapee was detained.

In addition, a residence ban may be imposed for a duration that cannot exceed five years.

###### Article 364-7 :

Is punished by imprisonment from two months to one year and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs the act, wherever it occurs, of delivering or causing to reach a detainee, or of receiving from him and transmitting sums of money, correspondence, objects or substances of any kind, as well as communicating by any means with a detained person, outside of cases authorized by regulations.

The penalty is imprisonment from six months to two years and a fine from five hundred thousand (500,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs if the perpetrator is responsible for the supervision of detainees or if he is authorized by his duties to enter a penitentiary establishment or to approach, in any capacity whatsoever, detainees.

###### Article 364-8 :

Is punished by imprisonment from two months to one year and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs the act of entering a penitentiary establishment or scaling its enclosure, without being authorized by virtue of legislative or regulatory provisions or having been authorized by the competent authorities.

###### Article 364-9 :

The attempt of the offenses provided for in this chapter is punishable.

#### TITLE VII: OF OFFENSES AGAINST CURRENCY, MARKS, TITLES

OR OTHER FIDUCIARY VALUES ISSUED BY THE PUBLIC

AUTHORITY AND FORGERIES

##### CHAPTER 1: OF COUNTERFEITING OF CURRENCY

###### Section 1: General provisions

###### Article 371-1 :

The provisions of this chapter are intended to suppress counterfeiting of currency and other offenses against monetary signs. They apply to offenses committed:

˗ on the national territory;

˗ on the territory of other member States of WAEMU;

˗ abroad, outside the member States of WAEMU, according to the distinctions and conditions provided for by the legislation in force.

###### Article 371-2 :

For the purposes of this chapter, the following terms mean:

˗ competent authorities: bodies which, by virtue of a legal or regulatory provision, are authorized to carry out or order the acts or measures provided for by the uniform law on counterfeiting of currency;

˗ BCEAO or Central Bank: the Central Bank of West African States;

˗ counterfeiting: the manufacture of a monetary sign imitating a monetary sign issued by the BCEAO or any other authorized foreign issuing institute;

˗ foreigner: any person living in the Union without having the nationality of one of the member States of WAEMU;

˗ falsification: the alteration of a monetary sign with a view to modifying its substance or its weight;

˗ false currency (false banknotes or false coins): banknotes and coins which have the appearance of banknotes or coins issued by the BCEAO or any other authorized foreign issuing organization or, although intended to be put into circulation, have not yet been issued by the institutions authorized for this purpose;

˗ counterfeiting of currency:

 all fraudulent acts (counterfeiting and falsification) of manufacture or alteration of monetary signs issued by the BCEAO or any other authorized foreign issuing institute, for this purpose, whatever the means employed to produce the result;

 the putting into circulation of false currency with full knowledge;

 the act of holding, importing, exporting, transporting, receiving or obtaining false currency, with the purpose of putting it into circulation with full knowledge;

 the fraudulent act of manufacturing, holding, receiving or obtaining instruments, objects, computer programs or any other process intended, by their nature, for the manufacture of false currency, for the alteration of currency or for the manufacture of security features of monetary signs;

˗ FCFA: Franc of the African Financial Community;

˗ putting into circulation of false currency: the issuance of false currency, regardless of the number of banknotes or coins circulated;

˗ reproduction of monetary signs: creation of any tangible or intangible image that presents a resemblance to a banknote or the image of a coin, regardless of the size of the image, the materials, instruments and techniques used to produce it and regardless of whether the patterns, letters and symbols appearing on the monetary sign have been modified or not;

˗ monetary signs: banknotes or coins having or having had legal tender;

˗ WAMU or Union: West African Monetary Union.

###### Section 2: Offenses and applicable penalties

###### Article 371-3 :

The counterfeiting or falsification of banknotes or coins having legal tender on the national territory of a member State of WAEMU or abroad is punished by fixed-term imprisonment from ten years to twenty years and a fine of an amount equal to ten times the value of said signs without being less than twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs. Is punished by the same penalties, the counterfeiting or falsification of banknotes or coins which, although intended to be put into circulation, have not yet been issued by the institutions authorized for this purpose. If the offender benefits from mitigating circumstances, the penalty cannot be less than five years of imprisonment and a fine of five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

Suspension of sentence cannot be granted.

###### Article 371-4 :

The custodial sentence provided for in the first two paragraphs of the preceding article includes a safety period of seven years.

During the safety period, the convicted person cannot benefit from the provisions relating to the mode of adjustment of sentences, in particular those concerning the suspension or splitting of the sentence, placement outside, exit permits, semi-liberty and conditional release.

###### Article 371-5 :

The counterfeiting or falsification of banknotes or coins having had legal tender on the national territory or abroad, is punished by imprisonment from two years to five years and a fine of an amount equal to ten times the value of said signs, without being less than two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 371-6 :

The manufacture of banknotes and coins carried out using authorized installations or equipment intended for this purpose, when it is carried out in violation of the conditions set by the institutions authorized to issue these monetary signs and without their agreement, is punished by the penalties provided for in Article 371-3 above.

###### Article 371-7 :

The circulation, use, exhibition, distribution, importation, exportation, transport, receipt, possession, with full knowledge, of counterfeited or falsified monetary signs having legal tender, is punished by imprisonment of five years to seven years and a fine equal to ten times the value of said signs without being less than five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

When they are committed by an organized gang, the offenses provided for in the preceding paragraph are punished by the penalties provided for in Article 371-3 above. The circulation, use, exhibition, distribution, importation, exportation, transport, receipt, possession, of counterfeited or falsified monetary signs that had legal tender, with full knowledge, is punished by imprisonment of one year to three years and a fine equal to ten times the value of said signs, without being less than one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.

When they are committed by an organized gang, the offenses provided for in paragraph 3 of this article are punished by the penalties provided for in Article 371-5 above.

###### Article 371-8 :

Are punished by a fine equal to ten times their value, without the amount of the fine being less than two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) CFA francs, those who, having received monetary signs believing them to be good and who, after having known their defects, knowingly keep them and refrain from handing them over to the BCEAO or to the competent authorities.

Are punished by a fine equal to ten times their value, without the amount being less than two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, credit institutions, decentralized financial systems, manual exchange dealers and postal financial services who, having received during operations with their clientele, counterfeited or falsified monetary signs, have not retained them, against receipt, for the purpose of handing them over to the BCEAO or to the competent authorities.

###### Article 371-9 :

The recirculation, after having discovered the defects, of counterfeited or falsified banknotes that were believed to be good at the time of receipt, is punished by imprisonment of two years to five years and a fine equal to ten times the value of said signs without being less than five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 371-10 :

The manufacture, offer, receipt, importation, exportation, or possession, without authorization, of marks, materials, apparatus, instruments, computer programs, or any other element specifically intended for the manufacture or protection against counterfeiting or falsification of banknotes or coins is punished by imprisonment of five years to seven years and a fine of five million (5,000,000) CFA francs to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 371-11 :

The manufacture, possession, circulation, use, exhibition, distribution, importation or exportation of unauthorized monetary signs, intended to replace coins or banknotes having legal tender in the national territory or abroad, is punished by imprisonment of five years to seven years and a fine of five million

(5,000,000) CFA francs to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs.

Is punished by the same penalties, the possession, circulation, use, exhibition, distribution, importation or exportation of banknotes and coins which, although intended to be put into circulation, have not yet been issued by the institutions empowered for this purpose and do not yet have legal tender in the national territory or abroad.

###### Article 371-12 :

The manufacture, possession, circulation, use, exhibition, distribution, importation or exportation of printed materials, forms or tokens intended to be accepted as a means of payment, is punished by imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of two million

(2,000,000) CFA francs to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 371-13 :

Is punished by imprisonment of six months to one year and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) CFA francs to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, whoever:

˗ reproduces, totally or partially, by any process whatsoever, monetary signs having legal tender in the national territory or abroad, without the prior authorization of the BCEAO or, in the case of foreign monetary signs, of the authority that issued them;

˗ exhibits, distributes, imports or exports reproductions of monetary signs, including by way of newspapers, books or prospectuses without the prior authorization of the Central Bank or, in the case of foreign monetary signs, of the authority that issued them;

˗ uses banknotes having legal tender in the national territory or abroad, as a medium for any advertising.

###### Article 371-14 :

The deliberate deterioration, defacement or overloading of a monetary sign is punished by imprisonment of one month to six months and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, when it has the effect of rendering it unfit for use as a means of payment.

###### Article 371-15 :

The refusal to receive currency having legal tender in a Member State of the Union according to the value for which it has tender is punished by a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 371-16 :

The collection of a commission in exchange for the handing over of monetary signs issued by the BCEAO against other monetary signs of its issue, is punished by imprisonment of one year to three years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) CFA francs to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 371-17 :

Are confiscated, whatever the qualification of the offense, the counterfeited or falsified monetary signs and others referred to in Articles 371-3 to 371-14 above as well as the metals, papers and other materials found in the possession of the culprits and intended for the commission of similar offenses. Are also confiscated, the instruments having served to commit the offense, except when they have been used without the knowledge of their owner.

###### Article 371-18 :

The competent court mandatorily pronounces against natural persons found guilty of the offenses provided for in Articles 371-3 to 371-12 above, the following complementary penalties:

˗ the prohibition from exercising an activity in the banking and financial sector for a period not exceeding twenty years;

˗ the prohibition of residence, definitively, or for a period not exceeding twenty years for foreigners.

It may, in addition, pronounce, against them, the prohibition of civil rights for a period not exceeding twenty years.

###### Article 371-19 :

Legal persons other than the State are criminally liable for the offenses defined herein, when they are committed on their behalf by their organs or representatives.

The legal person found criminally liable is, without prejudice to the sanctions incurred by the natural persons co-perpetrators or accomplices of the same acts, punished by a fine equal to five times the amount provided for natural persons.

The competent court also pronounces the following complementary penalties:

˗ the dissolution of the legal person, when it has been created or diverted from its corporate purpose to commit the offenses referred to in Articles 371-3 to 371-12 above;

˗ the definitive closure of the enterprise or for a period between one year and five years.

###### Article 371-20 :

The attempt of the offenses referred to in this chapter is punishable.

###### Article 371-21 :

In case of recidivism, the penalties provided for in this chapter are doubled.

Convictions pronounced by the criminal courts of a WAEMU Member State for the offenses provided for by this law are taken into account as recidivism in all other Member States.

###### Article 371-22 :

Any person who, having participated in the offenses provided for in articles 371-3 to 371-12 above, has informed the competent authorities or revealed the perpetrators before any prosecution, is exempted from penalties. Such person may, nevertheless, be subject to a residency ban if he or she has foreign national status. Any person who, having participated in the offenses referred to in the preceding paragraph, has, after the initiation of prosecution, enabled the arrest of the other participants, may be exempted from penalties, totally or partially. Such person may, nevertheless, be subject to a residency ban if he or she has foreign national status.

###### Article 371-23 :

When pronouncing a conviction in application of the provisions of this chapter, the competent court may order the posting or dissemination of all or part of the decision or of a communiqué informing the public of the grounds and operative part thereof.

It determines, where applicable, the excerpts from the decision and the terms of the communiqué that must be posted or disseminated.

The posting or dissemination of the decision or communiqué may include the identity of the victim only with his or her consent or that of his or her legal representative or successors in title.

Posting is carried out in the places and for the duration indicated by the court. Unless the court decides otherwise, posting may not exceed two months. In case of removal, concealment, or tearing of the posted notices, posting is carried out again at the expense of the person found guilty of these acts.

The dissemination of the decision is made by one or more press publications or by one or more electronic communication services to the public. The publications or electronic communication services to the public responsible for this dissemination are designated by the court. They may not object to this dissemination.

Posting and dissemination may be ordered cumulatively. The posting of the pronounced decision or the dissemination thereof is at the expense of the convicted person. The costs of posting or dissemination recovered from the latter may not, however, exceed the maximum fine incurred.

###### Section 3 : On the applicable procedure

###### Article 371-24 :

By way of derogation from the provisions of the Code of Criminal Procedure, the correctional courts have jurisdiction over the felonies provided for in this chapter.

The procedure followed is that applicable in correctional matters.

###### Article 371-25 :

By way of derogation from the provisions of the Code of Criminal Procedure, for the offenses provided for in this chapter, the public action is time-barred according to the following distinctions:

˗ by twenty years for felonies;

˗ by ten years for misdemeanors.

###### Article 371-26 :

When they are seized with cases relating to counterfeiting or discover, during their investigations, counterfeit or falsified monetary signs, the competent authorities are required to transmit to the Central Bank, for analysis and identification, at least one specimen of each type of banknotes or coins suspected to be counterfeit.

The provisions of the preceding paragraph are not applicable when there exists only one specimen of a type of banknotes or coins suspected to be counterfeit, as long as it is necessary for the manifestation of the truth.

###### Article 371-27 :

Counterfeit or falsified monetary signs as well as materials and instruments intended to be used for their manufacture, confiscated in application of article 371-17, are handed over to the Central Bank for their possible destruction, subject to the requirements of the administration of justice.

###### Article 371-28 :

When the Central Bank recognizes as counterfeit or falsified monetary signs that are submitted to it, it is authorized to retain them and possibly to destroy them, subject to the requirements of the administration of justice.

##### CHAPITRE 2 : ON THE COUNTERFEITING OF STATE SEALS, PUBLIC OR PRIVATE INSTRUMENTS, HALLMARKS, STAMPS AND MARKS

###### Article 372-1 :

Anyone who counterfeits a State seal or uses a counterfeit seal is punished by imprisonment of twenty-one years to thirty years and a fine of ten million (10,000,000) to one hundred million (100,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 372-2 :

Anyone who counterfeits or falsifies either one or more national stamps, or one or more State hallmarks used to mark gold or silver materials, or who uses falsified or counterfeit stamps or hallmarks, is punished by imprisonment of eleven years to thirty years and a fine of ten million (10,000,000) to fifty million (50,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 372-3 :

Anyone who, having unlawfully obtained genuine State stamps or hallmarks designated in the preceding article, makes an application or use thereof that is detrimental to the rights and interests of the State, its subdivisions and third parties, is punished by imprisonment of five years to ten years and a fine of five million (5,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 372-4 :

Anyone who commits any of the following acts is punished by imprisonment of two months to six months and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs:

˗ manufactures seals, stamps, marks, or seals of the State or of any authority without the written order of the accredited representatives of the State or of this authority;

˗ manufactures, holds, distributes, purchases or sells stamps, seals, marks or seals likely to be confused with those of the State or of any authority, even foreign.

###### Article 372-5 :

Anyone who manufactures, sells, peddles, distributes or uses printed matter which, by its format, color, text, typographical layout or any other characteristic, bears a resemblance to the letterheads or official printed matter in use in institutions, public administrations and the various courts, of a nature to cause confusion in the mind of the public, is punished by imprisonment of one to five years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 372-6 :

Anyone who commits any of the following acts is punished by imprisonment of one to five years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs:

˗ counterfeits marks intended to be affixed in the name of the government or of a public service on various kinds of foodstuffs or goods, or who uses these false marks;

˗ counterfeits the seal, stamp or mark of any authority or uses the counterfeit seal, stamp or mark;

˗ counterfeits or falsifies postage stamps, franking impressions or reply coupons issued by the Burkinabe postal administration, mobile fiscal stamps, stamped papers or forms, impressions, reply coupons, counterfeit or falsified stamped papers or forms. In addition, a prohibition from exercising civic and professional rights and/or a residency ban may be pronounced for a period which may not exceed five years.

###### Article 372-7 :

Is punished by imprisonment from six months to three years and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250 000) to one million (1 000 000) CFA francs, whoever having unlawfully obtained genuine seals, marks or printed matter provided for in the preceding article, makes or attempts to make fraudulent application or use thereof.

In addition, prohibition from exercising civil and/or professional rights and/or prohibition from residence may be pronounced for a period that cannot exceed five years.

###### Article 372-8 :

Is punished by imprisonment from two months to one year and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250 000) to six hundred thousand (600 000) CFA francs, whoever:

˗ knowingly uses postage stamps, mobile stamps or stamped papers or forms that have already been used or who, by any means, alters stamps in order to prevent their cancellation and thus allow their subsequent reuse;

˗ overprints by printing, perforation or any other means Burkinabé postage stamps or other postal fiduciary securities, expired or not, or who sells, hawks, offers, distributes, exports postage stamps thus overprinted;

˗ counterfeits, imitates or alters stamps, labels, postage meter impressions or reply coupons issued by the postal service of a foreign country, sells, hawks or distributes said stamps, labels, postage meter impressions or reply coupons or knowingly uses them.

###### Article 372-9 :

Is punished by imprisonment from six months to two years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300 000) to one million five hundred thousand (1 500 000) CFA francs, whoever is guilty of manufacturing, introducing into Burkina Faso, selling or distributing any objects, tokens, printed matter or forms, obtained by any process whatsoever and which, by their appearance, present with annuity certificates, shares, bonds, interest shares, dividend or interest coupons related thereto and generally with fiduciary securities issued by companies, firms or private enterprises, a resemblance such as to facilitate the acceptance of said objects, tokens, printed matter or forms in place of the imitated securities.

In addition, prohibition from exercising civil and professional rights and prohibition from residence may be pronounced for a period that cannot exceed five years.

###### Article 372-10 :

Is punished by imprisonment from twenty-one to thirty years and a fine of ten million (10 000 000) to one hundred million (100 000 000) CFA francs, whoever counterfeits, falsifies or alters certificates, notes or bonds issued by the public treasury with its stamp or mark, or interest coupons related to these certificates, notes or bonds.

###### Article 372-11 :

Is punished by imprisonment from twenty-one to thirty years and a fine of ten million (10 000 000) to one hundred million (100 000 000) CFA francs, whoever, in any manner whatsoever, knowingly participates in the issue, distribution, sale or introduction into Burkina Faso of the certificates, notes or bonds designated in the preceding article.

###### Article 372-12 :

Is punished by imprisonment from two years to ten years and a fine of six hundred thousand (600 000) to two million (2 000 000) CFA francs, whoever manufactures, acquires, possesses or transfers knowingly products or equipment intended for the commission of the offenses punished above unless the act constitutes a more serious offense.

###### Article 372-13 :

Benefits from an absolute excuse the one among the perpetrators of the crimes mentioned in Articles 372-10 and 372-11 above who, before the commission of these crimes and before any prosecution, gives notice thereof to the authorities and reveals the identity of the perpetrators or who, even after prosecution has begun, facilitates the arrest of the other perpetrators.

###### Article 372-14 :

In the case of the offenses referred to in Articles 372-9, 372-10 and 372-12 above, the trial court orders the confiscation of products and equipment.

It may also order the closure of the establishment for a period that cannot exceed five years.

##### CHAPITRE 3 : FORGERY

###### Article 373-1 :

Constitutes forgery, any fraudulent alteration of the truth likely to cause harm and accomplished by any means whatsoever, in a writing or any other medium of expression of thought which has the object or which may have the effect of establishing proof of a right or of a fact having legal consequences.

###### Article 373-2 :

Is punished by imprisonment from three months to three years and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250 000) to nine hundred thousand (900 000) CFA francs, whoever has unlawfully obtained or attempts to unlawfully obtain an authentic or public document:

˗ either by making false declarations;

˗ either by taking a false name or a false qualification;

˗ either by providing false information, certificates or attestations.

###### Article 373-3 :

Is punished by imprisonment from six months to five years and a fine of one million (1 000 000) to ten million (10 000 000) CFA francs, whoever has taken the name of a third party in circumstances which have determined or could have determined the registration of a conviction in the criminal record of that person. Is punished by the penalties provided for in the first paragraph whoever, by false declarations relating to the civil status of a person under investigation, has knowingly been the cause of the registration of a conviction on the criminal record of someone other than that person under investigation.

###### Article 373-4 :

Is punished by imprisonment from three months to three years and a fine of one hundred thousand (100 000) to one million (1 000 000) CFA francs whoever, by taking a false name or a false qualification, has obtained an extract of the criminal record of a third party.

Is punished by the same penalties whoever provides imaginary identity information which causes or could have caused erroneous entries in the criminal record.

###### Article 373-5 :

Is punished by imprisonment from one year to five years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300 000) to one million five hundred thousand (1 500 000) CFA francs, whoever not being a party to the act who makes before a public authority a declaration that he or she knew not to be in conformity with the truth.

However, benefits from an absolute excuse whoever, having made as a witness before a public authority a declaration not in conformity with the truth, retracts before any harm results from the use of the act for others and before he or she is himself or herself subject to prosecution.

###### Article 373-6:

Is punished by a sentence of imprisonment from one year to five years and a fine from three hundred thousand (300,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who commits or attempts to commit forgery in private, commercial or banking writing, under one of the following conditions:

˗ either by counterfeiting or alteration of writing or signature;

˗ either by fabrication of agreements, provisions, obligations or discharges or by their subsequent insertion in these acts;

˗ either by addition, omission or alteration of clauses, declarations or facts that these acts were intended to receive or establish;

˗ either by impersonation or substitution of persons.

The penalty is imprisonment from three years to ten years and a fine from one million (1,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, when the perpetrator is a banker, an administrator or a company director and generally a person having made a public offering for the issuance of shares, bonds, vouchers, shares or any securities, either of a company or of a commercial or industrial enterprise.

It may further be pronounced against the perpetrators of the above-mentioned acts the prohibition from exercising civil and professional rights and the prohibition of residence for a period that cannot exceed five years.

###### Article 373-7:

In cases of forgery in private, commercial or banking writing, the person who uses the document that he knew to be false is punished by the penalties provided for in Article 373-4 above.

###### Article 373-8:

Public writing is the work, or is deemed to be the work of a public agent. A public agent means any civilian or military person who holds a legislative, executive, administrative or judicial mandate, whether appointed or elected, on a permanent or temporary basis, whether remunerated or not, and regardless of their hierarchical level or seniority.

Authentic writing is the work that emanates or is deemed to emanate from a public officer or a person designated by law to draw up certain acts or make certain findings.

Commercial or banking writing is writing that is intended to establish a transaction constituting an act of commerce.

Private writing is that which is neither public, nor authentic, nor commercial.

###### Article 373-9:

Is punished by a sentence of imprisonment from eleven years to thirty years and a fine from five million (5,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, any civil servant or public officer who, in the exercise of his functions, commits forgery in authentic or public writing:

˗ either by false signature;

˗ either by alteration of acts, writings or signatures;

˗ either by impersonation or substitution of persons;

˗ either by writings made or inserted in registers or in other public acts, after their completion or closure.

###### Article 373-10:

Is punished by a sentence of imprisonment from eleven years to thirty years and a fine from five million (5,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, any civil servant or public officer who, in drafting acts of his function, fraudulently distorts their substance or circumstances either:

˗ by writing agreements other than those that were drawn up or dictated by the parties;

˗ by establishing as true facts that he knew to be false;

˗ by attesting as having been confessed or having occurred in his presence facts that were not;

˗ by voluntarily omitting or modifying declarations received by him.

###### Article 373-11:

Is punished by a sentence of imprisonment from five years to ten years and a fine from one million (1,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, anyone other than the person designated in the preceding article who commits forgery in authentic or public writing.

###### Article 373-12:

Is punished by a sentence of imprisonment from two years to ten years and a fine from one million (1,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who, in the cases mentioned above, uses the document that he knew to be false.

###### Article 373-13:

Are punished by a sentence of imprisonment from two months to six months and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, the lodging house keepers, hoteliers and innkeepers who knowingly register in their registers under false or assumed names the persons lodging with them or who, in collusion with them, fail to register them.

They are further civilly liable for restitution, compensation and costs awarded to the victims of crimes and offenses committed during their stay by these persons.

The closure of the establishment may be pronounced.

###### Article 373-14:

It may further be pronounced against the perpetrators of the above-mentioned acts the prohibition from exercising civil and professional rights and the prohibition of residence for a period that cannot exceed five years.

##### CHAPTER 4: ON FALSE TESTIMONY, FALSE OATH AND

FAILURE TO TESTIFY

###### Article 374-1:

False testimony is the voluntary alteration of the truth made under oath by a witness in a deposition that has become irrevocable for the purpose of deceiving justice in favor of or against one of the parties.

###### Article 374-2:

Is punished by a sentence of imprisonment from one year to ten years and a fine from one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who is guilty of false testimony in criminal matters against the accused or in his favor.

The penalty is imprisonment from five years to ten years and a fine from two million (2,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs if the false witness received money, any reward or promises.

In the event of conviction of the accused to life imprisonment, the false witness who testified against him incurs imprisonment from eleven years to thirty years and a fine from five million (5,000,000) to fifty million (50,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 374-3:

Is punished by a sentence of imprisonment from one year to five years and a fine from six hundred thousand (600,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs, anyone who is guilty of false testimony in misdemeanor matters against the defendant or in his favor.

The maximum penalty is pronounced if the false witness received money, any reward or promises.

###### Article 374-4:

Is punished by a sentence of imprisonment from three to six months and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, anyone who is guilty of false testimony in simple police matters against the defendant or in his favor.

The maximum penalty is pronounced if the false witness received money, any reward or promises.

###### Article 374-5:

Is punished by a sentence of imprisonment from one year to ten years and a fine from six hundred thousand (600,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who is guilty of false testimony in civil, commercial, social or administrative matters.

The maximum penalty is pronounced if the false witness received money, any reward or promises.

###### Article 374-6:

Commits the offense of suborning a witness and is punished by a sentence of imprisonment from one year to five years and a fine from one million (1,000

000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who in any matter, at any stage of proceedings or with a view to a claim or defense in court, uses promises, offers or gifts, pressure, threats, physical violence, maneuvers or artifices to induce another to make or deliver a false testimony, declaration or attestation, whether or not the subornation produced effect, unless the act constitutes complicity in one of the more serious offenses provided for in Articles 374-2, 374-3, 374-4 and 374-5 above.

###### Article 374-7 :

Is punished by the penalties for false testimony according to the distinctions provided for in Articles 374-2, 374-3, 374-4 and 374-5 above, the interpreter who, during a trial, knowingly distorts the substance of oral statements or documents translated orally.
