Where the distortion is made in the written translation of a document intended or capable of establishing proof of a right or of a fact having legal effects, the interpreter is punished by the penalties for forgery in writing according to the distinctions provided for in Articles 373-7, 373-8, 373-9 and 373-10 above.

###### Article 374-8 :

Is liable to the penalties for false testimony according to the distinctions provided for in Articles 374-2 to 374-5 above, the expert who, appointed by the judicial authority, gives orally or in writing, at any stage of the proceedings, a false opinion or affirms facts that he knows to be not in conformity with reality.

###### Article 374-9 :

The subornation of an interpreter or expert is punished as the subornation of a witness according to the provisions of Article 374-6 above.

###### Article 374-10 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from one year to five years and a fine from three hundred thousand (300,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs, anyone to whom an oath is deferred or referred in civil matters and who takes a false oath.

###### Article 374-11 :

Anyone who, knowing the proof of the innocence of a person provisionally incarcerated or convicted for an offence or crime, voluntarily refrains from immediately providing testimony thereof to the judicial or police authorities, is punished:

˗ if it concerns a crime, by a term of imprisonment from two years to five years and a fine from six hundred thousand (600,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs;

˗ if it concerns an offence, by a term of imprisonment from two months to two years and a fine from two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs.

However, no penalty is incurred by one who provides his testimony belatedly but spontaneously.

The provisions of this article do not apply:

˗ to relatives in direct line up to and including the fourth degree and their spouses;

˗ to brothers and sisters and their spouses.

##### CHAPITRE 5 : DE L'USURPATION OU DE L'USAGE IRREGULIER

DE FONCTIONS

###### Article 375-1 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from one year to five years and a fine from five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, unless the act constitutes a more serious offence, anyone who, without title, interferes in public, civil or military functions or performs an act of these functions.

###### Article 375-2 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from three months to two years and a fine from five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, unless more severe penalties are provided for by a special text, anyone who, without fulfilling the conditions required to hold it, uses or claims a title attached to a legally regulated profession, an official diploma or a qualification the conditions for attribution of which are fixed by the public authority.

###### Article 375-3 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from three months to one year and a fine from five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who, without right, publicly wears a regulatory uniform, a distinctive costume of a function or qualification, an official insignia or a decoration of a national or foreign order, unless the act is retained as an aggravating circumstance of a more serious offence.

###### Article 375-4 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from two months to six months and a fine from two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, anyone who, either in an official act or habitually, unduly attributes to himself a title or an honorific distinction.

###### Article 375-5 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from two months to six months and a fine from two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, anyone who publicly wears a costume presenting a resemblance likely to intentionally cause a mistake in the mind of the public with military and paramilitary uniforms or of any official exercising functions of judicial police or of auxiliary police forces.

###### Article 375-6 :

Is punished by a fine from fifty thousand two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, anyone who, without a public or authentic act, unduly attributes to himself an identity other than that resulting from the recitals of his civil status documents.

###### Article 375-7 :

In the cases provided for in the preceding articles, the trial court may order the application of the provisions of Article 214-17 of this law and furthermore that mention of the judgment be made in the margin of authentic acts or acts of civil status in which the title was unduly taken or the name altered.

###### Article 375-8 :

Is punished by a fine from two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, anyone who, exercising the profession of business agent or legal or tax adviser, makes or allows to appear his qualification as a former magistrate, former judicial police officer, former notary, former bailiff, former lawyer, official, former official, military officer or former military officer on any documents or writings whatsoever used in the context of his activity.

###### Article 375-9 :

Are punished by a term of imprisonment from two months to six months and a fine from two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, the founders, directors or managers of a company or establishment with a commercial, industrial or financial purpose who make or allow to appear the name of a member of the government or of an institution with mention of his qualification in any publicity made in the interest of the enterprise that they manage or that they propose to found.

Are punished by the same penalties, the founders, directors or managers of a company or establishment with a commercial, industrial or financial purpose who make or allow to appear the name of a former member of the government, of a magistrate or former magistrate, of an official or former official or of a high dignitary with mention of his qualification in any publicity made in the interest of the enterprise that they manage or that they propose to found.

##### CHAPITRE 6 : DES FRAUDES AUX EXAMENS ET CONCOURS PUBLICS

###### Article 376-1 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from six months to three years and a fine from five hundred thousand (500,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who, by any means and in whatever form, commits fraud in or on the occasion of an examination or public competition having as its purpose entry into a public administration or the obtaining of an official diploma.

###### Article 376-2 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from six months to two years and a fine from two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who through imprudence, negligence or failure to observe regulations facilitates fraud in an examination or a competition.

###### Article 376-3 :

The imprisonment is from six months to three years and the fine from three hundred thousand (300,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, where the fraud is committed on the occasion of an examination or competition other than public.

###### Article 376-4 :

Are notably considered as fraud in an exam or competition all practices tending to:

˗ transmit, communicate, disseminate or sell test papers, their answer keys or their solutions;

˗ substitute said test papers, results or candidate lists; ˗ modify by adding or removing grades or candidate names from lists relating to said exams or competitions;

˗ communicate a code or any sign to certain candidates, with a view to identifying them and awarding them an undeserved grade;

˗ corrupt a grader, an examiner, a supervisor, a jury president, a candidate or any person participating in the organization of the competition or exam;

˗ carry out any blackmail against an examiner, a grader, a supervisor, a jury president, a candidate or any person participating in the organization of the competition or exam;

˗ fraudulently use any means of communication.

###### Article 376-5 :

The attempt to commit the offenses provided for in this chapter is punishable.

#### TITRE VIII: NARCOTICS AND DRUGS

##### CHAPITRE 1: HIGH-RISK DRUGS

###### Article 381-1 :

Is punished by imprisonment from eleven to twenty-one years and a fine from five million (5,000,000) to twenty-five million (25,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who contravenes the legislative and regulatory provisions concerning the cultivation, production, manufacture, extraction, preparation or transformation of high-risk drugs.

###### Article 381-2 :

Is punished by imprisonment from eleven to twenty-one years and a fine from ten million (10,000,000) to fifty million (50,000,000) CFA francs anyone who contravenes the legislative and regulatory provisions concerning the importation, exportation and international transport of high-risk drugs.

###### Article 381-3 :

Is punished by imprisonment from eleven to twenty-one years and a fine from five million (5,000,000) to twenty-five million (25,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who contravenes the legislative and regulatory provisions concerning the offering, placing on sale, distribution, brokerage, sale, delivery in any capacity whatsoever, sending, dispatch, transport, purchase and possession of high-risk drugs.

###### Article 381-4 :

Is punished by imprisonment from two to five years and a fine from one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who contravenes the legislative and regulatory provisions concerning the use or possession of high-risk drugs for personal consumption purposes.

###### Article 381-5 :

Is punished by imprisonment from five to ten years and a fine from five million (5,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs anyone who: ˗ facilitates the illicit use of high-risk drugs by another person, for payment or free of charge, either by providing premises for this purpose or by any other means. This is notably the case for owners, managers, directors, operators in any capacity whatsoever of a hotel, furnished house, boarding house, drinking establishment, restaurant, club, circle, dance hall, any place of entertainment open to the public or used by the public or any other place, who tolerate the use of high-risk drugs in said establishments or their annexes or in said places. Fraudulent intent is presumed in the event of a positive check by a police service;

˗ issues accommodating prescriptions for high-risk drugs;

˗ knowing the fictitious or accommodating nature of the prescriptions, dispenses high-risk drugs;

˗ by means of fictitious or accommodating prescriptions, obtains or attempts to obtain high-risk drugs.

###### Article 381-6 :

Is punished by imprisonment from eleven years to twenty-one years and a fine from ten million (10,000,000) to fifty million (50,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who in any manner whatsoever and by any means, causes high-risk drugs to be consumed by a person without their knowledge.

###### Article 381-7 :

Is punished by imprisonment from two years to five years and a fine from five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs anyone who transfers or offers high-risk drugs to a person for their personal consumption.

##### CHAPITRE 2: AT-RISK DRUGS

###### Article 382-1 :

Is punished by imprisonment from five to ten years and a fine from five million (5,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who contravenes the legislative and regulatory provisions concerning the cultivation, production, transformation, importation, exportation, offering, placing on sale, distribution, brokerage, sale, delivery in any capacity whatsoever, sending, dispatch, transport, purchase and possession of at-risk drugs.

In the case of offering or transfer to a person for their personal consumption, or in the case of use or possession for personal consumption purposes, the imprisonment is from two to five years and the fine from five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 382-2 :

Is punished by imprisonment from two to five years and a fine from five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs anyone who:

˗ facilitates the illicit use of at-risk drugs by another person, for payment or free of charge either by providing premises for this purpose or by any other means. This is notably the case for owners, managers, directors, operators in any capacity whatsoever, of a hotel, furnished house, boarding house, club, circle, dance hall, any place of entertainment open to the public or used by the public or any other place, who tolerate the use of at-risk drugs in said establishments or their annexes or in said places. Fraudulent intent is presumed in the event of a positive check by a police service;

˗ issues accommodating prescriptions for at-risk drugs;

˗ knowing the fictitious or accommodating nature of the prescriptions, dispenses at-risk drugs;

˗ by means of fictitious or accommodating prescriptions, obtains or attempts to obtain at-risk drugs.

###### Article 382-3 :

Is punished by imprisonment from five to ten years and a fine from five million (5,000,000) to twenty-five million (25,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who, in any manner whatsoever and by any means causes at-risk drugs to be consumed by a person without their knowledge.

##### CHAPITRE 3: PRECURSORS

###### Article 383-1 :

Is punished by imprisonment from five to ten years and a fine from five million (5,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who produces, manufactures, imports, exports, transports, offers, sells, distributes, delivers in any capacity whatsoever, dispatches, purchases, sends or possesses precursors, equipment and materials, either with the aim of using them in or for the illicit cultivation, production or manufacture of drugs, or knowing that these precursors, equipment or materials must be used for such purposes.

##### CHAPTER 4: COMMON PROVISIONS APPLICABLE TO ALL DRUGS

###### Article 384-1:

Is punished by imprisonment from eleven to twenty-one years and a fine from fifty million (50,000,000) to one hundred million (100,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who:

˗ facilitates by any fraudulent means, the false justification of the origin of the resources or property of the perpetrator of the offenses provided for in this section;

˗ knowingly contributes to any operation of placement, conversion or concealment of proceeds or reconverts into the national economy the resources acquired through the commission of these offenses; ˗ acquires, holds or uses gains and resources, knowing that they come from one of the offenses listed in the preceding paragraphs.

###### Article 384-2:

When serious evidence suggests that a person is transporting drugs concealed in their body, judicial police officers and customs officials authorized to establish the offense may submit them to medical screening examinations.

Is punished by imprisonment from three months to one year and a fine from five hundred thousand (500,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, any person against whom there exists serious evidence suggesting that they could be transporting drugs concealed in their body who refuses to submit to the medical screening examination.

###### Article 384-3:

Anyone who by any means incites the commission of one of the offenses provided for in articles 381-1 to 383-1 of this law, even if such incitement has not been followed by effects, is punished by the penalties provided for the offense.

###### Article 384-4:

The attempt of one of the offenses provided for in articles 381-1 to 383-1 of this law, is punished as the completed offense.

The same applies to conspiracy or association formed with a view to committing one of these offenses.

###### Article 384-5:

Financial operations relating to one of the offenses provided for in articles 381-1 to 383-1 of this law are punished as the offense itself.

###### Article 384-6:

The penalties provided for in articles 381-1 to 383-1 of this law may be pronounced even when the various acts constituting the elements of the offense were accomplished in different countries.

##### CHAPTER 5: PROVISIONS RELATING TO THE ABUSE OF NARCOTICS

AND PSYCHOTROPIC SUBSTANCES

###### Article 385-1:

The use without medical prescriptions of controlled drugs is prohibited on the national territory. Any drug found in the possession of a person who uses it illicitly is seized and confiscated by court decision, even if said person is not subject to prosecution.

###### Article 385-2:

Notwithstanding the provisions of articles 381-1 to 383-1 of this law, anyone who, illicitly, purchases, holds or cultivates plants or substances classified as narcotics or psychotropic substances, the small quantity of which allows them to be considered intended for their personal consumption is punished:

˗ if it involves a plant or substance classified as a high-risk drug including cannabis oil, by imprisonment from one month to one year and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs;

˗ if it involves a derivative of the cannabis plant other than cannabis oil, by imprisonment from one to six months and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs;

˗ if it involves a plant or substance classified as a risk drug, by imprisonment from fifteen days to three months and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs.

The person concerned may be exempted from penalty or from its execution: ˗ if they have not reached the age of criminal majority;

˗ if they are not in a state of recidivism.

###### Article 385-3:

Any person who drives a land, river, or air motor vehicle under the influence of a drug, even in the absence of any outward sign of such illicitly consumed drug, is punished by imprisonment from one to three years and a fine from five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

Any person who refuses to submit to screening tests and verifications is punished by the penalties provided for in the preceding paragraph. When there is reason to apply the provisions relating to involuntary homicide and injuries, the penalties provided for in the first paragraph are doubled.

##### CHAPTER 6: SUPPLY TO MINORS OF TOXIC CHEMICAL

INHALANTS

###### Article 386-1:

Is punished by imprisonment from two to five years and a fine from five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who, knowingly, supplies to a minor one of the toxic chemical inhalants appearing on the list established by the minister responsible for health.

##### CHAPTER 7: PENALTY REGIME

###### Section 1: Causes of aggravation, exemption and mitigation of

penalties

###### Article 387-1:

The maximum penalties provided for in articles 381-1 to 384-5 of this law are doubled when:

˗ the perpetrator of the offense belongs to an organized gang or to a criminal association;

˗ the perpetrator of the offense has participated in other illegal activities facilitated by the commission of the offense;

˗ the perpetrator of the offense has used violence or weapons;

˗ the perpetrator of the offense exercises a public function and the offense is committed in the exercise or on the occasion of the exercise of their functions;

˗ the offense is committed by a health professional or by a person responsible for combating drug abuse or illicit drug trafficking; ˗ the drug is delivered or offered, or its use is facilitated to a minor, or a mentally disabled person or to a person undergoing detoxification treatment;

˗ a minor or a mentally disabled person has participated in the offense;

˗ the drugs delivered cause death or seriously compromise the health of one or more persons;

˗ the offense is committed in a penitentiary institution, a military establishment, an educational or training establishment, a hospital or care establishment, a social services center or in other places where schoolchildren or students engage in educational, sports or social activities, or in the immediate vicinity of these establishments and places, as well as in places of worship; ˗ the perpetrator of the offense has added to the drugs substances that aggravate the dangers;

˗ the perpetrator of the offense is in a state of recidivism.

Convictions pronounced abroad are taken into consideration for the establishment of recidivism.

###### Article 387-2:

Any person guilty of participation in an association or conspiracy with a view to committing one of the offenses provided for in articles 381-1 to 383-1 of this law is exempted from penalties if, having revealed the existence of this association or conspiracy to the judicial authority, they thus make it possible to prevent the commission of the offense and/or the identification of the other persons involved.

###### Article 387-3:

Apart from the cases provided for in the preceding article, any person who is the perpetrator or accomplice of one of the offenses listed in articles 381-1 to 383-1 of this law who, before any prosecution allows or facilitates the identification of the other perpetrators or after the initiation of prosecution allows or facilitates their arrest, shall be punished by half of the penalties provided for in said articles. However, said person is also exempted from the fine and where applicable, from ancillary measures and optional complementary penalties.

###### Section 2 : Penalties and Accessory or Complementary Measures

###### Article 387-4 :

In all cases provided for in Articles 381-1 to 383-1 of this law, the courts shall order the confiscation of the seized plants and substances, which are destroyed or handed over to an authorized body for their lawful use.

###### Article 387-5 :

In all cases provided for in Articles 381-1 to 383-1 of this law, the courts shall order the confiscation of installations, materials, equipment and other movable property used or intended to be used for the commission of the offense, to whomever they belong, unless the owners establish that they were unaware of their fraudulent use.

###### Article 387-6 :

In all cases provided for in Articles 381-1 to 383-1 of this law, the courts shall order the confiscation of the proceeds of the offense, movable or immovable property into which the proceeds are transformed or converted and up to their value, lawfully acquired property to which said proceeds are mixed, as well as revenues and other benefits derived from these proceeds, property into which they are transformed or invested or property to which they are mixed, to whomever these proceeds and these properties belong, unless the owners establish that they were unaware of their fraudulent origin.

###### Article 387-7 :

In the cases provided for in Articles 381-1 to 384-5 of this law, the courts may pronounce:

˗ permanent banishment from the territory or prohibition of residence in the territory for a period of ten years or more against any convicted foreigner;

˗ prohibition of residence for a period of two to five years if the offense is a misdemeanor and from five to twenty years if the offense is a felony;

˗ prohibition from leaving the national territory for a period of six months to three years;

˗ deprivation of civil, civic and family rights for a period of six months to three years;

˗ prohibition from driving motor vehicles on land, water and air and withdrawal of permits or licenses for a period of six months to three years;

˗ permanent prohibition or prohibition for a period of six months to three years from exercising the profession in the course of which the offense was committed;

˗ confiscation of all or part of the lawfully acquired property of the convicted person, whatever its nature, movable or immovable, divided or undivided.

In the cases provided for in the first paragraph of Article 381-5 of this law, the courts may order the confiscation of utensils, materials and furniture with which the premises were furnished or decorated.

In the cases provided for in Articles 381-1 to 382-3 and 384-3 of this law, the courts may order the closure for a period of six months to three years of hotels, furnished houses, boarding houses, drinking establishments, restaurants, clubs, circles, dance halls, places of entertainment or their annexes, or any places open to the public or used by the public or any other place where these offenses were committed by the operator or with his complicity.

###### Article 387-8 :

Without prejudice to provisions providing for more severe penalties, anyone who violates one of the prohibitions listed in Article 387-7 above or the closure of the establishment provided for in paragraph 3 of the same article is punishable by a term of imprisonment of six months to three years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 387-9 :

When a drug addict is convicted of one of the offenses provided for in Articles 381-1, 384-5, 385-1 to 385-3 of this law, the court may, in replacement or in addition to the penalty, order treatment or care measures appropriate to his condition. A regulatory text sets the terms for the execution of these measures.

###### Section 3 : Special Provision

###### Article 387-10 :

The classification of the different drugs is determined by law.

## BOOK IV : INTERNATIONAL CRIMES

#### TITLE I : WAR CRIMES

###### Article 411-1 :

For the purposes of this title, war crimes means offenses committed against persons or property during an international or non-international armed conflict and in relation to that conflict, in violation of the laws and customs of war or international conventions applicable to armed conflicts.

These include in particular:

˗ grave breaches of the Geneva Conventions of 12 August 1949;

˗ other serious violations of the laws and customs applicable to international armed conflicts within the established framework of international law;

˗ serious violations of Article 3 common to the four Geneva Conventions of 12 August 1949 in the case of an armed conflict not of an international character;

˗ other serious violations of the laws and customs applicable to armed conflicts not of an international character.

##### CHAPTER 1 : WAR CRIMES COMMON TO INTERNATIONAL AND NON-INTERNATIONAL

ARMED CONFLICTS

###### Article 411-2 :

Are punishable by a term of imprisonment of eleven years to thirty years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, willful attacks on life, the act of inflicting great suffering, attacks on physical integrity or health, torture or inhuman treatment including biological experiments, attacks on the dignity of the person including humiliating and degrading treatment when committed against a person protected by the law of armed conflicts.

###### Article 411-3 :

Is punishable by life imprisonment, anyone who intentionally directs attacks against the civilian population as such or against civilians not taking direct part in hostilities and which result in death or serious injury to physical integrity or seriously endanger their health.

###### Article 411-4 :

Is punishable by a term of imprisonment of eleven years to thirty years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, rape, sexual slavery, forced prostitution, forced pregnancy, forced sterilization, or any other form of sexual violence, committed against protected persons.

When the acts committed resulted in death or serious injury to physical integrity or health, the penalty is life imprisonment.

###### Article 411-5 :

Is punishable by life imprisonment, anyone who subjects persons from another party to the conflict who have fallen into his power to mutilations or to medical or scientific experiments of any kind which are neither justified by medical, dental or hospital treatment, nor carried out in the interest of these persons, and which cause the death of or seriously endanger the health of such persons.

###### Article 411-6 :

Is punishable by a term of imprisonment of eleven years to thirty years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who conscripts or enlists minors into armed forces or armed groups or uses them to participate actively in hostilities; this provision does not preclude the voluntary enlistment of minors over fifteen years of age in the national armed forces.

###### Article 411-7 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of eleven years to thirty years and a fine of one million (1 000 000) to ten million (10 000 000) CFA francs, whoever intentionally directs attacks against:

˗ buildings, equipment, units and medical transport, and personnel using, in accordance with international law, the distinctive emblems provided for by the Geneva Conventions;

˗ personnel, installations, equipment, units or vehicles employed in the context of a humanitarian aid or peacekeeping mission in accordance with the United Nations Charter, insofar as they are entitled to the protection that international law of armed conflicts guarantees to civilians and civilian property;

˗ buildings dedicated to religion, education, art, science or charitable action, historical monuments, hospitals and places where sick and wounded are gathered, provided that these buildings are not military objectives.

When these same offenses have resulted in loss of human life or serious harm to physical integrity or health, the penalty is life imprisonment.

###### Article 411-8 :

Is punished by a term of life imprisonment, whoever:

˗ kills or injures by treachery individuals belonging to the hostile nation or army;

˗ declares that no quarter will be given, threatens the adversary with this or conducts hostilities on the basis of this decision.

###### Article 411-9 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of eleven years to thirty years and a fine of one million (1 000 000) to ten million (10 000 000) CFA francs, whoever:

˗ pillages a town or locality, even when taken by assault;

˗ destroys or seizes the property of an adversary, except if these destructions or seizures are imperatively required by the necessities of the conflict.

###### Article 411-10 :

Are punished by a term of imprisonment of eleven years to thirty years and a fine of one million (1 000 000) to ten million (10 000 000) CFA francs, the taking of hostages.

When these have resulted in death or serious harm to physical integrity or health, the penalty is life imprisonment.

##### CHAPITRE 2 : DES CRIMES DE GUERRE PROPRES AUX CONFLITS ARMES

INTERNATIONAUX

###### Article 412-1 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of eleven years to thirty years and a fine of one million (1 000 000) to ten million (10 000 000) CFA francs, whoever directs attacks against civilian property, that is to say property that is not a military objective as well as the destruction or appropriation of property not justified by military necessities and executed on a large scale in an unlawful and arbitrary manner.

###### Article 412-2 :

Is punished by a term of life imprisonment whoever attacks or bombards, by whatever means, towns, villages, dwellings or buildings, which are undefended and which are not military objectives.

###### Article 412-3 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of eleven years to thirty years and a fine of one million (1 000 000) to ten million (10 000 000) CFA francs, whoever, except in cases where the safety of civilians or military imperatives require it, takes part in either the direct or indirect transfer, by an occupying power, of part of its civilian population into the territory it occupies, or in the deportation or transfer within or outside the occupied territory of all or part of the population of that territory.

###### Article 412-4 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of eleven years to thirty years and a fine of one million (1 000 000) to ten million (10 000 000) CFA francs, whoever improperly uses the flag of truce, the flag or military insignia and uniform of the enemy or of the United Nations, as well as the distinctive signs provided for by the Geneva Conventions of 12 August 1949 and their additional protocols and, in so doing, causes loss of human life or serious harm to physical integrity or health.

###### Article 412-5 :

Is punished by a term of life imprisonment whoever:

˗ employs poison or poisoned weapons;

˗ employs asphyxiating, toxic or similar gases as well as all analogous liquids, materials or devices;

˗ uses bullets which expand or flatten easily in the human body;

˗ employs weapons, projectiles, materials and methods of warfare of a nature to cause superfluous injury or unnecessary suffering or to strike indiscriminately in violation of the international law of armed conflicts.

###### Article 412-6 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of eleven years to thirty years and a fine of one million (1 000 000) to ten million (10 000 000) CFA francs whoever uses the presence of a civilian or other protected person to render certain points, zones or military forces immune from military operations.

###### Article 412-7 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of eleven years to thirty years and a fine of one million (1 000 000) to ten million (10 000 000) CFA francs, whoever deliberately starves civilians as a method of warfare, by depriving them of objects indispensable to their survival, including by intentionally impeding relief supplies provided for by the Geneva Conventions.

###### Article 412-8 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of eleven years to thirty years and a fine of one million (1 000 000) to ten million (10 000 000) CFA francs, whoever:

˗ declares abolished, suspended or inadmissible in a court of law the rights and actions of nationals of the adverse party;

˗ intentionally deprives a protected person of his or her right to a fair and impartial trial.

###### Article 412-9 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of eleven years to thirty years and a fine of one million (1 000 000) to ten million (10 000 000) CFA francs, whoever:

˗ compels a prisoner of war or a protected person to serve in the hostile forces;

˗ compels the nationals of the adverse party to take part in the operations of war directed against their own country even if they were in the service of the belligerent power before the commencement of the war.

###### Article 412-10 :

Is punished by a term of life imprisonment, whoever intentionally launches an attack in the knowledge that such attack will cause incidental loss of life or injury to civilians which would be clearly excessive in relation to the concrete and direct overall military advantage anticipated.

###### Article 412-11 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of eleven years to thirty years and a fine of one million (1 000 000) to ten million (10 000 000) CFA francs, whoever intentionally launches an attack in the knowledge that such attack will cause incidentally:

˗ damage to civilian objects which would be clearly excessive in relation to the concrete and direct overall military advantage anticipated;

˗ widespread, long-term and severe damage to the natural environment which would be clearly excessive in relation to the concrete and direct overall military advantage anticipated.

###### Article 412-12 :

Is punished by a term of life imprisonment, whoever kills or wounds a combatant who, having laid down his arms or having no longer means of defence, has surrendered at discretion.

##### CHAPITRE 3 : DES CRIMES DE GUERRE PROPRES AUX CONFLITS ARMES

NON INTERNATIONAUX

###### Article 413-1 :

Is punishable by imprisonment of eleven years to thirty years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who pronounces convictions and executes sentences without a prior judgment, rendered by a regularly constituted tribunal, accompanied by the judicial guarantees generally recognized as essential.

###### Article 413-2 :

Is punishable by imprisonment of eleven years to thirty years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who orders the displacement of the civilian population for reasons relating to the conflict, except in cases where the security of civilians or military imperatives require it.

###### Article 413-3 :

The provisions of this chapter apply to offenses committed in the context of an armed conflict, that is, a conflict which opposes either the armed forces of at least two States (international armed conflict), or in a prolonged manner on the national territory the governmental authorities and organized armed groups or, organized armed groups among themselves (non-international armed conflict). It does not therefore apply to other situations of internal troubles and tensions such as riots, isolated and sporadic acts of violence or acts of a similar nature.

#### TITRE II : DU CRIME DE GENOCIDE ET DU CRIME CONTRE L'HUMANITE

##### CHAPITRE 1 : DU CRIME DE GENOCIDE

###### Article 421-1 :

Constitutes genocide punishable by life imprisonment any of the following acts committed with intent to destroy, in whole or in part, a national, ethnic, racial or religious group or a group determined on the basis of any other arbitrary criterion:

˗ murder;

˗ serious harm to physical or mental integrity;

˗ deliberately inflicting conditions of life calculated to bring about the total or partial physical destruction of the group;

˗ measures intended to prevent births;

˗ forcible transfer of children.

##### CHAPITRE 2 : DU CRIME CONTRE L'HUMANITE

###### Article 422-1 :

Constitutes a crime against humanity and punishable by life imprisonment, the act of anyone committing any of the following acts in execution of a concerted plan as part of a widespread or systematic attack directed against any civilian population:

˗ murder;

˗ extermination;

˗ enslavement;

˗ deportation or forcible transfer of population;

˗ imprisonment or other severe deprivation of physical liberty in violation of fundamental provisions of international law;

˗ torture;

˗ rape, sexual slavery, enforced prostitution, forced pregnancy, enforced sterilization or any other form of sexual violence of comparable gravity;

˗ persecution of any group or any identifiable collectivity on political, racial, national, ethnic, cultural, religious or gender grounds, or on the basis of other criteria universally recognized as impermissible under international law;

˗ enforced disappearances;

˗ the crime of apartheid;

˗ other inhumane acts of a similar character intentionally causing great suffering or serious harm to physical integrity or to physical or mental health.

###### Article 422-2 :

The perpetrators of the offenses defined in this title also incur the following penalties:

˗ confiscation of the proceeds, property and assets derived directly or indirectly from the crime, without prejudice to the rights of third parties in good faith; ˗ territorial ban which may not exceed five years.

## LIVRE V : DES CRIMES ET DELITS CONTRE LES PERSONNES

#### TITRE I : DES ATTEINTES VOLONTAIRES A L'INTEGRITE PHYSIQUE DE LA

PERSONNE

##### CHAPITRE 1 : DE LA TRAITE DES PERSONNES ET DU TRAFIC ILLICITE DE

MIGRANTS

###### Section 1 : De la traite des personnes et des pratiques assimilées

###### Article 511-1 :

For the purposes of this section, trafficking in persons means the recruitment, transportation, harbouring or receipt of persons, by means of the threat or use of force or other forms of coercion, by abduction, fraud, deception, abuse of authority or of a position of vulnerability, or by the giving or receiving of payments or benefits to achieve the consent of a person having control over another person, for the purpose of exploitation.

Exploitation includes, in particular, the exploitation of the prostitution of others or other forms of sexual exploitation, forced labour or services, slavery or practices similar to slavery, servitude or the removal of organs.

###### Article 511-2 :

Constitutes the offense of trafficking in persons: the recruitment, transportation, transfer, harbouring or receipt of a minor for the purpose of exploitation, even if none of the means enumerated in Article 511-1 above is used.

###### Article 511-3 :

The consent of a victim of trafficking in persons as defined in Article 511-1 above does not eliminate the offense.

###### Article 511-4 :

Is guilty of trafficking in persons and punishable by imprisonment of five years to ten years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who commits one of the acts provided for in Articles 511-1 and 511-2 above.

###### Article 511-5 :

The term of imprisonment is eleven years to twenty-one years and the fine two million (2,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, when the offense was committed with one of the following aggravating circumstances: ˗ if the victim is a minor of at most fifteen years;

˗ if the person is particularly vulnerable due to physical or mental deficiency, or pregnancy, advanced age or state of health;

˗ if the act was committed by fraud or violence, by use of false quality, false title or falsified or altered documents or false authorization; ˗ if the perpetrator uses narcotics or any other substance likely to alter the will of the victim;

˗ if the perpetrator is carrying a visible or concealed weapon;

˗ if the perpetrator is an ascendant or a person having authority over the victim; ˗ if the victim was sequestered, deprived of food or exposed in a public or private place of recruitment;

˗ if the victim is exposed to dangerous, arduous work or the worst forms of child labour;

˗ if the perpetrator committed sexual abuse on the victim.

###### Article 511-6 :

The penalty is life imprisonment when:

˗ the victim died;

˗ the victim suffered mutilation or permanent disability;

˗ the trafficking was for the purpose of organ removal.

###### Article 511-7 :

Is punishable by imprisonment of two months to six months, anyone who, having means of subsistence or being able to obtain them through work, engages in begging in any place whatsoever.

###### Article 511-8 :

Is punishable by imprisonment of three months to one year, any beggar, even if disabled or destitute, who solicits alms by:

˗ using threats;

˗ simulating wounds or infirmities;

˗ being accompanied by one or more young children;

˗ entering a dwelling or its outbuildings without authorization from the owner or occupants;

˗ assembling, unless it be husband and wife, the disabled person and his or her guide.

###### Article 511-9 :

Is punishable by imprisonment of one year to three years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs, anyone who, either openly or under the guise of a profession, incites or employs other persons to beg.

###### Article 511-10 :

Is punished by a penalty of imprisonment of six months to two years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who, having authority over a minor, exposes them to delinquency or delivers them to individuals who incite or employ them to beg.

If it concerns the father and mother, the loss of parental authority provided for by the provisions of the code of persons and the family may be pronounced.

###### Article 511-11 :

Is punished by a penalty of imprisonment of three months to three years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, the act by the father and mother of a family of keeping a child under six years old on the public highway or in a space allocated to collective passenger transport, with the aim of soliciting the generosity of passers-by.

###### Article 511-12 :

Is punished by a penalty of imprisonment of six months to two years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who causes a minor to leave the home of their parents, guardian or employer or favors their delinquency.

###### Article 511-13 :

The exploitation of another person's begging means anyone who organizes or exploits the begging of a person, trains or diverts a person to deliver them to begging, exerts pressure on a person to make them beg or continue to do so, is accompanied by one or more young children with a view to deriving directly or indirectly a financial, material or any other advantage.

###### Article 511-14 :

Is guilty of exploitation of another person's begging and punished by a penalty of imprisonment of one year to ten years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs anyone who commits one of the following acts:

˗ organizes another person's begging with a view to deriving profit;

˗ derives profit from another person's begging, shares the benefits or receives subsidies from a person habitually engaging in begging;

˗ hires, trains or diverts a person with a view to delivering them to begging, or to exerting pressure on them to make them beg or continue to do so;

˗ hires, trains or diverts, for purposes of personal enrichment, a person with a view to delivering them to performing a service in exchange for a donation on the public highway.

Is assimilated to the exploitation of another person's begging the fact of not being able to justify resources corresponding to one's lifestyle while exercising a de facto influence, permanent or not, on one or more persons engaging in begging or being in habitual relation with this or these persons.

###### Article 511- 15 :

The maximum of the penalties provided for in article 511-14 above is pronounced when the offense is committed against:

˗ a minor;

˗ a particularly vulnerable person by reason of a physical or psychic deficiency, their state of pregnancy, their advanced age or their state of health;

˗ a person subjected to begging by constraint, violence or fraudulent maneuvers.

The attempt of exploitation of another person's begging is punishable.

###### Article 511-16 :

Is punished by a penalty of imprisonment of one year to three years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, any beggar referred to in articles 511-7 and 511-8 above who is found in possession of weapons, instruments or objects suitable for committing crimes or offenses.

###### Article 511-17 :

It may furthermore be pronounced against the perpetrators of the offenses of begging and exploitation of another person's begging, the prohibition from staying for a duration that cannot exceed five years.

###### Section 2 : Du trafic illicite de migrants

###### Article 511-18 :

The illicit trafficking of migrants means the act for any person or group of persons of organizing transport by land, by sea or by air, accommodation or transit of migrants without satisfying the legal conditions for crossing borders in order to derive directly or indirectly a financial, material or any other advantage and that the national territory serves as a zone of origin, transit or destination.

The illicit trafficking of migrants is punished by a penalty of imprisonment of five years to ten years and a fine of five million (5,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 511-19 :

Is punished by the same penalties, the fraud or falsification, the counterfeiting of visas, documents or travel titles or any other documents attesting to the quality of resident or national of Burkina Faso or a foreign country or granting the benefit of refugee, stateless person, displaced person or victim of human trafficking status.

###### Article 511-20 :

The penalty is an imprisonment of eleven years to twenty-one years and a fine of five million (5,000,000) to twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs, when the offense is committed with a single one of the following circumstances: ˗ the act of endangering or risking endangering the life or safety of the migrants concerned;

˗ the act of subjecting the migrants to inhuman or degrading treatment, including their exploitation.

###### Article 511-21 :

The court seized of an offense referred to in this chapter, furthermore orders, in the judgment or decision of conviction:

˗ the confiscation of the means of commission of the offense as well as the proceeds of the offense;

˗ the destruction of the titles, travel documents and identification documents having facilitated the commission of the offense;

˗ the definitive withdrawal of license, approval or any other authorization or administrative document from any entity whatever its legal form or from any person whose activity has favored the commission of the offense.

###### Section 3 : De la protection des victimes et des témoins

###### Article 511-22 :

In the cases provided for in article 511-21 above, the court seized may furthermore pronounce the prohibition from staying and/or the temporary prohibition from exercising certain civic, civil or family rights.

###### Article 511-23 :

Any person having taken part in an association or an agreement with a view to committing one of the offenses referred to in this chapter is exempted from penalty if, having revealed the existence of this association or agreement to the judicial authority, they thus allow the identification of the other persons involved and/or to avoid the realization of the offense.

###### Article 511-24 :

For the protection of the identity and privacy of the victims and witnesses, the trial courts may order closed session.

The trial court may dispense the victims or witnesses from an appearance at the hearing or take useful measures for the protection of their identity and their privacy.

###### Article 511-25 :

The victims of foreign nationality of the offenses referred to in this chapter may request their retention on the national territory on a temporary or permanent basis.

###### Article 511-26 :

The victims of the offenses referred to in this chapter, when they present particular vulnerability or are minors, are assisted before the investigating and trial courts by a lawyer of their choice or appointed ex officio.

###### Article 511-27 :

For the exercise of the civil action, the public prosecutor may require the placement under guardianship or legal administration of minor victims whose legal representative is not known or does not present guarantees of safeguarding the rights and welfare of the child.

###### Article 511-28 :

There is established, by decree taken in Council of Ministers, a national body of vigilance and monitoring in matters of combating trafficking in persons and related practices.

##### CHAPTER 2: SERIOUS VIOLATIONS OF LIFE OR THE INTEGRITY OF THE

PERSON

###### Section 1: Torture and related practices

###### Paragraph 1: Punishment of torture and related practices

###### Article 512-1:

For the purposes of this section, the following definitions apply:

˗ State agent: any of the following persons, whether exercising their powers in Burkina Faso or abroad:

 a public servant or any other person charged with a public service mission;

 a member of the security or defense forces;

 any person vested with a public or elective mandate.

˗ practices related to torture: acts or omissions constituting cruel, inhuman or degrading punishment or treatment which are not acts of torture within the meaning of bullet point 3 of this article but which are committed by a State agent or any other person acting in an official capacity or at their instigation or with their express or tacit consent, including arbitrary arrest and detention.

˗ torture: any act or omission by which severe pain or suffering, whether physical or mental, is intentionally inflicted on a person for such purposes as obtaining from them or a third person information or confessions, punishing them for an act they or a third person has committed or is suspected of having committed, intimidating or coercing them or intimidating or coercing a third person, or for any reason based on discrimination of any kind, when such pain or suffering is inflicted by a State agent or any other person acting in an official capacity or at their instigation or with their express or tacit consent.

###### Article 512-2:

Anyone guilty of acts of torture or related practices shall be punished by imprisonment of three to five years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 512-3:

Anyone who commits torture or related practices in the following circumstances shall be punished by imprisonment of five to ten years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs: ˗ if the victim is under eighteen years of age;

˗ if the victim had a disability at the time of the acts;

˗ if the victim is a pregnant woman;

˗ if the victim is an elderly person;

˗ if temporary disability resulted therefrom.

###### Article 512-4:

The perpetrator of torture or related practices with at least one of the following circumstances shall be punished by life imprisonment:

˗ if it resulted in the death of the victim;

˗ if the acts were committed during a period of armed conflict or internal unrest;

˗ if the acts resulted in permanent disability.

###### Paragraph 2: Miscellaneous provisions

###### Article 512-5:

The Burkinabe courts have jurisdiction to try and punish any person who commits an act of torture if:

- the act is committed on the territory of Burkina Faso;
- the act is committed on board a ship registered under Burkinabe law or in respect of which a permit or identification number has been issued in accordance with such law;
- the act is committed on board an aircraft, either registered in Burkina Faso or leased without crew and operated by a person meeting the conditions to be the owner of an aircraft in Burkina Faso;
- the alleged perpetrator has Burkinabe nationality;
- the complainant or victim has Burkinabe nationality;
- the alleged perpetrator of the acts is in Burkina Faso after commission of the acts.

###### Article 512-6:

The competent authorities shall take measures to ensure the protection of the complainant and witnesses against any ill-treatment or intimidation.

###### Article 512-7:

The victim has the right to fair and adequate reparation and compensation, including the means necessary for their fullest possible rehabilitation. In the event of death of the victim resulting from an act of torture or related practices, their beneficiaries are entitled to compensation. Notwithstanding any criminal proceedings, the State has an obligation to provide reparation to victims.

###### Article 512-8:

Anyone who, by action, inaction, refusal to act or any other means, obstructs or attempts to obstruct the performance of the missions assigned to the National Observatory for the Prevention of Torture and Other Related Practices, hereinafter referred to as the Observatory, established by Article 20 of Law No. 022-2014/AN on the prevention and punishment of torture and related practices, shall be punished by imprisonment of six months to two years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 512-9:

Anyone who exerts or attempts to exert pressure, intimidation, threats, reprisals, or violence on persons who have provided or are suspected of having provided true or false information or of having cooperated with the Observatory shall be punished by imprisonment of six months to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 512-10:

Anyone who, having been informed of acts of torture or related practices in a place under their responsibility or within their jurisdiction, fails to inform the Observatory, shall be punished by imprisonment of three months to one year and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs.

###### Section 2: Intentional violations of life, intentional violations of

physical integrity and harm to health

###### Paragraph 1: Intentional violations of life

###### Article 512-11:

Homicide committed intentionally is classified as murder.

Any murder committed with premeditation or ambush is classified as assassination.

###### Article 512-12:

The murder of one's biological or adoptive father or mother or any other legitimate or biological ascendant is classified as parricide.

###### Article 512-13:

Infanticide is the murder or assassination of a newborn child. A child is considered newborn until the expiration of the prescribed period for birth registration.

###### Article 512-14:

The act of attempting against a person's life by means of substances that can cause death is classified as poisoning, regardless of the manner in which such substances were used or administered and whatever the consequences.

###### Article 512-15:

The perpetrator of assassination, parricide, poisoning or infanticide shall be punished by life imprisonment.

Anyone guilty of murder committed for purposes of cannibalism, worship, occult practices or commerce shall also be punished by life imprisonment.

However, a mother who is the principal perpetrator or accomplice in the assassination or murder of her newborn child shall be punished in the first case by life imprisonment and in the second case by imprisonment of eleven to twenty-one years, but this provision shall not apply to her co-perpetrators or accomplices.

###### Article 512-16:

Murder is punished by imprisonment of eleven to thirty years.

###### Article 512-17:

Is punished by life imprisonment any perpetrator of murder committed with one of the following aggravating circumstances:

˗ the victim is a minor under fifteen years of age;

˗ the murder is preceded or accompanied by rape, torture or acts of barbarism;

˗ the murder is committed against a magistrate, a national police officer, a military gendarme, a member of the prison administration staff or any other person holding public authority, during the exercise of or by reason of their duties; ˗ the murder is preceded, accompanied or followed by another crime;

˗ the purpose of the murder is either to prepare or facilitate an offense, or to favor the escape or ensure the impunity of the perpetrator or accomplice of an offense.

###### Paragraph 2: Intentional assaults on physical integrity

Sub-paragraph 1: Intentional violence

###### Article 512-18:

Is punished by imprisonment from two months to three years and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who, intentionally, causes injuries or strikes blows, or commits any other violence or assault, if these acts of violence result in an illness or total incapacity for personal work of more than seven days and less than twenty-one days.

If there was premeditation or ambush, the imprisonment is the maximum of the penalty prescribed in the preceding paragraph.

###### Article 512-19:

Is punished by imprisonment from two months to one year and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, anyone who, intentionally, causes injuries or strikes blows, or commits any other violence or assault, if these acts of violence result in an illness or total incapacity for personal work of seven days or less and if there was premeditation or ambush or when the acts were committed publicly.

###### Article 512-20:

Is punished by imprisonment from two years to seven years and a fine from one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, any perpetrator of injuries or blows or other violence or assault that causes an illness or total incapacity for personal work of twenty-one days or more.

###### Article 512-21:

Is punished by imprisonment from two years to ten years and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, any perpetrator of intentional assault and battery and assault that results in mutilations, amputations or deprivation of the use of a limb, blindness, loss of an eye or other permanent disabilities.

If the blows struck or injuries intentionally inflicted result in death without intention to cause it, the perpetrator is punished by imprisonment from seven years to ten years and a fine from three hundred thousand (300,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 512-22:

The penalty is life imprisonment when assault and battery, violence and assault are carried out with premeditation or ambush and if they result in mutilations, amputation or deprivation of the use of a limb, blindness or loss of an eye or other permanent disabilities.

###### Article 512-23:

Anyone who intentionally causes injuries or strikes blows to an ascendant, to their adoptive father or mother is punished:

˗ by imprisonment from two months to two years and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs if the injuries or blows did not cause any illness or incapacity or if they resulted in total incapacity for personal work of seven days or less;

˗ by imprisonment from two years to five years and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs if the assault and battery caused total incapacity for personal work of more than seven days and less than twenty-one days; ˗ by imprisonment from five years to ten years, if the assault and battery caused total incapacity for work of at least twenty-one days;

˗ by imprisonment from ten years to twenty years, if the above-mentioned violence is followed by mutilations, amputations or deprivation of the use of a limb;

˗ by life imprisonment if the blows struck or injuries intentionally inflicted without intention to cause death nevertheless resulted in death.

In all cases of premeditation or ambush, the maximum penalty is pronounced.

###### Article 512-24:

Is punished by imprisonment from one year to ten years and a fine from one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who participates in a brawl, rebellion or seditious gathering during which violence is committed that results in death without intention to cause it, unless they incur a more serious penalty as the perpetrator of said violence.

###### Article 512-25:

Is punished by imprisonment from three months to five years and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who participates in a brawl, rebellion or seditious gathering during which blows are struck or injuries are inflicted, unless they incur a more serious penalty as the perpetrator of said violence.

###### Article 512-26:

In the cases provided for in Articles 512-24 and 512-25 above, the leaders, perpetrators, instigators and provocateurs of the brawl, rebellion or seditious gathering are punished as if they had personally committed said violence. Sub-paragraph 2: Other assaults on physical integrity

###### Article 512-27:

Is punished by imprisonment from eleven years to thirty years, anyone who is guilty of the crime of castration.

If death results from it, the perpetrator is punished by life imprisonment.

###### Article 512-28:

The act of provoking or aiding the suicide of another is punished by imprisonment from one year to five years and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, when the provocation was followed by suicide or a suicide attempt.

The imprisonment is from three years to five years and the fine from one million (1,000,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs when the victim of the offense defined in the preceding paragraph is a minor of fifteen years or less. Natural or legal persons guilty of the offense provided for in this article also incur the additional penalty of prohibition from engaging in the activity of professional training provider within the meaning of the labor code for a period not exceeding five years.

The propaganda or advertising, regardless of the method, in favor of products, objects or methods recommended as means of causing one's own death is punished by imprisonment from one year to three years and a fine from one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs. Sub-paragraph 3: Harm to health and related offenses

###### Article 512-29:

Any person having a presence of HIV or anti-HIV antibodies in their body at the time of screening test is HIV-positive.

###### Article 512-30:

Is punished by a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who, having knowledge of their serological status, refrains from informing their spouse or sexual partner.

###### Article 512-31 :

Any person who, knowing their HIV-positive status, refrains from informing their spouse or sexual partner and contaminates them shall be punished by imprisonment of one to ten years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

If death results from this contamination, the perpetrator incurs imprisonment of eleven to thirty years.

If the contamination results from rape, the perpetrator incurs imprisonment of eleven to thirty years.

###### Article 512-32 :

Any person who knowingly transmits or attempts to transmit HIV to any other person by any means whatsoever shall be punished by imprisonment of three to ten years and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs. If contamination of at least two persons results, the penalty is life imprisonment.

###### Article 512-33 :

Any person who causes another person illness or incapacity for personal work by administering to them, in any manner whatsoever, knowingly but without intent to cause death, substances harmful to health shall be punished by imprisonment of two to three years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs. When this results in illness or total incapacity for personal work greater than or equal to twenty-one days, the penalty is imprisonment of two to five years and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

When the substances administered cause either an apparently incurable illness, or loss of use of an organ, or permanent disability, the penalty of imprisonment is five to ten years and the fine is five million (5,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs. When they cause death without intent to cause it, the penalty is imprisonment of eleven to twenty-one years and a fine of ten million (10,000,000) to twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs.

In all cases provided for in paragraphs 2, 3 and 4 above, the court seized may also pronounce prohibition from exercising family rights, prohibition from residence and/or professional prohibition for a period that may not exceed five years.

###### Article 512-34 :

When the acts specified in Article 512-33 above are committed by an ancestor, descendant, spouse or successor of the victim or a person having authority over them or having custody of them, the penalties of imprisonment are increased to:

˗ two to five years in the case provided for in paragraph 1;

˗ five to ten years in the case provided for in paragraph 2;

˗ eleven to twenty-one years in the case provided for in paragraph 3;

˗ eleven to thirty years in the case provided for in paragraph 4.

The court seized may also pronounce prohibition from exercising family rights for a period between five and ten years.

###### Paragraphe 3 : Various provisions

###### Article 512-35 :

The attempt of the offenses provided for in Articles 512-18 to 512-28 above is punishable.

###### Article 512-36 :

Murder, injuries and blows are excusable if they are provoked by serious blows or violence against persons.

###### Article 512-37 :

Murder, injuries and blows are excusable if they are committed while repelling during the day the scaling or breaking of fences, walls or entrance of an inhabited house or apartment or their dependencies.

###### Article 512-38 :

Murder committed or blows struck or injuries inflicted by one spouse on the other as well as on their accomplice at the moment when they are caught in flagrante delicto of adultery at the conjugal home are excusable.

###### Article 512-39 :

The crime of castration is excusable if it was immediately provoked by an indecent assault committed with violence or by rape.

###### Article 512-40 :

Injuries and blows are excusable when they are committed on the person of an adult caught in flagrante delicto of indecent assault carried out with or without violence on a child under thirteen years of age.

###### Article 512-41 :

The excuses provided for in Articles 512-36 to 512-40 above are mitigating excuses.

###### Article 512-42 :

Parricide is not excusable.

###### Article 512-43 :

In all cases provided for in this section, the confiscation of weapons, objects and instruments having been used to commit the offense is pronounced.

##### CHAPITRE 3 : OFFENSES AGAINST WOMEN AND GIRLS

###### Article 513-1 :

For the purposes of this chapter, the following terms mean:

˗ violence against women and girls: any act of violence directed against persons of the female sex, and causing or likely to cause women and girls physical, sexual, psychological, moral, economic and cultural harm or suffering, including the threat of such acts, whether in public or private life; ˗ cultural violence: any harmful and degrading practice against women and girls drawing its justification from customs, traditions and religions;

˗ economic violence: the act of using one's means to slow down or prevent the economic or financial development of any person or the act of preventing any person from enjoying their socio-economic rights;

˗ moral and psychological violence: any behavior, words and attitude that undermine the personality of the woman or girl, her image, self-esteem and inner balance;

˗ patrimonial violence: any act or negligence affecting the survival of the victim and consisting of transforming, removing, destroying, withholding or misappropriating objects, documents, goods and values, patrimonial rights or economic resources intended to cover her needs and which may extend to damage caused to common or personal property of the victim;

˗ physical violence: any act or behavior that undermines the physical integrity of the woman or girl;

˗ sexual violence: any sexual assault committed with violence, coercion, threat, on a woman or girl.

###### Section 1 : Abduction, abuse, sexual slavery, moral violence

against women and girls

###### Article 513-2 :

Constitutes abduction the act by a person of forcibly abducting a woman or girl with a view to imposing marriage or union on her without her consent. Any perpetrator of abduction shall be punished by imprisonment of six months to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.

When the perpetrator of the abduction engaged in sexual abuse or rape of the victim, the penalty is imprisonment of five to ten years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 513-3 :

Sexual abuse or torture constitutes the act of introducing a substance into the genital organs of a woman or girl, or applying an object or substance to the breasts of a woman or girl with a view to inflicting burns, lesions or suffering on her.

Any person who commits sexual abuse or torture shall be punished by imprisonment of two to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 513-4 :

Constitutes an offense of sexual slavery the act of compelling by coercion a girl or woman to submit or engage in various sexual practices. Any person who commits the offense of sexual slavery shall be punished by imprisonment of two to five years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 513-5 :

Constitute moral and psychological violence against a girl or a woman:

˗ any remark or attitude, whether or not accompanied by physical aggression, the objective of which is to undermine the self-esteem of the woman or girl, to denigrate her and reduce the victim to a state of helplessness or submission;

˗ gestures, words, writings, by which an indecent or malicious intention or a manifest will to cause material damage, to injure or to kill the woman or girl is signified;

˗ infringement of the sexual health and reproductive health rights of the woman or girl, limitation of the enjoyment of these rights, by means of coercion, blackmail, corruption or manipulation, in particular the prohibition of using contraceptive methods;

˗ repudiation or ill-treatment inflicted on a woman who gives birth to a child of a sex not desired by her spouse;

˗ ill-treatment inflicted on sterile women;

˗ prohibition without grounds based on the interest or stability of the household, from visiting her parents or receiving their visits;

˗ prohibition without founded reason from exercising a profession, from practicing an income-generating activity, an associative and political activity; ˗ unequal treatment of wives in the context of a polygamous marriage.

Is punished by a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, whoever commits one of the acts referred to in this article.

###### Article 513-6 :

Is punished by imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of six hundred thousand (600,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs whoever drives away, dismisses, rejects or inflicts ill-treatment on a girl or a woman accused or suspected of witchcraft.

###### Section 2 : Female genital mutilation

###### Article 513-7 :

Is punished by imprisonment of one year to ten years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, whoever harms or attempts to harm the integrity of the female genital organ by ablation, by excision, by infibulation, by desensitization or by any other means.

If death has resulted therefrom, the penalty is imprisonment of eleven years to twenty-one years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 513-8 :

The penalties are raised to the maximum if the perpetrator is from the medical or paramedical profession.

The court seised may furthermore pronounce against him the prohibition from exercising his profession for a period which cannot exceed five years.

###### Article 513-9 :

Is punished by imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, whoever through his public speech, remarks or writing, encourages female genital mutilation.

###### Section 3 : Abortion

###### Article 513-10 :

Is punished by imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, whoever, by food, beverages, medicines, maneuvers, violence or by any other means, procures or attempts to procure the abortion of a pregnant or supposedly pregnant woman, whether or not she has consented thereto.

The penalty of imprisonment is five years to ten years and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs if the perpetrator habitually engaged in such acts.

If death has resulted therefrom, the penalty is imprisonment of eleven years to twenty-one years and a fine of five million (5,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs.

The penalty is life imprisonment if the perpetrator habitually engaged in such acts.

The court seised may furthermore pronounce professional prohibition and/or prohibition of residence for a period which cannot exceed five years.

###### Article 513-11 :

Is punished by imprisonment of six months to two years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, whoever contravenes the prohibition from exercising his profession pronounced pursuant to the last paragraph of the preceding article.

###### Article 513-12 :

Is punished by imprisonment of six months to two years and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs, voluntary termination of pregnancy or the attempt, subject to the cases provided for in the following articles.

###### Article 513-13 :

Voluntary termination of pregnancy may at any gestational age be performed if a doctor attests after examinations that the continuation of the pregnancy endangers the health of the woman or that there exists a strong probability that the unborn child is affected by a disease or infirmity of particular severity recognized as incurable at the time of diagnosis.

###### Article 513-14 :

In case of rape or incest, if the materiality of the distress is established by the public prosecutor, the pregnant woman may request from a doctor within the first fourteen weeks, the termination of her pregnancy.

###### Article 513-15 :

Is punished by imprisonment of two months to two years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, whoever, by any means of dissemination or advertising incites abortion.

###### Article 513-16 :

In case of conviction for one of the offenses provided for in this section, the court pronounces the confiscation of objects used for the abortion. It may furthermore pronounce the closure of establishment and/or the prohibition of exercise of civic and family rights for a period which cannot exceed five years.

Any conviction for attempt or complicity in the same offenses entails the same prohibitions.

###### Article 513-17 :

Is punished by imprisonment of six months to two years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, whoever contravenes the prohibition by which he is affected in application of the preceding article.

###### Article 513-18 :

Is punished by imprisonment of three months to three years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, whoever, in the course of the exercise of his functions, has knowledge of one of the offenses referred to in this chapter, does not inform the competent judicial or administrative authorities thereof.

###### Article 513-19 :

Is punished by imprisonment of two years to five years and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, whoever, by threats of reprisals, obstructs a denunciation on the part of the persons referred to in article 513-18 above.

##### CHAPITRE 4 : ACCUSATION OF WITCHCRAFT

###### Article 514-1 :

The accusation of practice of witchcraft is any imputation, to one or more persons, of facts of a magical, abstract, imaginary, supernatural or paranormal nature which cannot be materially or scientifically proven and which is of a nature to undermine the honor, reputation, security or life of the latter.

The material element of the offense is characterized by any fact, any act qualified as charlatanism, occultism, by rites or remarks, speeches and cries tending to accuse others of one or more facts of a supernatural or paranormal nature, which cannot be materially or scientifically proven.
