The intentional element is deduced from the knowledge of the damaging consequences of the act of accusation on the victim such as dishonor, social exclusion, violence and assault.

###### Article 514-2 :

Are accomplices to the offense of accusation of witchcraft practice: ˗ those who have provided any means or instrument used to allegedly detect a person as practicing witchcraft;

˗ those who have, knowingly, aided or assisted the perpetrator or perpetrators of the act in the facts, who prepared, facilitated or consummated it; ˗ those who, having had knowledge of occult practices aimed at allegedly detecting persons practicing witchcraft, will not have informed the administrative or judicial authorities;

˗ those who have harbored persons presumed to be authors or co-authors of the offense;

˗ those who have provided support for writings and statements accusing one or more persons of witchcraft practice.

###### Article 514-3 :

Is punished by imprisonment from one year to five years and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, any person found guilty or accomplice to accusation of witchcraft practice.

The imprisonment penalty is from three to five years in cases where the accusation of witchcraft has given rise to:

˗ the social exclusion of the victim;

˗ blows, injuries and assaults on the victim;

˗ damage to movable and immovable property.

In case of death of the victim, the offense is punished by imprisonment from five years to ten years and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to two million five hundred thousand (2,500,000) CFA francs.

#### TITRE II: OTHER OFFENSES AGAINST THE PERSON

##### CHAPITRE 1: THREATS, RISKS CAUSED TO OTHERS AND FAILURE TO PROVIDE ASSISTANCE

###### Article 521-1 :

Is punished by imprisonment from two years to five years and a fine from two million (2,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who, by any means whatsoever, makes a conditional threat of harm to persons constituting an offense that the law punishes with a criminal penalty.

###### Article 521-2 :

Is punished by imprisonment from three months to three years and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who, by any means whatsoever, makes a conditional threat of harm to persons that the law punishes with a misdemeanor penalty. When the threat is made to a magistrate, an officer or agent of the judicial police, a public or ministerial officer, an auxiliary of justice, in the exercise or on the occasion of the exercise of his functions, the penalty is imprisonment from two to five years and a fine of two million

(2,000,000) francs to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

The same applies when the threat is made to a witness, a victim or any other person, either with a view to inducing them not to report the facts, not to file a complaint, not to make a statement, or by reason of the report, the complaint or the statement.

###### Article 521-3 :

Are punished by imprisonment from six months to five years and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, conditional threats of harm to property that the law punishes with a criminal penalty.

###### Article 521-4 :

Is punished by imprisonment from two to ten years and a fine from two million (2,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who makes a death threat by anonymous or signed writing, image, symbol, emblem or any form of expression of death.

###### Article 521-5 :

For the purposes of this code, endangering the person of another is the act of deliberately exposing another to death or injury.

Is punished by imprisonment from one year to five years and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, the act of endangering the person of another.

The act of directly exposing another to an immediate risk of death or injury likely to result in mutilation or permanent disability through the deliberate violation of an obligation of prudence or safety imposed by law or regulation also constitutes endangering the person of another and is punished by the penalties provided in the preceding paragraph.

###### Article 521-6 :

Legal persons declared criminally liable for the offenses defined in Article 521-5 above incur, in addition to the fine, the complementary sanctions applicable to them, including the prohibition from exercising the activity that is at the origin of the commission of the offense.

###### Article 521-7 :

Is punished by imprisonment from three months to three years and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who, without risk to himself or to third parties, can prevent by his personal action either an act classified as a crime or an offense against the bodily integrity of a person, voluntarily abstains from doing so.

Is punished by the same penalties anyone who voluntarily abstains from providing to a person in danger the assistance that, without risk to himself or to third parties, he could have given either by his personal action or by calling for help.

###### Article 521-8 :

The act of voluntarily obstructing the arrival of assistance intended to enable a person to escape imminent danger or to combat a disaster presenting a danger to the safety of persons is punished by imprisonment from one year to three years and a fine from three hundred thousand (300,000) to two million five hundred thousand (2,500,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 521-9 :

Is punished by imprisonment from two months to six months and a fine from six hundred thousand (600,000) to two million five hundred thousand (2,500,000) CFA francs, anyone who directly exposes another to a risk of death or injury through the deliberate violation of an obligation expressly placed upon him by laws or regulations.

##### CHAPTER 2: INVOLUNTARY HOMICIDES AND INJURIES

###### Article 522-1:

The act of causing the death of another through clumsiness, recklessness, inattention, negligence or failure to observe a law or regulation constitutes involuntary homicide.

Anyone who commits involuntary homicide is punished by imprisonment from six months to three years and a fine from two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

The penalty is imprisonment from one year to seven years and a fine from five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs when the offender acted while intoxicated, committed a hit-and-run offense or attempted by any other means to escape the liability that may be incurred. With regard to vehicle drivers, the penalties are, likewise, from one year to seven years imprisonment and a fine from five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs when:

˗ the driver was in a state of intoxication or was under the influence of an alcoholic state characterized by a concentration of alcohol in the blood or in exhaled air equal to or exceeding the rates fixed by the legislative or regulatory provisions of the traffic code or other laws and regulations;

˗ the involuntary homicide occurred because the driver attempted to escape or refused to submit to a road safety check;

˗ it results from a blood test or verification that the driver had used substances or plants classified as narcotics; ˗ the driver did not hold the driving license required by law or regulation or his license had been cancelled, invalidated, suspended or withdrawn; ˗ the driver exceeded the maximum authorized speed; ˗ the driver, knowing that he had just caused or occasioned an accident, did not stop and thus attempted to escape the criminal or civil liability that may be incurred.

The penalties are imprisonment from one year to ten years and a fine from six hundred thousand (600,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, when the involuntary homicide was committed with several of the circumstances mentioned in this article, concerning drivers.

###### Article 522-2:

Anyone who causes to another through clumsiness, recklessness, inattention, negligence or failure to observe a law or regulation, injuries, blows, illnesses resulting in total incapacity to work for more than three months is punished by imprisonment from three months to two years and a fine from two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs. The penalties are from six months to three years imprisonment and a fine from three hundred thousand (300,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs for any vehicle driver when:

˗ he was in a state of intoxication or was under the influence of an alcoholic state characterized by a concentration of alcohol in the blood or in exhaled air equal to or exceeding the rates fixed by the legislative or regulatory provisions of the traffic code or other laws and regulations;

˗ the offense occurred because he attempted to escape or refused to submit to a road safety check;

˗ It results from a blood test or verification that he had used substances or plants classified as narcotics.

###### Article 522-3:

Anyone who causes to another through clumsiness, recklessness, inattention, negligence or failure to observe a law or regulation, injuries, blows, illnesses resulting in total incapacity to work for more than three months when the offense was committed with one of the following circumstances is punished by imprisonment from six months to three years and a fine from three hundred thousand (300,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs:

˗ in a state of intoxication;

˗ in case of hit-and-run offense;

˗ by fraudulently attempting to escape the liability that could be incurred.

##### CHAPTER 3: INFRINGEMENTS ON INDIVIDUAL LIBERTY AND

THE INVIOLABILITY OF THE HOME

###### Article 523-1:

Anyone who, without order from the constituted authorities and outside the cases where the law permits or orders it, abducts, arrests, detains, sequesters a person or knowingly provides a place to detain or sequester a person is punished by imprisonment from five years to ten years and a fine from five hundred thousand (500,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs. If the detention or sequestration lasts more than one month, the penalty is imprisonment from eleven years to twenty-one years and a fine from one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 523-2:

The maximum of the penalty provided in paragraph 2 of Article 523-1 above is pronounced, if the arrest or abduction is executed either while wearing a uniform or a regulatory insignia or appearing as such, or under a false name, a false capacity or upon a false order of public authority.

The same penalty is applicable, if the arrest or abduction was carried out using a motorized means of transport or if the victim was threatened with death.

###### Article 523-3:

The perpetrator is punished by imprisonment from eleven years to thirty years and a fine from one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, if the abducted, arrested, detained or sequestered person has been subjected to torture or corporal abuse.

If the torture resulted in death, the mutilation of an organ or any permanent infirmity, the perpetrator is punished by life imprisonment and a fine from five million (5,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs. The penalties provided in the preceding paragraph are applicable if the abducted, arrested, detained or sequestered person disappears.

###### Article 523-4:

Anyone who is guilty of enforced disappearance of a person is punished by imprisonment from twenty years to thirty years and a fine from five hundred thousand (500,000) to twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs.

Enforced disappearance of a person means the arrest, detention, abduction or any other form of deprivation of liberty of a person or group of persons, under conditions removing them from the protection of the law, by one or more agents of the State or by a person or group of persons acting with the authorization, support or acquiescence of the State authorities, who then refuse to acknowledge that these persons are deprived of liberty or to disclose the fate reserved for them or the place where they are located.

###### Article 523-5:

Anyone who, involved in the commission of an enforced disappearance, has effectively contributed to the recovery alive of the disappeared person or enabled the elucidation of cases of enforced disappearance or the identification of the perpetrators of an enforced disappearance benefits from a mitigating excuse.

###### Article 523-6:

Anyone who, by fraud or by means of threats or violence against persons or property, enters or attempts to enter the dwelling of another is punished by imprisonment from three months to one year and a fine from two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs.

The imprisonment penalty is from two years to ten years and the fine from one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, if the violation of dwelling is committed either at night, or by means of scaling or breaking in, or by several persons, or with the carrying of visible or concealed weapons by one or more of the perpetrators.

##### CHAPTER 4: INFRINGEMENTS ON HONOR, THE

REPUTATION OF PERSONS AND PRIVACY

###### Article 524-1 :

Any allegation or imputation of a fact that damages the honor or reputation of persons or the body to which the fact is imputed, is defamation.

The following cannot give rise to prosecution for defamation:

˗ parliamentary debates;

˗ judicial debates;

˗ pronouncements or writings produced before the courts;

˗ the faithful and good faith report of such debates and speeches with the exception of defamation trials;

˗ the publication of judicial decisions including those rendered in defamation matters;

˗ the official report made in good faith by a person duly appointed to conduct an investigation and within the framework of that investigation; ˗ the imputation made in good faith by a superior or his subordinate; ˗ information given in good faith about a person or a third party who has a personal or official interest in knowing it or who has the power to remedy an alleged injustice;

˗ criticism of a work, a show, an opinion expressed publicly provided that such criticism does not constitute a personal attack.

###### Article 524-2 :

Any offensive expression, contemptuous term or invective that does not contain the imputation of any fact, is an insult.

###### Article 524-3 :

Intentionally false denunciation of a false fact likely to expose the person who is the subject of it to a sanction by the administrative authority or his employer or to judicial prosecution is calumnious.

###### Article 524-4 :

Defamation committed by speech, shouts or threats uttered in public places or meetings, by writings, printed matter sold or distributed, offered for sale or displayed in public places or meetings by placards or posters displayed to public view, by any other means of dissemination is punished:

˗ by imprisonment of two months to one year and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, if the defamation is committed against:

 public administrations, constituted bodies, the armed forces, courts and tribunals;

 one or more members of the government or constituted bodies, any person responsible for a temporary or permanent public service or mandate by reason of their functions or capacity and witnesses by reason of their testimony;

˗ by imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, if the defamation is committed by means of electronic communication;

˗ by imprisonment of two months to six months and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs, if the defamation is committed against private individuals.

This article applies to defamation directed against the memory of a deceased person when the author of the defamation intended to damage the honor or reputation of the living heirs, spouses or universal legatees.

###### Article 524-5 :

Prosecution is initiated upon complaint by the victim or his legal representative. Until final conviction, withdrawal of the complaint stops the exercise of the public action.

When the truth of the defamatory fact is established, and that fact was committed by a public authority in the exercise or on the occasion of the exercise of its functions, the perpetrator of the offense is discharged.

###### Article 524-6 :

Public insult committed against private individuals, by speech, shouts or threats uttered in public places or meetings, by writings, printed matter sold or distributed, offered for sale or displayed in public places or meetings by placards or posters displayed to public view, by any other means of dissemination, is punished by imprisonment of two months to six months and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.

This article applies to insult made to the memory of a deceased person, when the author of the insult intended to damage the honor or reputation of the living heirs, spouses or universal legatees.

###### Article 524-7 :

Anyone who, by any means whatsoever, makes a calumnious denunciation against one or more individuals, to the judicial authorities, to administrative or judicial police officers, or to authorities having the power to follow up on it or to refer the matter to the competent authority, or to the hierarchical superiors or employers of the person denounced, is punished by imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

The court seised may, in addition, order the insertion of its decision in full or by extracts in one or more newspapers at the expense of the convicted person.

If the denounced fact is liable to criminal or disciplinary sanction, prosecution for calumnious denunciation may be initiated by the victim either after judgment or decision of acquittal or discharge, or after order or decision of no case to answer, or after dismissal of the denunciation by the magistrate, official, superior authority or employer competent to give it the follow-up it was liable to receive.

The court seised is required to stay the proceedings if prosecution concerning the denounced fact is pending.

###### Article 524-8 :

For the offenses referred to in Articles 524-1 to 524-7 above, the limitation period for public prosecution is three months from the commission of the acts or the last act of prosecution or investigation.

In the case referred to in paragraph 3 of Article 524-7 above, the period runs from the date of the order or decision of no case to answer, the notice of dismissal without further action, the judgment or decision of acquittal or discharge.

###### Article 524-9 :

Anyone who, by any means whatsoever, has willfully infringed the privacy of another's private life, is punished by imprisonment of two months to one year and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs:

˗ by capturing, listening to, recording or transmitting words spoken in a private place by a person without that person's consent;

˗ by fixing, recording or transmitting without that person's consent, the image of a person in a private place.

When the acts stated above have been accomplished in the full knowledge and sight of the interested parties without their having objected to it, when they were in a position to do so, their consent is presumed.

The same penalties apply to the act of keeping, bringing or allowing to be brought to the knowledge of the public or a third party or using in any manner whatsoever any recording or document obtained by means of one of the above acts.

When the offense provided for in the preceding paragraph is committed through the press, the special provisions of the laws and regulations governing these matters are applicable with regard to the determination of the persons responsible. In all cases, the court seised may, without prejudice to the reparation of the damage suffered, prescribe all measures, such as sequestration, seizure and others, appropriate to prevent or stop an infringement of the privacy of private life; these measures may, if there is urgency, be ordered in summary proceedings.

###### Article 524-10 :

Is punished by a sentence of imprisonment from one year to five years and a fine from three hundred thousand (300,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who engages in:

˗ the manufacture, importation, possession, display, offer, rental or sale of apparatus or technical devices designed to commit the offense provided for in paragraph 1 of Article 524-9 above;

˗ the manufacture, importation, possession, display, offer, rental or sale of apparatus or technical devices designed for remote detection of conversations, enabling the commission of the offense provided for in paragraph 1 of Article 524-9 above or intended for the illegal capture of data;

˗ the manufacture, importation, possession, display, offer, rental or sale of apparatus or technical devices designed to intercept, divert, use or disclose, intentionally, correspondence sent, transmitted or received by electronic means or to proceed with the installation of apparatus designed to carry out such interceptions.

###### Article 524-11 :

Is punished by a sentence of imprisonment from two months to one year and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who has knowingly published, by any means whatsoever, a montage made with the words or image of a person, without that person's consent, if it is not evident that it is a montage or if it is not expressly mentioned.

###### Article 524-12 :

The attempt of the offenses referred to in Articles 524-9 to 524-11 above is punishable.

Public action may only be initiated upon complaint by the victim, their legal representative or their successors in interest.

The court may order the confiscation of equipment used to commit the offense, any montage medium, any recording or document obtained using one of the methods provided for in Article 524-9 above.

###### Article 524-13 :

The provisions of this chapter do not apply to natural or legal persons falling under the status of the print press, online press and audiovisual press.

##### CHAPITRE 5 : DE LA VIOLATION DE SECRET

###### Article 525-1 :

Is punished by a sentence of imprisonment from six months to two years and a fine from five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, the disclosure of information of a secret nature by a person who is the custodian thereof either by status or by profession, or by reason of a function or temporary mission.

###### Article 525-2 :

The provisions of Article 525-1 above are not applicable: ˗ in cases where the law requires or authorizes the disclosure of the secret; ˗ to anyone who informs the judicial, medical or administrative authorities of deprivations or abuse, including when it involves sexual assaults or mutilations, of which they became aware and which were inflicted on a minor or on a person who is unable to protect themselves due to their age or physical or mental incapacity;

˗ to the physician who, with the victim's consent, brings to the knowledge of the prosecutor of Faso the abuse or deprivations that they observed, on the physical or mental level, in the exercise of their profession and which allow them to presume that physical, sexual or mental violence of any kind has been committed.

When the victim is a minor or a person who is unable to protect themselves due to their age or physical or mental incapacity, their consent is not necessary;

˗ to health or social work professionals who inform the competent authorities of the dangerous nature for themselves or for others of persons who consult them and whom they know possess a weapon or have expressed their intention to acquire one. Reporting to the competent authorities made under the conditions provided for in this article may not be subject to any disciplinary sanction.

###### Article 525-3 :

In the event that any person, knowing themselves to be infected with HIV, fails to voluntarily comply with the obligation to inform their spouse, the physician must ensure that the disclosure takes place. Failure by the physician to comply with this obligation is punished by a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to one million

(1,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 525-4 :

Is punished by a sentence of imprisonment from two months to one year and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, anyone who, outside the cases provided for in Article 341-8, in bad faith, opens or suppresses letters or correspondence addressed to third parties.

Is punished by the same penalties, anyone who, in bad faith:

˗ delays or diverts correspondence that has or has not reached its destination and addressed to third parties;

˗ fraudulently takes knowledge of the content;

˗ intercepts, diverts, uses or discloses correspondence sent, transmitted or received by electronic means or proceeds with the installation of apparatus designed to carry out such interceptions.

This article is not applicable to fathers, mothers, guardians or persons responsible with regard to unemancipated minor children when they act in the interest of the latter.

#### TITRE III : DES CRIMES ET DELITS CONTRE LA FAMILLE ET LES BONNES

MŒURS

##### CHAPITRE 1 : DES CRIMES ET DELITS CONTRE LA FAMILLE

###### Section 1 : Des infractions en matière de mariage

###### Article 531-1 :

For the purposes of this code, marriage means any form of union between a man and a woman, celebrated by a civil registrar or celebrated according to customary or religious rules.

###### Article 531-2 :

Is qualified as moral, material or emotional abandonment, in violation of marital obligations and duties of relief and assistance, except by court decision, the act of abandoning, for any reason whatsoever, one's spouse. Also guilty of material, moral or emotional abandonment is anyone who, for the reasons mentioned in the preceding paragraph, abandons:

˗ a woman who is pregnant or menopausal, married according to customary or religious rules or who has had a continuous and stable relationship with her partner;

˗ any person married according to customary or religious rules or who has had a continuous and stable relationship with their partner and whose particular vulnerability, due to their age, illness, infirmity, physical or mental deficiency that is apparent or known to its perpetrator.

###### Article 531-3 :

Is punished by a sentence of imprisonment from two to six months and a fine from three hundred thousand (300,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who is guilty of moral, material or emotional abandonment, in violation of marital obligations and duties of relief and assistance.

###### Article 531-4 :

Is punished by a sentence of imprisonment from six months to two years, anyone who forces a person into marriage.

The penalty is imprisonment from one to three years if the victim is a minor. The maximum penalty is incurred if the victim is under thirteen years of age. Anyone who contracts or facilitates a marriage under such conditions is considered an accomplice.

###### Article 531-5 :

Is punished by a sentence of imprisonment from six months to two years and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to nine hundred thousand (900,000) CFA francs, anyone who, being bound by a monogamous marriage, contracts another before the dissolution of that marriage.

###### Section 2: On domestic violence

###### Article 531-8:

Domestic violence is that exercised within the framework of a relationship between spouses, cohabitants or between ascendants and descendants which is expressed through physical, psychological, sexual, economic and spiritual assaults such as:

‑ voluntary assault and battery;

‑ rape;

‑ deprivation of food or care;

‑ confiscation of civil status documents used for identification of the person;

‑ infringement of property rights;

‑ serious violations of provisions relating to family rights; ‑ infringement of freedom of work and belief.

###### Article 531-9:

Prosecution for domestic violence is initiated on complaint by the victim or their legal representative, on denunciation made to the judicial authorities, or to any competent authority, or ex officio by the public prosecutor.

Any person who commits an act classified as domestic violence is punished by a term of imprisonment of two months to one year and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.

The penalty is imprisonment of two months to three years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, when such violence has resulted in an illness or a total incapacity for personal work of more than seven days and less than twenty-one days.

When the victim is a minor, a pregnant woman, a disabled person, a legitimate or natural ascendant, or if the violence has resulted in a personal work incapacity greater than or equal to twenty-one days, the penalty is imprisonment of one year to ten years and a fine of six hundred thousand (600,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs.

If the death of the victim or permanent disability has resulted, the penalty is life imprisonment.

The court seized may in addition order the prohibition of exercise of civil and/or family rights for a period which may not exceed five years.

###### Article 531-10:

Any person who aids or assists the perpetrator of domestic violence is punished by the same penalties as the perpetrator.

Any person who has knowledge of acts of domestic violence and who refrains from reporting them to the competent authorities is punished by a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 531-11:

The act of harassing one's spouse, one's partner, one's cohabitant through repeated actions having the purpose or effect of degrading their living conditions resulting in an alteration of their physical or mental health is punished by a term of imprisonment of two years to four years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs when such acts have caused a total work incapacity of less than or equal to eight days or have not resulted in any work incapacity.

The penalty is imprisonment of three years to five years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, when the acts have caused a total work incapacity of more than eight days.

The same penalties are incurred when the offense is committed by a former spouse, a former cohabitant of the victim, or a former partner.

###### Section 3: On abandonment of family

###### Article 531-12:

Any person who is guilty of moral, material or emotional abandonment, in violation of conjugal obligations and duties of support and assistance, is punished by a term of imprisonment of two months to six months and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 531-13:

The act by the father or mother of a family of abandoning, for more than three months, the family residence and evading, without legitimate reason, their legal obligations to the extent of compromising the health, safety, morality or education of their minor child, is punished by a term of imprisonment of three months to three years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs.

This period may only be interrupted by a return to the home implying the intention to permanently resume family life.

The following are punished by a term of imprisonment of three months to three years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs:

˗ the act, by an ascendant or any other person exercising parental authority or having authority over a minor of fifteen years, of depriving them of food or care to the extent of compromising their health;

˗ the husband or cohabitant who, knowing his wife or cohabitant to be pregnant, willfully abandons her for more than three months without serious or legitimate reason.

###### Article 531-14:

The following are punished by a term of imprisonment of three months to three years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs:

˗ the act, by a person, of not executing a judicial decision or a judicially approved agreement requiring them to pay for the benefit of a minor child, a descendant, an ascendant or the spouse a pension, a contribution, subsidies or benefits of any nature owed by reason of one of the family obligations provided for by law, by remaining more than two months without fully satisfying this obligation;

˗ the act, by a person bound by the obligation to pay a pension, a contribution, subsidies or benefits of any nature, of not notifying their change of domicile to the creditor within one month from such change.

In case of recidivism, the penalty of imprisonment is always imposed.

###### Article 531-15:

In addition to the normally competent courts, the court of the residence of the abandoned person or beneficiary of the pension may hear prosecutions brought pursuant to the provisions of Articles 531-13 and 531-14 above.

Prosecutions may only be brought on complaint by the abandoned person or beneficiary of the pension or their legal representative with production of the instrument relied upon. However, they are brought ex officio by the public prosecutor when the perpetrator of the offense happens to be such legal representative.

Prosecutions are preceded by a formal notice to the debtor of the obligation or pension to comply within fifteen days. This formal notice is effected at the request of the public prosecutor by a judicial police officer in the form of an interpellation.

If the debtor is a fugitive or has no known domicile, mention is made thereof by the judicial police officer and it is proceeded with.

###### Article 531-16:

Whether or not forfeiture of parental authority is pronounced against them, the father or mother who seriously compromises through ill-treatment, through pernicious examples of drunkenness or notorious misconduct, through lack of care or through lack of necessary direction, either the health, or the safety, or the morality of one or more of their children, is punished by a term of imprisonment of three months to one year and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.

For the offenses provided for in this section, the court seized may order the prohibition of exercise of civic and family rights for a period which may not exceed five years.

##### CHAPTER 2: ON OFFENSES AGAINST CHILDREN AND VULNERABLE PERSONS

###### Section 1: On exposure or abandonment of children, incapacitated persons or elderly persons

###### Article 532-1 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from one year to three years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to seven million (7,000,000) CFA francs, whoever exposes or causes to be exposed, abandons or causes to be abandoned in a solitary place a child or an incapable person unable to protect himself or herself by reason of his or her physical or mental state.

If the exposure or abandonment has resulted in an illness, an infirmity or a total incapacity of twenty-one days or more, the term of imprisonment is from two to five years.

If it has resulted in a mutilation or a permanent infirmity, the term of imprisonment is increased from five years to ten years.

If the exposure or abandonment has caused death, the term of imprisonment is increased from eleven years to twenty-one years.

###### Article 532-2 :

If the perpetrators are the ascendants or any other persons having authority over the child or the incapable person or having his or her custody, the penalty is:

˗ imprisonment from two years to five years in the case provided for in paragraph 1 of Article 532-1 above;

˗ imprisonment from five years to ten years in the case provided for in paragraph 2 of Article 532-1 above;

˗ imprisonment from eleven years to twenty-five years in the case provided for in paragraph 3 of Article 532-1 above;

˗ life imprisonment in the case provided for in paragraph 4 of Article 532-1 above.

###### Article 532-3 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from three months to three years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, whoever exposes or causes to be exposed, abandons or causes to be abandoned in a non-solitary place a child or an incapable person unable to protect himself or herself by reason of his or her physical or mental state.

If the exposure or abandonment has resulted in an illness or total incapacity of twenty-one days or more, the penalty is imprisonment from six months to two years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to three million

(3,000,000) CFA francs.

If the child or the incapable person has remained mutilated or crippled or if he or she has remained affected by a permanent infirmity, the penalty is imprisonment from two years to five years.

If death has resulted, the penalty is imprisonment from five years to ten years.

###### Article 532-4 :

If the perpetrators are the ascendants or any other persons having authority over the child or the incapable person or having his or her custody, the penalty is:

˗ imprisonment from six months to two years in the case provided for in Article 532-3 paragraph 1 above;

˗ imprisonment from one year to three years in the case provided for in Article 532-3 paragraph 2 above;

˗ imprisonment from five years to ten years in the case provided for in Article 532-3 paragraph 3 above;

˗ imprisonment from eleven years to twenty years in the case provided for in Article 532-3 paragraph 4 above.

###### Article 532-5 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from one year to five years and a fine of one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, whoever, voluntarily, inflicts injuries, strikes blows or deprives of food or care a child under fifteen years of age to the point of compromising his or her health.

The term of imprisonment is from two years to five years and the fine from three hundred thousand (300,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs, if the injuries, blows or deprivation of food or care results in an illness or a total incapacity for work of twenty-one days or more, or if there has been premeditation or ambush.

If the perpetrators are the legitimate, natural or adoptive father and mother or other legitimate ascendants or any other persons having authority over the child or having his or her custody, the penalty is:

˗ imprisonment from two years to five years and a fine of six hundred thousand (600,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs when the illness or incapacity for personal work is less than twenty-one days;

˗ imprisonment from five years to ten years and a fine of one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs in the event of illness or total incapacity for personal work of twenty-one days or more, premeditation or ambush.

If the violence or deprivation practiced has caused death without intent to cause it, the penalty is imprisonment from eleven years to twenty-one years. If the violence or deprivation is practiced with the intent to cause death, the penalty is life imprisonment.

###### Article 532-6 :

For the offences referred to in this section, the court seised may pronounce the prohibition from exercising civil and/or family rights for a period that may not exceed five years.

###### Article 532-7 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from one year to three years and a fine of one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, whoever, for lucrative purposes or for any other advantage, induces or incites the parents or one of them to abandon their child born or unborn or provides or attempts to provide his or her intermediary services to have a child born or unborn taken in or adopted.

###### Article 532-8 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from one year to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs, whoever fraudulently benefits from benefits and social assistance intended for an elderly person.

###### Article 532-9 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from three years to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, whoever fraudulently diverts sums of money due to widowers or widows and/or to orphaned children by way of a pension or the execution of a court decision.

###### Article 532-10 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from one year to three years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs, whoever commits abandonment or exclusion from the family environment with regard to an elderly person.

If the perpetrator is a natural or legitimate descendant of the elderly person, the penalty is imprisonment from three years to five years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 532-11 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from one year to three years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to two million five hundred thousand (2,500,000) CFA francs, whoever commits an abuse of the state of ignorance or the situation of weakness, either of a minor, or of a person whose particular vulnerability due to his or her age, an illness, an infirmity, a physical or mental deficiency or a state of pregnancy, is apparent or known to its perpetrator, or of a person in a state of psychological or physical subjection resulting from the exercise of serious or repeated pressures or techniques capable of altering his or her judgment, to lead this minor or this person to an act or an abstention that are seriously prejudicial to him or her. When the offence is committed by the de facto or de jure leader of a group that pursues activities having the purpose or effect of creating, maintaining or exploiting the psychological or mental subjection of persons who participate in these activities, the penalty is imprisonment from two years to five years and a fine of six hundred thousand (600,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 532-12 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from two months to six months and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs, any person guilty of exclusion from the family environment with regard to a pregnant girl or who refuses a forced marriage.

###### Section 2: Offenses Intended to Prevent the Identification of a Child

###### Article 532-13:

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of two years to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who, knowingly, under conditions likely to make their identification impossible, moves a child, conceals them, causes them to disappear or substitutes another child for them or materially presents them as born of a woman who has not given birth or of a man who, clearly, could not be the father. When the perpetrator of the above-mentioned acts is health personnel, the penalty is imprisonment of three years to ten years and a fine of three million

(3,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs. A prohibition from practicing the profession for a period not exceeding five years may additionally be ordered.

If it is established that the child did not live, the perpetrator is punished by a term of imprisonment of two months to six months and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs.

###### Section 3: Abduction and Failure to Produce Minors

###### Article 532-14:

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of five years to ten years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who by violence, threats or fraud abducts or causes to be abducted a minor or lures, diverts or moves them or causes them to be lured, diverted or moved from the places where they were placed by authority or to whose supervision they were subject or entrusted.

###### Article 532-15:

The penalty is imprisonment of eleven years to twenty-one years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs if the minor thus abducted or diverted is under thirteen years of age. However, if the minor is found alive before the conviction decision has been rendered, the imprisonment penalty is reduced to five years to ten years.

###### Article 532-16:

The penalty is life imprisonment, regardless of the age of the minor, if the perpetrator has themselves paid or had the purpose of having themselves paid a ransom by the persons under whose authority or supervision the minor was placed.

The same penalty is applied if the purpose of the abduction was the execution of an order or condition.

However, if the minor is found alive before the conviction decision is pronounced, the penalty is imprisonment of eleven years to twenty-one years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 532-17:

In the cases provided for in Articles 532-14 to 532-16 above, the abduction is punished by life imprisonment if it has been followed by the death of the minor.

###### Article 532-18:

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to ten years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who, without violence, threat or fraud abducts or attempts to abduct or divert a minor.

###### Article 532-19:

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of two months to one year and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, when a decision has been made regarding the custody of a minor by provisionally or finally enforceable court decision, or by judicially approved agreement, the father, mother, or any other person who does not produce this minor to those who have the right to claim them or who, even without fraud or violence, abducts, diverts or causes them to be abducted or diverted from the hands of those to whom their custody has been entrusted, or from the places where the latter have placed them. If the perpetrator had been declared deprived of parental authority, the imprisonment may be extended to three years.

###### Article 532-20:

Any person who transfers their domicile to another place after divorce, legal separation or annulment of marriage while the children usually reside with them, must notify any change of their domicile and any change of the children's residence to those who may exercise a right of visitation or accommodation with respect to them by virtue of a judgment or judicially approved agreement.

If they fail to make this notification within one month, they are punished by a term of imprisonment of two months to six months and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 532-21:

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to ten years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, except where the act constitutes a punishable act of complicity, anyone who has knowingly hidden or concealed from searches a minor who has been abducted or diverted or who has removed them from the authority to which they are legally subject.

###### Section 4: Circulation of Minors

###### Article 532-22:

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of two months to six months and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, any person who, managing an establishment prohibited to minors under eighteen years of age, allows them to enter.

In case of recidivism, the permanent or temporary closure of the establishment is ordered. When the closure is ordered on a temporary basis, its duration may not exceed five years.

###### Article 532-23:

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of two months to six months and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, any person who, managing an establishment, serves alcoholic beverages to a minor under eighteen years of age even when accompanied by their parents or guardians.

In this latter case, the parents, guardians or representatives who accompany the minor children are punished by a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 532-24:

When a film prohibited particularly to minors under eighteen years of age or under thirteen years of age is shown in a cinema or any other public place, a poster of fifty centimeters by twenty bearing exclusively the mention "Film prohibited to minors under eighteen years of age" or "Film prohibited to minors under thirteen years of age" must be posted in a very conspicuous manner at ticket counters above the price list or schedule of showings or on the posters; mention must be made in a very legible manner in all advertising concerning said film including trailers.

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one month to three months and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, any person who, managing a cinema, does not ensure the publicity of the prohibition under the prescribed conditions.

In case of recidivism, the temporary closure for a period not exceeding five years, or permanent closure of the cinema is ordered.

##### CHAPTER 3: CRIMES AND OFFENSES AGAINST MORALS

###### Section 1: Offenses Against Morals and Sexual Assaults

###### Article 533-1:

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of three months to two years and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who commits a public indecency.

Constitutes a public indecency any sexual exhibition or any other intentional act contrary to good morals performed publicly or in a private place accessible to public view, likely to offend the modesty and moral sense of persons who are involuntary witnesses to it. However, such an act committed in private, in the presence of a minor constitutes an offense of inciting a minor to debauchery.

The attempt to commit public indecency is punished by the same penalties.

###### Article 533-2 :

Constitutes an indecent assault any act of a sexual nature contrary to public decency committed directly and intentionally on a minor or with violence, coercion or surprise on an adult.

###### Article 533-3 :

Is punishable by imprisonment of one year to seven years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, any indecent assault, completed or attempted without violence, coercion or surprise on the person of a minor under thirteen years of age of either sex. If the perpetrator is an ancestor of the minor or if he is one of those who have authority conferred on him by his function or if he acted in a group, he is punishable by imprisonment of seven years to ten years and a fine of one million (1,000

000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 533-4 :

Is punishable by imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, any indecent assault completed or attempted without violence, coercion or surprise on the person of a minor of thirteen to fifteen years of age of either sex. Is punishable by imprisonment of six months to five years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs any indecent assault completed or attempted without violence, coercion or surprise on the person of a minor over fifteen years of age and under eighteen years of age of either sex.

If the perpetrator is an ancestor of the minor or if he is one of those who have authority over him or if he abused the authority conferred on him by his function or if he acted in a group, he is punishable by imprisonment of four years to ten years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 533-5 :

Is punishable by imprisonment of three years to ten years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, any indecent assault completed or attempted with violence, coercion or surprise on the person of a minor under thirteen years of age, of either sex.

If the perpetrator is an ancestor of the minor or if he is one of those who have authority over him or if he abused the authority conferred on him by his function or if he acted in a group, he is punishable by imprisonment of eleven years to twenty-one years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 533-6 :

Is punishable by imprisonment of two years to ten years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs any indecent assault completed or attempted with violence, coercion or surprise on the person of a minor of thirteen to fifteen years of age, of either sex. Is punishable by imprisonment of one year to ten years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs any indecent assault completed or attempted with violence, coercion or surprise on the person of a minor over fifteen years of age and under eighteen years of age of either sex.

If the perpetrator is an ancestor of the minor or if he is one of those who have authority over the child, or if he abused the authority conferred on him by his function or if he acted in a group, he is punishable by imprisonment of eleven years to thirty years and a fine of three million (3,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 533-7 :

Is punishable by imprisonment of one year to three years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, any indecent assault completed or attempted with violence, coercion or surprise against persons of either sex.

If the perpetrator has authority over the victim or if he abused the authority conferred on him by his function or if he acted in a group, he is punishable by imprisonment of three years to ten years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 533-8 :

The attempt of indecent assault is punishable.

###### Article 533-9 :

Sexual harassment is the act of imposing on a person, repeatedly, remarks or behaviours with sexual connotation which either violate his or her dignity by reason of their degrading or humiliating nature, or create an intimidating, hostile or offensive situation against him or her.

Is assimilated to sexual harassment the act, even if not repeated, of using any form of serious pressure, with the real or apparent purpose of obtaining an act of a sexual nature, for oneself or for the benefit of a third party.

Sexual harassment is punishable by imprisonment of one year to three years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

The penalty is imprisonment of three years to five years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs when the acts are committed:

˗ by a person who abuses the authority conferred on him by his functions; ˗ on a minor under eighteen years of age;

˗ on a person whose particular vulnerability due to his age, to an illness, to an infirmity, to a physical or mental deficiency or to a state of pregnancy, is apparent or known to the perpetrator.

###### Article 533-10 :

Any act of sexual penetration, of any nature whatsoever, committed on the person of another by violence, coercion, threat or surprise constitutes rape.

Rape is punishable by imprisonment of seven years to ten years and a fine of six hundred thousand (600,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 533-11 :

Rape is punishable by imprisonment of eleven years to twenty-one years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs:

˗ when it has resulted in mutilation or permanent disability;

˗ when it is committed on a minor of thirteen to fifteen years of age at most; ˗ when it is committed on a person whose particular vulnerability, due to his age, to an illness, to an infirmity, to a physical or mental deficiency or to a state of pregnancy, is apparent or known to the perpetrator; ˗ when it is committed by an ancestor or by any other person having authority of law or fact over the victim;

˗ when it is committed by a person who abuses the authority conferred on him by his functions;

˗ when it is committed by several persons acting as perpetrators or accomplices;

˗ when it is committed with use or threat of a weapon;

˗ when the victim was put in contact with the perpetrator of the acts through the use, for the dissemination of messages intended for an undetermined public, of an electronic communication network;

˗ when it is committed in conjunction with one or more other rapes committed on other victims;

˗ when it is committed by a person acting in a state of manifest intoxication or under the manifest influence of narcotics.

###### Article 533-12 :

When rape is committed repeatedly on an intimate and habitual partner with whom the perpetrator maintains stable and continuous sexual relations or when said partner is in any physical incapacity to accomplish a sexual relation, the penalty is a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 533-13 :

Rape committed on a minor person under thirteen years of age of either sex, is punishable by imprisonment of eleven years to thirty years and a fine of three million (3,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 533-14 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of five years to ten years and a fine of nine hundred thousand (900,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, the act by a member of teaching staff or of any educational system of having a sexual relationship with a male or female pupil, apprentice or trainee who is a minor.

If this sexual relationship results in the pregnancy of the female pupil, apprentice or trainee, the term of imprisonment is seven years to ten years and the fine is three million (3,000,000) to six million (6,000,000) CFA francs.

The court seized may in addition pronounce a prohibition from practicing the profession of teacher or member of the educational system for a period which may not exceed five years.

###### Article 533-15 :

Constitutes the offense of adultery, the act by a married person of having sexual relations with a person other than their spouse.

Any person convicted of adultery is punished by a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 533-16 :

Prosecution for adultery may only be initiated on complaint of the spouse. Withdrawal of the complaint ends the prosecution brought against the adulterous spouse and their accomplice.

Withdrawal occurring after a conviction that has become final stops the effects of that conviction both with regard to the adulterous spouse and their accomplice.

###### Article 533-17 :

Proof of adultery is established either by bailiff's report, or by official report of flagrante delicto drawn up by a judicial police officer, or by confession related in letters, correspondence or documents emanating from the accused or their accomplice or by judicial confession.

###### Article 533-18 :

Constitutes the offense of incest punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, the act of having sexual relations with one's ascendants or descendants without limitation of degree or with a brother or sister who is a full, consanguine or uterine sibling.

Except in cases of notorious cohabitation or incestuous marriage, prosecution may only be initiated on complaint of a relative and only against the person or persons designated in the complaint.

###### Section 2 : Of prostitution and corruption of youth

###### Article 533-19 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of two years to five years and a fine of one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, whoever habitually incites to debauchery or favors the corruption of minors from thirteen to eighteen years of either sex or even occasionally of minors under eighteen years.

###### Article 533-20 :

Prostitution is the act by a person of either sex of habitually engaging in sexual acts with others for remuneration. Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one month to three months and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, the act, by any means, including by an even passive attitude, of publicly soliciting others with a view to inciting them to sexual relations in exchange for remuneration or a promise of remuneration.

###### Article 533-21 :

The attempt of the offenses provided for in articles 531-1 to 533-20 above is punishable.

###### Article 533-22 :

Procuring is the act, by anyone, in whatever manner: ˗ of helping, assisting or protecting the prostitution of others;

˗ of deriving profit from the prostitution of others, sharing the proceeds thereof or receiving subsidies from a person habitually engaging in prostitution;

˗ of recruiting, leading or diverting a person with a view to prostitution or exerting pressure on them to prostitute themselves or continue to do so.

Procuring is punished by a term of imprisonment of three years to ten years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to six million (6,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 533-23 :

Is assimilated to procuring and punished by the same penalties as those provided for in article 533-22 above, the act, by anyone, in whatever manner:

˗ of acting as intermediary between two persons one of whom engages in prostitution and the other exploits or remunerates the prostitution of others; ˗ of facilitating for a procurer the justification of fictitious resources; ˗ of being unable to justify resources corresponding to one's lifestyle while living with a person who habitually engages in prostitution or while being in habitual relations with one or more persons engaging in prostitution;

˗ of obstructing the action of prevention, control, assistance or rehabilitation undertaken by qualified organizations with regard to persons in danger of prostitution or engaging in prostitution.

###### Article 533-24 :

Procuring is punished by a term of imprisonment of five years to ten years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs when it is committed:

˗ with regard to a person whose particular vulnerability, due to their age, an illness, an infirmity, a physical or mental deficiency or a state of pregnancy, is apparent or known to its perpetrator;

˗ with regard to several persons;

˗ with regard to a person who has been incited to engage in prostitution either outside the territory of Burkina Faso, or upon their arrival on Burkinabe territory;

˗ by a legitimate, natural or adoptive ascendant of the person who prostitutes themselves or by a person who has authority over them or abuses the authority conferred upon them by their functions;

˗ by a person called to participate, by virtue of their functions, in the fight against prostitution, the protection of health or the maintenance of public order;

˗ by a person carrying a weapon;

˗ with the use of constraint, violence or fraudulent maneuvers; ˗ by several persons acting in the capacity of perpetrators or accomplices, without their constituting an organized gang;

˗ through the use, for the dissemination of messages intended for an undetermined public, of an electronic communication network.

###### Article 533-25 :

The acts provided for in article 533-24 above are punished by a term of imprisonment of eleven years to thirty years and a fine of three million (3,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs when they are committed in an organized gang.

Procuring committed by resorting to torture or acts of barbarism is punished by life imprisonment.

###### Article 533-26 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of five years to ten years and a fine of three million (3,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs the act, by anyone, acting directly or through an intermediary:

˗ of owning, managing, operating, directing, running, financing or contributing to financing an establishment of prostitution;

˗ of owning, managing, operating, directing, running, financing or contributing to financing any establishment open to the public or used by the public, accepting or habitually tolerating that one or more persons engage in prostitution inside the establishment or its annexes or seek clients there with a view to prostitution;

˗ of selling or making available to one or more persons premises or locations not used by the public, knowing that they will engage in prostitution there;

˗ of selling, renting or making available, in whatever manner, to one or more persons, vehicles of any nature knowing that they will engage in prostitution there.

###### Article 533-27 :

Any person who habitually receives one or more persons engaging in prostitution inside a hotel, boarding house, drinking establishment, club, circle, dance hall or place of entertainment or their annexes or any place open to the public or used by the public and of which he is the holder, manager or employee shall be punished by a term of imprisonment of two years to five years and a fine of one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs. The same penalties are applicable to any person who assists said holders, managers or employees.

In all cases, the conviction decision must order the withdrawal of the license of which the convicted person was a beneficiary. It may, in addition, order the temporary or permanent closure of the establishment.

###### Article 533-28 :

Any person who, by gestures, words or by any other means, publicly solicits persons of either sex with a view to inciting them to debauchery shall be punished by a term of imprisonment of two months to one year and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 533-29 :

Any person who tolerates the habitual and clandestine practice of debauchery in premises or locations not used by the public which he has at his disposal in any capacity whatsoever shall be punished by a term of imprisonment of two months to two years and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 533-30 :

For the offenses provided for in this section, the court seized may order the prohibition of civil and/or family rights for a period which may not exceed five years.

###### Section 3 : De la vente d'enfants, de la prostitution des enfants et de la

pornographie mettant en scène des enfants

###### Article 533-31 :

For the purposes of this section:

˗ sale of children: any act or transaction whereby a child is delivered by any person or group of persons to another person or to another group of persons in return for remuneration or any other advantage;

˗ prostitution of children: the use of a child for the purposes of sexual activities in return for remuneration or any other form of advantage;

˗ child pornography: any representation, by whatever means, of a child engaging in explicit sexual activities, whether real or simulated, or any representation of the body of a child, for principally sexual purposes;

˗ pornographic material: any medium including methods and materials conveying or serving to convey a visual or audio representation or a description of a person engaged in a real or simulated sexual act, demonstration or performance;

˗ victim of sale of children, child pornography and prostitution of children: any child who has been the object of sale of children and/or child pornography and/or prostitution of children;

˗ witness of sale of children, child pornography and prostitution of children: any person, eyewitness or indirect, that is to say having heard cries coming from the places where these offenses were committed.

###### Paragraphe 1 : De la vente d'enfants

###### Article 533-32 :

Child means any human being under the age of eighteen years. The age of the child is determined by the production of birth certificates, declaratory judgments or any other documents corroborated by medical examination. In case of conflict as to the determination of age, the competent court seized shall rule at its discretion.

If the documents produced specify only the year of birth, it shall be considered to have occurred on the thirty-first of December of the said year. If the month is specified, the birth shall be considered to have occurred on the last day of the said month.

###### Article 533-33 :

The offense of sale of children consists of:

˗ offering, delivering or accepting a child, whatever the means used, for the purposes of exploiting him, transferring the child's organs for consideration, subjecting the child to forced labor;

˗ improperly obtaining, as an intermediary, consent to the adoption of a child, in return for remuneration or any other form of advantage.

###### Article 533-34 :

Sale of a child is punished by a term of imprisonment of five years to ten years and a fine of one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Paragraphe 2 : De la prostitution des enfants

###### Article 533-35 :

The offense of prostitution of children consists of offering, accepting, procuring or obtaining a child for the purposes of prostitution.

###### Article 533-36 :

Child prostitution is punished by a term of imprisonment of five years to ten years and a fine of one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Paragraphe 3 : De la pornographie enfantine

###### Article 533-37 :

The offense of child pornography consists of any person: ˗ offering, accepting, procuring or obtaining a child for the purposes of pornography as defined by law;

˗ producing, creating, distributing, disseminating, importing, exporting, offering, ordering, selling, purchasing, storing, possessing or holding through a computer system or through any other medium child pornography;

˗ exposing pornographic material to a child to train him in sexual activities;

˗ using the Internet or any other computer technology to manufacture, disseminate, acquire, exchange images or pornographic materials depicting children;

˗ using the Internet or any other computer technology to attract a child online for the purposes of pornography;

˗ using the Internet or any other computer technology or material to view pornographic images depicting children;

˗ directing to pornographic material depicting children or facilitating the possession or downloading of pornographic materials depicting children;

˗ failing to take measures to prevent the possession, viewing or downloading of pornographic materials depicting children;

˗ facilitating for a child access to images, documents, sound or a representation of a pornographic nature;

˗ habitually consulting or in return for payment for an online public communication service making available images or videos of child pornography;

˗ making, for any adult person, sexual propositions to a child under fifteen years of age or to a person presenting himself as such by using a means of electronic communication.

###### Article 533-38 :

Child pornography is punished by a term of imprisonment of five years to ten years and a fine of one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs.

In the case referred to in Article 533-37 dash 9, the term of imprisonment is eleven to fifteen years and the fine from two million five hundred thousand (2,500,000) francs to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Paragraphe 4 : Des dispositions communes

###### Article 533-39 :

Any person suspected either as perpetrator or as accomplice of one of the offenses referred to in this section, committed outside Burkina Faso, may be prosecuted and tried in accordance with Burkinabè law when the victim is of Burkinabè nationality.

###### Article 533-40 :

Any person who, having taken part in an association or agreement with a view to committing one of the offenses provided for in this section, may benefit from mitigating circumstances if, having revealed the existence of this association or this agreement to the judicial authority, they thereby allow the identification of the other persons involved and/or prevent the commission of the offense.

###### Article 533-41 :

Any person who, in the exercise of their profession or on any other occasion, has knowledge of cases of sale of children, child prostitution or child pornography, has the obligation to inform the competent judicial or administrative authority, under penalty of prosecution for complicity.

###### Article 533-42 :

In case of recidivism of one of the offenses provided for in this section, the penalty incurred is from eleven years to twenty-one years of imprisonment and a fine of three million (3 000 000) to six million (6 000 000) CFA francs.

###### Article 533-43 :

The imprisonment penalty is from eleven years to twenty-one years and the fine from three million (3 000 000) to six million (6 000 000) CFA francs, when the offense of sale of children, child prostitution or pornography involving children has been committed in one of the following aggravating circumstances:

˗ if the victim is particularly vulnerable due to a disability; ˗ if it resulted in temporary disability;

˗ if the victim is sequestered, deprived of food or exposed in a public or private place;

˗ if the act was committed by fraud or violence, by use of false capacity, false title or falsified or altered documents or false authorizations;

˗ if the perpetrator uses narcotics or any other substance likely to alter the victim's will;

˗ if the perpetrator has abused the authority conferred upon them by their functions or capacity with regard to the victim;

˗ if the perpetrator is carrying an apparent or concealed weapon;

˗ if the perpetrator is an ascendant or a person having authority over the victim.

###### Article 533-44 :

The perpetrator of the offenses referred to in articles 533-33, 533-35, and 533-37 incurs life imprisonment:

˗ when it results for the victim, a mutilation or permanent disability;

˗ when it results in the death of the victim.

###### Article 533-45 :

The court seized orders:

˗ the confiscation of the means of committing the offense as well as the proceeds of the offense;

˗ the confiscation of property or income derived from the offense.

###### Article 533-46 :

The court seized may furthermore:

˗ pronounce the prohibition of residence and/or temporary prohibition from exercising certain civic, civil or family rights;

˗ order the temporary or permanent withdrawal of license, approval or any other authorization or administrative document from any entity regardless of its legal form or from any person whose activity facilitated the commission of the offense;

˗ order the provisional or permanent closure of premises used for the commission of said offenses.

###### Paragraphe 5 : Protection of victims and witnesses

###### Article 533-47 :

The State guarantees the protection of victims and witnesses of sale of children, child prostitution and child pornography. No one may be subject to attacks, disciplinary sanctions or dismissal for having testified to the acts defined in article 533-31.

###### Article 533-48 :

Victims are assisted either by a social worker, or by a recognized person working in the field of childhood and approved by the competent children's court, or by the person who exercises parental authority. The child witness is assisted either by a social worker, or by a recognized person working in the field of childhood and approved by the competent children's court, or by the person who exercises parental authority.

###### Article 533-49 :

For the protection of the identity and privacy of victims and witnesses, the trial courts may order in camera proceedings. The trial courts may exempt victims or witnesses from appearing at the hearing or take any useful measures to protect their identity and privacy.

###### Article 533-50 :

The victims of the offenses referred to in this section are assisted before the investigation and trial courts by a lawyer of their choice or appointed ex officio.

###### Article 533-51 :

The public prosecutor may request the placement under guardianship of victims whose legal representative is not known or does not present sufficient guarantees for safeguarding the rights and welfare of the child, and this, after, where applicable, social investigation initiated by the competent services.

###### Article 533-52 :

Children who are victims or witnesses of an offense of sale of children, child prostitution or child pornography are not criminally liable.

## LIVRE VI : CRIMES AND OFFENSES AGAINST PROPERTY

#### TITRE I : THEFT, EXTORTION, ORGANIZED CRIME,

FRAUD, BREACH OF TRUST, USURY,

RECEIVING STOLEN GOODS, COUNTERFEITING

##### CHAPITRE 1 : THEFT AND EXTORTION

###### Article 611-1 :

Any person who fraudulently removes a thing belonging to another is guilty of theft.

###### Article 611-2 :

Theft is punished by a sentence of imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500 000) to one million (1 000 000) CFA francs.

###### Article 611-3 :

Any building, dwelling, lodge, caravan, tent or hut, even mobile, which, even without being currently inhabited, is intended for habitation and everything dependent on it such as courtyards, farmyards, stables, buildings enclosed therein, whatever their use, is deemed to be an inhabited house.

###### Article 611-4 :

Any forcing, breaking, degradation, demolition, removal of walls, roofs, floors, doors, windows, locks, padlocks or other tools or instruments used to close or prevent passage and any kind of enclosure, whatever it may be, is qualified as breaking and entering.

External breaking and entering is that by means of which one can enter houses, courtyards, farmyards, enclosures or outbuildings or private apartments or dwellings.

Internal breaking and entering is that which, after entry into the places mentioned in the preceding paragraph, is done to doors, interior enclosures, as well as cupboards or other closed furniture.

Included in the class of internal breaking and entering is the simple removal of crates, boxes, bales under canvas and rope, and other closed furniture containing any effects, although the breaking and entering was not done on the spot.

###### Article 611-5 :
