Scaling is the act of entering any place, either over an element of enclosure, or through any opening not intended to serve as an entrance.

###### Article 611-6 :

All picks, imitated, counterfeited or altered keys or keys which were not intended by the owner or tenant for any locks to which the offender has used them are qualified as false keys.

The genuine key unduly retained by the perpetrator of the offense is also considered a false key.

###### Article 611-7 :

The perpetrator of a theft carrying an apparent or concealed weapon is punished by a sentence of imprisonment of eleven years to twenty-one years and a fine of one million five hundred thousand (1 500 000) to five million (5 000 000) CFA francs. The same penalty applies to the perpetrator who held a weapon in a motorized vehicle used to go to the scene of the offense or to ensure his escape.

###### Article 611-8 :

Is punished by a sentence of imprisonment from eleven years to thirty years and a fine of three million (3 000 000) to ten million (10 000 000) CFA francs, anyone who commits a theft with at least three of the following circumstances: ˗ if the theft was committed with violence or threats of violence or illegal wearing of a uniform or usurpation of an authority function;

˗ if the theft was committed at night;

˗ if the theft was committed in a group by two or more persons;

˗ if the theft was committed by means of scaling, external or internal breaking and entering, underground opening or false keys in a house, apartment, room or dwelling inhabited or used for habitation or their outbuildings;

˗ if the perpetrator of the theft secured the use of a motorized vehicle in order to facilitate his undertaking or favor his escape;

˗ if the perpetrator is a domestic servant even when he committed the theft against persons he did not serve, but who were either in his employer's house or in the one where he accompanied him;

˗ if the perpetrator is an employee or apprentice in the house, workshop or store of his employer or if he is an individual working in the dwelling where he stole.

###### Article 611-9 :

Is punished by a sentence of imprisonment from twenty years to thirty years and a fine of five million (5 000 000) to ten million (10 000 000) CFA francs, anyone who commits a theft on public roads or in vehicles used for the transport of passengers, mail or baggage or within the precincts of stations, airports, disembarkation or embarkation quays when the theft was committed in the circumstances referred to in the preceding article. If the victim sustained injuries, the sentence is life imprisonment.

###### Article 611-10 :

Are considered public roads, the roads, tracks, paths or all other places dedicated to public use located outside urban areas and where any individual can freely circulate at any hour of the day or night without legal opposition from anyone.

###### Article 611-11 :

Theft is punished by a sentence of imprisonment from five years to ten years, and a fine of one million (1 000 000) to three million (3 000 000) CFA francs when it:

˗ is committed by several persons acting as perpetrators or accomplices, without constituting an organized gang;

˗ is committed by a person holding public authority or charged with a public service mission, in the exercise or on the occasion of the exercise of his functions or mission;

˗ is committed by a person who unduly assumes the status of a person holding public authority or charged with a public service mission;

˗ is preceded, accompanied or followed by threats of violence or violence against others not having caused total incapacity for work;

˗ is committed in a place used or intended for the storage of funds, securities, goods or equipment;

˗ is committed in a vehicle assigned to public passenger transport or in a place intended for access to a means of public passenger transport;

˗ is preceded, accompanied or followed by an act of destruction, degradation or damage;

˗ is committed by reason of the membership or non-membership, real or supposed, of the victim in a particular ethnic group, nation, race or religion;

˗ is committed by a person voluntarily concealing all or part of his face in order not to be identified;

˗ is committed in educational or teaching establishments as well as, during the entries or exits of students or in a time very close to them, in the vicinity of these establishments;

˗ is preceded, accompanied or followed by violence against others having caused total incapacity for work for eight days at most;

‑ is committed by means of scaling, external or internal breaking and entering, underground opening or false keys in a building even not used for habitation;

‑ is committed at night;

˗ is committed with illegal wearing of a uniform or usurpation of an authority function;

˗ is committed during a fire or after an explosion, collapse, flood, catastrophe, revolt, riot or any other disturbance;

˗ involved an object that ensured the safety of any means of public or private transport.

###### Article 611-12 :

Theft is punished by a sentence of imprisonment from five years to seven years and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250 000) to two million (2 000 000) CFA francs when it is committed by an adult with the help of one or more minors aged thirteen to less than eighteen years, acting as perpetrators or accomplices.

The sentences are increased to five years to ten years of imprisonment and a fine of one million (1 000 000) to three million (3 000 000) CFA francs when the adult is helped by one or more minors under thirteen years of age.

###### Article 611-13 :

Theft is punished by a sentence of imprisonment from eleven years to twenty-one years and a fine of one million five hundred thousand (1 500 000) to five million (5 000

000) CFA francs when it involves:

˗ a movable object classified or registered in application of the provisions of the law relating to the protection of cultural heritage in Burkina Faso, or a private archive document classified in application of the provisions of the same law;

˗ an archaeological discovery made during excavations or fortuitously; ˗ a cultural property that falls under the movable public domain or that is exhibited, preserved or deposited, even temporarily, either in a museum of Burkina Faso, a library, a media library or an archives service, or in a place belonging to a public person or a private person ensuring a mission of general interest, or in a building assigned to worship.

The sentence of imprisonment is twenty-one years to thirty years and the fine five million (5 000 000) to ten million (10 000 000) CFA francs when the offense provided for in this article is committed with one of the circumstances provided for in Article 611-7.

The fines mentioned in this article may be increased up to half the value of the stolen property.

###### Article 611-14 :

Theft is punished by a sentence of imprisonment from eleven years to twenty and years and a fine of one million five hundred thousand (1 500 000) to five million (5 000

000) CFA francs:

˗ when it is facilitated by the state of a person whose particular vulnerability, due to his age, illness, infirmity, physical or mental deficiency, is apparent or known to its perpetrator;

˗ when it is facilitated by the state of a person whose particular vulnerability, due to a state of pregnancy, is apparent or known to its perpetrator.

###### Article 611-15 :

Is punished by a sentence of imprisonment from six months to three years and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250 000) to six hundred thousand (600 000) CFA francs, anyone who counterfeits or alters keys.

The sentence of imprisonment is four years to ten years and the fine one million (1 000 000) to three million (3 000 000) CFA francs, if the guilty party is a locksmith by profession.

The court seised orders the confiscation of the false keys and equipment. The court may also order on a provisional basis for a period not exceeding five years or on a definitive basis, the closure of the establishment in the cases referred to in paragraph 2.

###### Article 611-16 :

Is punished by a sentence of imprisonment from three years to ten years and a fine of one million (1 000 000) to three million (3 000 000) CFA francs, anyone who steals from fields animals, beasts of burden or draft, large and small livestock or agricultural instruments.

The same penalties are applicable to the theft of wood from cuttings, stones from quarries, as well as the theft of fish from ponds, fish farms or reservoirs.

###### Article 611-17:

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of two years to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who steals from fields crops or other useful products of the earth detached from the soil or not, or from granaries.

If the theft was committed at night, either by several persons, or with the aid of a vehicle or pack animals, the imprisonment is five years to ten years and the fine one million (1,000,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 611-18:

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to ten years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who by any means whatsoever fraudulently withdraws from a distribution network running water, fuel, electrical energy or any other energy having economic value or fraudulently uses any product emanating from a device for the purpose of transmitting or receiving information.

###### Article 611-19:

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of six months to one year and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, anyone who uses a motorized vehicle without the knowledge or against the will of the owner.

Prosecution takes place only upon complaint of the injured person; withdrawal of the complaint terminates the prosecution.

###### Article 611-20:

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, the co-heir or claimant to an estate who fraudulently disposes before partition of all or part of the inheritance.

###### Article 611-21:

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, the person under seizure who voluntarily destroys or diverts seized objects entrusted to his custody or that of a third party.

###### Article 611-22:

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, any debtor, borrower or third party pledgor who diverts or voluntarily destroys an object given as pledge of which he is the owner.

###### Article 611-23:

Is punished by imprisonment of two months to one year and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, or one of these two penalties, anyone who, having fortuitously found a movable thing, appropriates it without notifying the competent local authority or the owner.

Is punished by the same penalty anyone who fraudulently appropriates a movable thing that came into his possession by mistake or fortuitously.

###### Article 611-24:

Is punished by a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, anyone who having found a treasure, even on his property, refrains from notifying the public authority within fifteen days of the discovery.

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of two months to six months and two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, any finder who, having or not having notified the public authority, appropriates the treasure, in whole or in part, without having been sent into possession by the competent magistrate.

###### Article 611-25:

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of two months to six months and two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, anyone who, knowing that he is in absolute impossibility of paying or who is determined not to pay, has beverages or food served to him which he consumes in whole or in part in establishments intended for that purpose, even if he is lodged in said establishments.

The same penalty is applicable to anyone who, knowing that he is in absolute impossibility of paying or who is determined not to pay, has one or more rooms allocated to him in a hotel, inn or camp and effectively occupies them.

However, in the cases provided for in the preceding paragraphs, the occupation of lodging must not exceed fifteen hotel days.

###### Article 611-26:

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of two months to six months and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) francs to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, anyone who, knowing that he is in impossibility of paying or who is determined not to pay, hires a cab or takes a seat in a public transport vehicle.

###### Article 611-27:

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of two months to six months and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) francs to six hundred thousand (600

000) CFA francs, anyone who, knowing that he is in absolute impossibility of paying or who is determined not to pay, has fuel or lubricants served to him by distribution professionals.

###### Article 611-28:

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of five years to ten years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who, by force, violence or constraint, extorts the signature or delivery of a writing, an act, computer data, a title or any document whatsoever containing or effecting obligations, dispositions or releases.

###### Article 611-29:

Is guilty of blackmail and punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to ten years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who, by means of written or verbal threat, of revelation or defamatory imputation, obtains either the delivery of funds or securities, or the delivery of computer data, or the signature or delivery of writings provided for in article 611-28 above.

###### Article 611-30:

In all cases provided for in this chapter, the court seized may further pronounce the prohibition from exercising civil rights and/or prohibition from residence for a duration that may not exceed five years.

The attempt of the offenses provided for in this chapter is punishable.

##### CHAPITRE 2: DU GRAND BANDITISME

###### Article 612-1:

For the purposes of this chapter, acts of armed robbery are understood to mean thefts characterized by the use of any weapons, all forms of violence against persons or any other mechanical or chemical means.

###### Article 612-2:

The following in particular constitute acts of armed robbery:

˗ theft, when it was committed on public roads or in private vehicles or vehicles used for transporting passengers, correspondence or luggage, in the circumstances provided for in article 612-1 above; ˗ theft, when it was preceded, accompanied or followed by another crime; ˗ theft committed with use of a weapon;

˗ theft committed with carrying of a visible or concealed weapon;

˗ theft committed with violence or accompanied by any act of barbarity or torture;

˗ theft committed by using mechanical or chemical means.

###### Article 612-3:

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of eleven years to life imprisonment and a fine of five million (5,000,000) to fifty million (50,000,000) CFA francs, any person found guilty of an act of armed robbery as defined in articles 612-1 and 612-2 above.

The provisions of this chapter are not applicable in case of attack on life.

###### Article 612-4:

In case of conviction, the court may pronounce a sentence with partial suspension.

The court that pronounces a firm term of imprisonment must accompany it with a security period at least equal to half of the sentence pronounced. The security period determines a maximum non-reducible detention period.

###### Article 612-5:

In matters of armed robbery, special protection may be granted to witnesses as well as to their families.

###### Article 612-6 :

The offenses provided for in this chapter are recorded and prosecuted in accordance with the provisions of criminal procedure applicable to grand banditry.

##### CHAPTER 3: FRAUD AND BREACH OF TRUST

###### Section 1: Fraud

###### Article 613-1 :

Is guilty of fraud any person who, either by using a false name or a false capacity, or by abusing a true capacity, or by employing fraudulent schemes to deceive a natural or legal person and thereby induce them, to their detriment or to the detriment of another, to hand over funds, securities, computer data or any property, to provide a service or to consent to an act operating an obligation or discharge.

###### Article 613-2 :

Fraud is punished by a term of imprisonment of two years to five years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

The penalty is five years to ten years of imprisonment and a fine of three million (3,000,000) to fifteen million (15,000,000) CFA francs when the fraud is committed:

˗ by a person holding public authority or charged with a public service mission, in the exercise or on the occasion of the exercise of their functions or mission;

˗ by a person who improperly assumes the capacity of a person holding public authority or charged with a public service mission;

˗ by a person having made a public appeal for the issuance of shares, bonds, notes, interests or any securities, either of a company or of a commercial or industrial enterprise;

˗ by a person who makes a public appeal for the issuance of securities or for the collection of funds for humanitarian or social assistance purposes;

˗ by a person using a means of electronic communication;

˗ to the detriment of a person whose particular vulnerability, due to their age, an illness, an infirmity, a physical or psychological deficiency or a state of pregnancy, is apparent or known to its perpetrator.

The term of imprisonment is seven years to ten years and the fine five million (5,000,000) to thirty million (30,000,000) CFA francs when the fraud is committed by an organized gang.

In all cases, the court seized may pronounce the prohibition from exercising civil rights and/or the prohibition of residence, for a period that cannot exceed five years.

The attempt of the offenses provided for in this section is punishable.

###### Section 2: Breach of trust

###### Article 613-3 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, any person who misappropriates or squanders to the detriment of another, funds, securities, computer data or any property that have been entrusted to them and that they have accepted on condition of returning them, producing them or making a specified use of them.

###### Article 613-4 :

The term of imprisonment is five years to ten years and the fine three million (3,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs when the breach of trust is committed:

˗ by a ministerial public officer, a judicial representative, a sequestrator, a business agent, a commercial representative or any person whose profession is to manage the affairs of others, in the exercise or on the occasion of the exercise of their functions or profession;

˗ by a person who makes a public appeal in order to obtain the delivery of funds or securities either for their own account, or as an officer (director, administrator, manager in particular) or agent in law or in fact of an industrial or commercial enterprise;

˗ by any other person who, habitually, engages in or lends their assistance, even on an ancillary basis, to transactions involving the property of third parties on whose behalf they collect funds or securities; ˗ by a person using a means of electronic communication;

˗ to the detriment of an association that makes a public appeal for the collection of funds for humanitarian or social assistance purposes;

˗ to the detriment of a person whose particular vulnerability, due to their age, an illness, an infirmity, a physical or psychological deficiency or a state of pregnancy, apparent or known to its perpetrator.

###### Article 613-5 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one month to three months and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, any person who, after having produced in a judicial dispute any deed, document or brief, removes it in any manner whatsoever.

###### Article 613-6 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of two years to five years and a fine of six hundred thousand (600,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs, any person who abuses the needs, weaknesses or passions of a minor or an incapacitated adult, to make them sign, to their detriment, obligations, receipts or releases, for a loan of money or movable things or commercial instruments or any other binding instruments, in whatever form this transaction was made or disguised.

The fine may however be increased to one quarter of the value of the restitutions and damages if this quarter exceeds the maximum provided for in the preceding paragraph.

The court seized may furthermore pronounce the prohibition from exercising civil rights, and/or public functions or employment as well as the prohibition of residence for a period that cannot exceed five years.

###### Article 613-7 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs, any person who, abusing a blank signature that has been entrusted to them, fraudulently writes above it an obligation or a release or any other act that may compromise the person or fortune of the signatory.

In the case where the blank signature has not been entrusted to them, the perpetrator is prosecuted as a forger.

##### CHAPTER 4: RECEIVING STOLEN GOODS AND COUNTERFEITING

###### Section 1: Receiving stolen goods

###### Article 614-1 :

Receiving stolen goods is the act of concealing, holding or transmitting a thing or computer data, or acting as an intermediary in order to transmit them, knowing that this thing or this computer data comes from a crime or an offense.

Also constitutes receiving stolen goods the act, knowingly, of benefiting, by any means, from the proceeds of a crime or an offense.

Receiving stolen goods is punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to ten years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs. The fine may be increased beyond three million (3,000,000) CFA francs without exceeding half the value of the objects received, all without prejudice to heavier penalties if applicable in case of complicity in a crime.

###### Article 614-2 :

Are punished by the penalties provided for in Article 614-1 above, heirs who divert or conceal the assets of an estate or falsely evaluate the composition of an estate, with the aim of harming the interests of other co-heirs.

The accomplices of the perpetrators of the offenses provided for in the preceding paragraph, when they exercise prerogatives of parental authority, guardianship or curatorship are punished with the maximum penalties provided.

The penalties of paragraph 2 are applied to the liquidator of an estate.

###### Article 614-3 :

In the case where a criminal penalty is applicable to the act that procured the concealed things, the receiver is punished with the penalty attached by law to the crime and to the circumstances of the crime of which they had knowledge at the time of receiving.

###### Section 2 : Of Counterfeiting

###### Article 614-4:

Constitutes the offense of counterfeiting and is punished by imprisonment of two months to one year and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, any edition of writing, musical composition, drawing, painting or any other production published in Burkina Faso or abroad, printed or engraved in whole or in part, made in bad faith and in defiance of laws and regulations relating to artistic or literary property.

Is punished by the same penalty, the export or import of counterfeit works.

Is also punished by the same penalty any reproduction, representation, distribution, translation, adaptation by any means whatsoever of a work of the mind in violation of copyright as defined and regulated by law.

###### Article 614-5:

The penalty is imprisonment of six months to two years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to four million (4,000,000) CFA francs, if it is established that the perpetrator habitually engages in the acts referred to in the preceding article.

###### Article 614-6:

The counterfeit works as well as the proceeds or shares of proceeds that have been confiscated are handed over to the author or his beneficiaries to compensate them accordingly for the damage they have suffered; the remainder of their compensation or the full compensation, if there has been no confiscation of material, counterfeit objects or proceeds, is settled by ordinary means. The court seized may order at the request of the civil party the publication of the conviction.

###### Article 614-7:

Is punished by imprisonment of one year to three years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, whoever discloses without the authorization of the person to whom it belongs, a fact or an industrial or commercial process of which he became aware by reason of his employment.

##### CHAPTER 5: OF USURY

###### Article 615-1:

Constitutes a usurious loan, any loan or any agreement concealing a money loan granted, in any matter, at an effective global interest rate exceeding, at the date of its stipulation, the usury rate.

The usury rate is determined by the Council of Ministers of the West African Monetary Union. It is published in the Official Journal or in a legal notices newspaper, at the initiative of the minister responsible for finance.

###### Article 615-2:

Is punished by imprisonment of two months to two years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs or one of these penalties, whoever grants to another a usurious loan or knowingly provides, in any capacity and in any manner whatsoever, directly or indirectly, his assistance in obtaining or granting a usurious loan.

In case of recidivism, the maximum penalty shall be increased to five years' imprisonment and fifteen million (15,000,000) CFA francs fine.

###### Article 615-3:

In addition to the penalties set by Article 615-2 above, the court may order: ˗ the publication of its decision at the expense of the convicted person in the newspapers it designates, as well as in any form it deems appropriate;

˗ the temporary or permanent closure of the enterprise not approved as a credit institution or Decentralized Financial System (DFS) which has engaged or whose managers have engaged in usurious operations, together with the appointment of an administrator or liquidator;

˗ in the case of credit institutions and DFSs, the temporary or permanent closure for the reasons invoked in the second bullet point of this article may only be pronounced after favorable opinion of the Banking Commission of WAEMU or the Central Bank, under the conditions and according to the procedure provided by the provisions of the law on banking regulations and that on the regulation of DFSs, with regard to collective procedures for clearing liabilities.

In case of temporary closure, the offender or the enterprise must continue to pay to its personnel the salaries and allowances of any kind to which it is entitled. This period may not exceed three months.

In case of recidivism, permanent closure is ordered.

###### Article 615-4:

Are liable to the penalties provided in Article 615-2 above and possibly to the measures set in Article 615-3 above, those who, responsible in any capacity for the management or administration of an enterprise, company, association, cooperative, or other legal person, knowingly allow any person subject to their authority or control to violate the provisions of this chapter.

##### CHAPTER 6: OF FAMILY IMMUNITIES

###### Article 616-1:

Are not punishable and may only give rise to civil reparations theft, fraud, breach of trust and receiving committed between spouses or by ascendants to the detriment of their children or other descendants.

###### Article 616-2:

Theft, fraud, breach of trust or receiving committed by descendants or between collateral relatives up to and including the fourth degree or relatives by marriage, may only be prosecuted upon complaint of the injured person. Withdrawal of the complaint terminates the prosecution.

###### Article 616-3:

Persons other than those designated in Articles 616-1 and 616-2 above who have acted as co-authors or accomplices of these offenses or who have received the proceeds thereof, may not benefit from the provisions of said articles.

#### TITLE II: OF INFRINGEMENTS ON IMMOVABLE PROPERTY, DESTRUCTIONS, DEGRADATIONS AND DAMAGES

##### CHAPTER 1: OF INFRINGEMENTS ON IMMOVABLE PROPERTY

###### Article 621-1:

Constitutes stellionate the act of appropriating, alienating, selling, exchanging, mortgaging or pledging an immovable property which one knew one did not own or of deliberately presenting it as not encumbered with a mortgage or as alienable.

###### Article 621-2:

Stellionate is punished by imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

Is deemed a stellionataire:

˗ whoever has registered in his name an immovable property which he knows he does not own;

˗ whoever has a real right recorded on a title which he knows does not belong to him and whoever knowingly accepts the certificate of registration thus established;

˗ whoever has an immovable property registered while knowingly omitting to have recorded the mortgages, real rights or charges with which this immovable property is encumbered;

˗ whoever, knowingly, transfers a title of ownership which he knows does not belong to him and whoever knowingly accepts this transfer;

˗ whoever, required to register a legal mortgage on property subject to registration or a forced mortgage on registered property, grants a conventional mortgage on property that should have been encumbered.

###### Article 621-3:

Also constitutes the offense of stellionate and is punished by the penalties provided in Article 621-2 above one of the following acts:

˗ the fraudulent gift or fraudulent alienation of an immovable property;

˗ the fraudulent organization of the loss or destruction of an urban dwelling permit, a title of ownership or any other provisional or final land title or document;

˗ the sale to another person, a second or multiple times, of an immovable property previously sold to a person;

˗ the act of selling, exchanging, giving in payment or as security, fraudulently, an inalienable immovable property or one that is the subject of an unresolved dispute;

˗ the act of selling, exchanging, giving in payment or as security, fraudulently, an immovable property that is the subject of a promise of sale.

###### Article 621-4 :

The imprisonment penalty is from two years to seven years and the fine from six hundred thousand (600,000) to six million (6,000,000) CFA francs, if the perpetrator or accomplice is a registrar, an agent of land services, a magistrate, a lawyer, a ministerial officer.

The imprisonment penalty is from two years to ten years and the fine from one million (1,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, if the perpetrator or accomplice is a member of government, a deputy, a municipal councillor, a mayor, a prefect, a high commissioner, a governor.

The attempt of stellionate is punishable.

##### CHAPITRE 2 : DESTRUCTIONS, DEGRADATIONS AND DAMAGE

###### Section 1 : Destructions, degradations and damage other than by an

incendiary means

###### Article 622-1 :

Is punished by an imprisonment penalty of three months to two years and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, whoever voluntarily destroys or seriously deteriorates movable property or immovable property belonging to another.

The imprisonment penalty is from one year to ten years and the fine from one million (1,000,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, when the destruction or deterioration is committed with breaking and entering.

The same applies:

˗ when the offense is committed against a magistrate or counsel, with a view to influencing their behavior in the exercise or on the occasion of the exercise of their duties;

˗ when the offense is committed against a witness, a victim or any other person, either with a view to inducing them not to report the facts, not to file a complaint, not to make a deposition or to make a false deposition, or by reason of the report, the complaint or the deposition.

###### Section 2 : Destructions, degradations and damage by use of an

explosive substance or an incendiary means

###### Article 622-2 :

Is punished by an imprisonment penalty of five years to ten years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, whoever voluntarily destroys or deteriorates movable property or immovable property belonging to another by the effect of an explosive or incendiary substance, or a fire or any other means likely to create a danger to the safety of persons.

The imprisonment penalty is from eleven years to twenty-one years and the fine from one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, if the offense is committed by an organized gang or under the conditions of paragraph 3 of article 622-1 above.

The penalty is life imprisonment, when the destruction or deterioration has resulted in the death of a person or an infirmity.

###### Article 622-3 :

Is punished by life imprisonment, whoever voluntarily destroys or deteriorates a dwelling house, a railway car or a vehicle sheltering or containing persons, when the offense is committed by the effect of an explosive or incendiary substance, a fire or any other means likely to create a danger to the safety of persons and it has resulted in the death of a person.

###### Article 622-4 :

Is punished by an imprisonment penalty of one year to ten years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, whoever voluntarily sets fire to the bush, the forest, woods, coppice or standing or cut crops.

###### Article 622-5 :

The threat to set fire to a dwelling or any other property is punished by an imprisonment penalty of two years to ten years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 622-6 :

Whoever voluntarily burns or destroys in any manner registers, minutes or original acts of public authority, titles, notes, bills of exchange, commercial or bank instruments containing or operating obligations, dispositions or discharge or knowingly destroys, removes, conceals, hides or alters a public or private document likely to facilitate the investigation of crimes or offenses, the discovery of evidence or the punishment of their perpetrator, is punished as follows, unless the facts constitute a more serious offense:

˗ an imprisonment penalty of five years to ten years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) francs to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, if the destroyed documents are acts of public authority or commercial or bank instruments;

˗ an imprisonment penalty of two years to five years and a fine of six hundred thousand (600,000) francs to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs, if it concerns any other document.

###### Section 3 : Pillage and devastation

###### Article 622-7 :

Is punished by an imprisonment penalty of five years to ten years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, any pillage, any damage to foodstuffs or goods, effects, movable property committed in assembly or gang and by open force.

###### Article 622-8 :

Is punished by an imprisonment penalty of two years to ten years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, whoever devastates standing crops or plants grown naturally or by hand of man.

###### Article 622-9 :

Is punished by an imprisonment penalty of eleven years to twenty-one years and a fine of one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, whoever commits the offenses of pillage provided for in article 622-7 above when they are committed in time of war.

Is punished by the same penalty any theft committed in a premises or in any building abandoned by its occupants, even momentarily or partially destroyed, as a result of war events.

###### Section 4 : Offenses against trees, crops and animals

###### Article 622-10 :

Is punished by an imprisonment penalty of two months to six months and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, whoever fells without prior authorization one or more trees in the public domain.

The same penalties are applicable to whoever mutilates, cuts or strips the bark of a tree in such a way as to cause it to die or destroys its grafts.

###### Article 622-11 :

The imprisonment penalty is from six months to one year and the fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, if the trees were planted in public squares, roads, paths, streets or ways.

###### Article 622-12 :

Is punished by an imprisonment penalty of two months to six months and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, whoever cuts grain or fodder that they knew belonged to another.

The imprisonment is from six months to one year and the fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs if green grain has been cut.

###### Article 622-13 :

Is punished by an imprisonment penalty of one year to ten years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, whoever poisons domestic animals, tamed or kept in captivity or fish in ponds, lakes, rivers, fishponds or reservoirs.

###### Article 622-14 :

Anyone who kills without necessity one of the animals mentioned in Article 622-13 above is punished:

˗ by imprisonment from two months to six months and a fine from two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, if the offense was committed in the buildings, enclosures and dependencies or on the land of which the owner of the killed animal was the owner, tenant or leaseholder;

˗ by imprisonment from two months to four months and a fine from two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, if it was committed in the places of which the offender was the owner, tenant or leaseholder;

˗ by imprisonment from two months to three months and a fine from two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, if it was committed in any other place.

The maximum penalty is always pronounced in case of violation of enclosure.

## BOOK VII: OFFENSES IN COMPUTER MATTERS AND BY MEANS OF

INFORMATION AND COMMUNICATION

TECHNOLOGIES

###### Article 700-1 :

For the purposes of this book, the following are understood as:

˗ computer system: any isolated device or set of interconnected or related devices, which ensures or of which one or more elements ensure, in execution of a program, automated data processing;

˗ electronic communication: any transmission to the public or to a category of public, by an electronic or magnetic communication process, of signs, signals, writings, images, sounds or messages of any nature;

˗ computer data: any representation of facts, information or concepts in a form suitable for computer processing, including a program capable of causing a computer system to execute a function;

˗ subscriber data: any information, contained in the form of computer data or in any other form, held by a service provider and relating to subscribers to its services, other than traffic or content data, and allowing to establish:

 the type of communication service used, the technical arrangements made in this regard and the service period;

 the identity, postal or geographical address and telephone number of the subscriber, and any other access number, billing and payment data, available on the basis of a contract or service arrangement;

 any other information relating to the location of communication equipment, available on the basis of a contract or service arrangement.

˗ traffic data: all data relating to a communication passing through a computer system, produced by the latter as part of the communication chain, indicating the origin, destination, route, time, date, size and duration of the communication or the type of underlying service.

˗ service provider: any public or private entity that offers its service users the possibility of communicating by means of a computer system; any other entity processing or storing computer data for this communication service or its users;

˗ information and communication technologies (ICT): technologies used to collect, store, use and transmit information as well as those involving the use of computers or any communication system including telecommunications.

#### TITLE I: OFFENSES IN COMPUTER MATTERS

##### CHAPTER 1: OFFENSES RELATING TO COMPUTER SYSTEMS AND

COMPUTER DATA

###### Article 711-1 :

Is punished by imprisonment from two months to two years and a fine from two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, anyone who intentionally and without right accesses all or part of a computer system.

When it results in either the deletion, modification or alteration of the data contained in the system or an alteration of the functioning of this system, the imprisonment is from one year to five years and the fine from one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 711-2 :

Is punished by imprisonment from two months to two years and a fine from two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) francs, anyone who intentionally and without right remains in all or part of a computer system.

When it results in either the deletion, modification or alteration of the data contained in the system or an alteration of the functioning of this system, the imprisonment is from two months to three years and the fine from six hundred thousand (600,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 711-3 :

Is punished by imprisonment from three months to five years and a fine from two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs, anyone who intentionally and without right, hinders or distorts the functioning of a computer system, by the introduction, transmission, damage, erasure, deterioration, alteration and deletion of computer data.

###### Article 711-4 :

Is punished by imprisonment from three months to five years and a fine from two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs, anyone who intentionally and without right, directly or indirectly introduces computer data into a computer system.

###### Article 711-5 :

Is punished by imprisonment from one year to five years and a fine from five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who intentionally and without right, intercepts, by technical means, computer data, during non-public transmissions, to, from or within a computer system, including electromagnetic emissions from a computer system carrying such computer data.

###### Article 711-6 :

Is punished by imprisonment from one year to ten years and a fine from two million (2,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who intentionally and without right, damages, erases, deteriorates, alters, modifies, or deletes computer data.

###### Article 711-7 :

Is punished by imprisonment from one year to ten years and a fine from one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who intentionally and without right, introduces, alters, modifies, erases or deletes computer data, generating non-authentic data, with the intention that they be taken into account or used for legal purposes as if they were authentic, whether or not they are directly readable and intelligible.

###### Article 711-8 :

Is punished by the same penalties anyone who, intentionally and without right, makes use of data obtained under the conditions provided for in Article 711-7 above.

###### Article 711-9 :

Is punished by imprisonment from two years to ten years and a fine from two million (2,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who, intentionally and without right, causes patrimonial damage to another by the introduction, alteration, modification, erasure or deletion of computer data or by any form of interference with the functioning of a computer system, with the fraudulent or criminal intention to obtain without right an economic benefit for oneself or for another.

###### Article 711-10 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from two years to ten years and a fine from two million (2,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, whoever, intentionally and without right, produces, sells, obtains for use, imports, disseminates or makes available in any form:

˗ a device, including a computer program, primarily designed or adapted to allow the commission of one of the offenses provided for by Articles 711-1 to 711-6 above.

˗ a password, an access code or similar computer data allowing access to all or part of a computer system with the intention that they be used to commit one or another of the offenses provided for by Articles 711-1 to 711-6 above.

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from two years to ten years and a fine from two million (2,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, whoever, intentionally and without right, possesses a device, password, access code or similar data referred to in the first paragraph of this article with the intention that it be used to commit one or another of the offenses referred to by Articles 711-1 to 711-6 above.

The offenses provided for by this article are not established when the production, sale, obtaining for use, importation, dissemination or other forms of making available is not for the purpose of committing an offense, as in the case of authorized testing or protection of a computer system.

###### Article 711-11 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from eleven years to twenty-one years and a fine from ten million (10,000,000) to fifty million (50,000,000) CFA francs, whoever, intentionally and without right, participates in a formed association or an established agreement with a view to preparing or committing one or more offenses provided for by this title.

###### Article 711-12 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from one year to five years and a fine from one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) francs

CFA, whoever, intentionally and without right, assumes the digital identity of a third party or makes use of one or more data of any nature allowing their identification, with a view to disturbing their tranquility, harming their honor, their private life, their property or that of a third party.

###### Article 711-13 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from one year to ten years and a fine from one million (1,000,000) to four million (4,000,000) CFA francs, whoever, intentionally and without right, reproduces, extracts or intentionally copies computer data belonging to others.

##### CHAPITRE 2 : FAILURES TO COMPLY WITH LEGAL OBLIGATIONS IN THE

IMPLEMENTATION AND USE OF AN AUTOMATED

PERSONAL DATA PROCESSING SYSTEM

###### Article 712-1 :

Constitutes personal data any information which allows, in any form whatsoever, directly or indirectly, the identification of natural persons, in particular by reference to an identification number or to one or more elements specific to their physical, physiological, genetic, mental, cultural, social or economic identity.

###### Article 712-2 :

Is referred to as personal data processing any operation or set of operations performed using automated or non-automated processes by a natural or legal person, and applied to personal data, such as collection, exploitation, recording, organization, storage, adaptation, modification, extraction, backup, copying, consultation, use, communication by transmission, dissemination or any other form of making available, matching or interconnection, as well as locking, encryption, erasure or destruction of personal data.

###### Article 712-3 :

The data controller is the natural or legal person, public or private, who has the power to decide on the creation of personal data.

The recipient of personal data processing is any natural or legal person, public or private, other than the data subject, authorized to receive communication of such data.

The data subject is the identifiable person to whom the personal data relates.

###### Article 712-4 :

The act of carrying out or having carried out automated processing of nominative information without complying with the formalities prior to their implementation provided for by law is punished by a term of imprisonment from three months to five years and a fine from five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 712-5 :

When this information is required by law, is punished by a term of imprisonment from one month to two years and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs the act, for the controller of automated personal data processing: ˗ of failing to inform the person from whom personal data concerning them is collected:

 of the identity of the data controller and, where applicable, that of their representative;

 of the purpose pursued by the processing for which the data is intended;

 of the mandatory or optional nature of the responses;

 of the possible consequences for them of failure to respond;  of the recipients or categories of recipients of the data;

 of their rights of opposition, inquiry, access and rectification;

 where applicable, of transfers of personal data envisaged to a foreign State;

˗ when the data is collected by questionnaire, of failing to include on the questionnaire the information relating to:

 the identity of the data controller and, where applicable, that of their representative;

 the purpose pursued by the processing for which the data is intended;

 the mandatory or optional nature of the responses;

 the rights of opposition, inquiry, access and rectification of the persons from whom the data is collected;

˗ of failing to inform in a clear and precise manner any user of electronic communication networks:

 of the purpose of any action aimed at accessing, by electronic transmission, information stored in their connection terminal equipment or at recording, by the same means, information in their connection terminal equipment;

 of the means available to them to oppose it;

˗ of failing to provide to the data subject, when personal data has not been collected from them, the information listed in items 1 and 2 from the time the data is recorded or, if communication of the data to third parties is envisaged, at the latest at the time of the first communication of the data.

###### Article 712-6 :

Is punished by a term of imprisonment from one year to five years and a fine from one million (1,000,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, the act of communicating to unauthorized third parties or accessing without authorization or illegally the personal data of others.

###### Article 712-7 :

Is punished by the penalties provided for the offense itself or for the most severely punished offense, whoever participates in a formed association or an established agreement with a view to the preparation, materialized by one or more material facts, of one or more offenses provided for by the articles above of this chapter.

###### Article 712-8 :

The act of carrying out or having carried out automated processing of personal information without taking all useful precautions to preserve the security of said information, in particular to prevent it from being distorted, damaged or communicated to unauthorized third parties, is punishable by a term of imprisonment from three months to five years and a fine from five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 712-9 :

Is punishable by a term of imprisonment from three months to five years and a fine from five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, the diversion of purpose of a collection or processing of personal data.

###### Article 712-10 :

The act of collecting data by fraudulent, unfair or unlawful means, or of carrying out processing of personal information concerning a natural person despite their opposition, when such opposition is based on legitimate reasons, is punishable by a term of imprisonment from three months to five years and a fine from two million (2,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

In the case of automated processing of personal data for the purpose of research in the field of health, the act of carrying out data processing is punishable by the same penalties:

˗ without having previously informed the persons concerned individually of their right of access, rectification and opposition, the nature of the information transmitted and the recipients of the data;

˗ despite the opposition of the person concerned or, where provided by law, in the absence of the informed and express consent of the person, or, in the case of a deceased person, despite the refusal expressed by that person during their lifetime.

###### Article 712-11 :

Except in cases provided by law, the act of storing or keeping in computerized memory, without the express consent of the person concerned, personal data which, directly or indirectly, reveals the racial or ethnic origins or political, philosophical or religious opinions or trade union memberships or morals of persons, is punishable by a term of imprisonment from three months to five years and a fine from five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

Is punishable by the same penalties the act, except in cases provided by law, of storing or keeping in computerized memory personal information concerning offences, convictions or security measures.

###### Article 712-12 :

The act, without the consent of the Commission on Information Technology and Freedoms, of keeping information in personal form beyond the period specified in the request for an opinion or in the declaration prior to the implementation of computerized processing, is punishable by a term of imprisonment from three months to five years and a fine from five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 712-13 :

The act, by any person who has collected, during their recording, classification, transmission or another form of processing, personal information the disclosure of which would have the effect of damaging the honor and reputation of the person concerned or the privacy of their private life, of bringing, without the authorization of the person concerned, such information to the knowledge of a third party who is not qualified to receive it, is punishable by a term of imprisonment from three months to five years and a fine from one million (1,000,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs.

The disclosure provided for in paragraph 1 is punishable by a term of imprisonment from three months to five years and a fine from five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs when it was committed through imprudence or negligence.

In the cases provided for in the two preceding paragraphs, prosecution may only be brought upon complaint of the victim, their legal representative or their beneficiaries.

###### Article 712-14 :

Is punishable by a term of imprisonment from one month to one year and a fine from two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, the act of obstructing the action of the Commission either:

˗ by opposing on-site verifications;

˗ by refusing to communicate to its members or agents, the information and documents useful to the mission entrusted to them or by concealing or causing the disappearance of said documents;

˗ by communicating information that does not conform to the content of the records at the time the request was made or that does not present them in a directly intelligible form.

###### Article 712-15 :

The provisions of Articles 712-6 and 712-8 to 712-13 apply to non-automated or mechanographic files whose use does not fall exclusively within the exercise of the right to privacy.

#### TITLE II: OFFENCES COMMITTED BY MEANS OF INFORMATION

AND COMMUNICATION TECHNOLOGIES

##### CHAPTER 1: OFFENCES BY MEANS OF INFORMATION AND

COMMUNICATION TECHNOLOGIES

###### Article 721-1 :

Is punishable by a term of imprisonment from one to ten years and a fine from one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who, participating in the execution of an electronic communications service, violates the secrecy of correspondence or who, without the authorization of the operator or the recipient, discloses to a third party not authorized by law, publishes or uses the content of said correspondence.

###### Article 721-2 :

Is punishable by a term of imprisonment from one to ten years and a fine from five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million five hundred thousand (2,500,000) CFA francs, anyone who knowingly uses services obtained by means of the offence referred to in Article 721-1 above.

###### Article 721-3 :

Is punishable by a term of imprisonment from one to ten years and a fine from five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million five hundred thousand (2,500,000) CFA francs, anyone who fraudulently uses, for personal purposes or otherwise, a network or electronic communications service open to the public or connects by any means to a private line.

###### Article 721-4 :

Is punished by imprisonment from one year to ten years and a fine from ten million (10,000,000) to fifty million (50,000,000) CFA francs, whoever:

˗ establishes or causes to be established a network open to the public or an independent network, provides or causes to be provided an electronic communications service in violation of the law or maintains it in violation of a suspension or withdrawal decision;

˗ performs or causes to be performed a service subject to individual license or general authorization, without authorization or without license or in violation of a decision to suspend or withdraw the license or authorization provided for this purpose by law;

˗ performs or causes to be performed a service subject to approval, without approval or in violation of a decision to suspend or withdraw the approval provided for this purpose;

˗ uses a frequency that has not been previously assigned to them by the regulatory authority;

˗ fraudulently uses "call-back" or means of bypassing electronic communications networks open to the public for commercial or personal purposes.

Bypassing within the meaning of the preceding paragraph covers any arrangement or installation allowing a user to access long-distance, international or other services, without using the networks of local operators and thus avoiding the payment of access charges and, more generally, any means allowing a user not to use the service or facilities of operators holding an authorization.

Call-back means the procedure for establishing telephone communications between correspondents, by which the person requesting the communication is called back by their correspondent.

The court seised may, at the request of the regulatory authority, order the confiscation of installations, apparatus or means of transmission used without authorization or their destruction at the expense of the offender.

###### Article 721-5 :

Is punished by imprisonment from one year to ten years and a fine from one million (1,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, any person who knowingly transmits or broadcasts false or misleading radioelectric signals or distress and safety calls.

###### Article 721-6 :

Is punished by imprisonment from one year to ten years and a fine from one million (1,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, any person who carries out radioelectric transmissions knowingly using a call sign from the international series assigned to a State station or a private station.

###### Article 721-7 :

Is punished by imprisonment from one year to ten years and a fine from five million (5,000,000) to twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs, whoever intentionally causes, by any means, the interruption of electronic communications.

When the interruption of electronic communications occurs following an act committed intentionally but without intent to interrupt electronic communications, the perpetrator of the act is punished by imprisonment from three months to one year and a fine from one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 721-8 :

Is punished by imprisonment from eleven years to twenty-one years and a fine from fifty million (50,000,000) to one hundred million (100,000,000) CFA francs, whoever knowingly commits an act of theft or vandalism on electronic communications infrastructure.

###### Article 721-9 :

Are punished by imprisonment from three months to one year and a fine from one million (1,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, offenses relating to private or administrative easements.

###### Article 721-10 :

Attempt to commit the offenses provided for by this title is punishable.

##### CHAPITRE 2 : DES PEINES COMPLEMENTAIRES

###### Article 722-1 :

In case of conviction for an offense committed through an electronic means of communication, the competent court may order the confiscation of materials, equipment, instruments, computer programs or data resulting from the offense.

The court may also order as additional penalties the prohibition from sending electronic communication messages, the prohibition on a provisional or permanent basis from accessing the site used to commit the offense or the prohibition from hosting the site by all available technical means.

The judge may enjoin any person legally responsible for the site used to commit the offense, any qualified person to implement the necessary technical means to ensure the prohibition of access to or hosting of the incriminated site.

The violation of the pronounced prohibitions is punished by imprisonment from one to three years and a fine from five hundred thousand (500,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs.

In case of conviction for an offense committed through an electronic means of communication, the judge orders as an additional measure the publication at the convicted person's expense, by extract, of the decision on the same electronic means of communication.

The publication provided for in the preceding paragraph is carried out within fifteen days following the day on which the conviction becomes final.

The convicted person who has not published or caused to be published the extract of the conviction decision within the time period set in the preceding paragraph, is punished by a fine from five hundred thousand (500,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs.

## LIVRE VIII : DES INFRACTIONS PREVUES PAR LES ACTES UNIFORMES DE

L'ORGANISATION POUR L'HARMONISATION EN AFRIQUE DU

DROIT DES AFFAIRES (OHADA)

#### TITRE I : DES DISPOSITIONS GENERALES ET PEINES APPLICABLES

##### CHAPITRE 1 : DES DISPOSITIONS GENERALES

###### Article 811-1 :

This title sets the penalties applicable to offenses provided for within the framework of the Organization for the Harmonization of Business Law in Africa (OHADA) by the Uniform Acts:

˗ on general commercial law;

˗ on the organization of security interests;

˗ relating to commercial companies and economic interest groups law;

˗ on the organization of collective procedures for clearing liabilities; ˗ on the organization of simplified recovery procedures and methods of execution;

˗ on the organization and harmonization of business accounting; ˗ relating to cooperative societies law.

##### CHAPITRE 2 : DES PEINES APPLICABLES

###### Section 1 : Des peines applicables aux infractions prévues par l'acte

uniforme relatif au droit commercial général

###### Article 812-1 :

In application of Article 69 of the Uniform Act on general commercial law, is punished by imprisonment from one to three years and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs, any person required to complete one of the formalities prescribed in the Uniform Act and who fails to do so, or who carries out a formality by fraud.

If necessary, the court pronouncing the conviction orders the registration or correction of inaccurate entries.

###### Article 812-2 :

In application of Article 140 of the Uniform Act on general commercial law, is punished by the penalties provided for in Article 812-1 above the lessee-manager who does not indicate at the head of their order forms, invoices and other documents of a financial or commercial nature, along with their registration number in the trade and movable credit register, their capacity as lessee-manager of the business.

###### Section 2 : Des peines applicables aux infractions prévues par l'acte

uniforme portant organisation des sûretés

###### Article 812-3 :

In application of article 65 of the Uniform Act organizing security interests, is punished by imprisonment of three months to three years and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to one million

(1,000,000) CFA francs, any person who registers a movable security interest either by fraud, or by providing inaccurate information or information given in bad faith.

If applicable, the court that pronounces the conviction orders the registration or rectification of inaccurate entries.

###### Article 812-4 :

In application of article 184 of the Uniform Act organizing security interests, is punished by imprisonment of three months to one year and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, the lessee or any person who, by fraudulent maneuvers, totally or partially deprives the lessor of his privilege.

###### Section 3 : Penalties applicable to offenses provided for by the uniform

act relating to the law of commercial companies and economic interest groups

###### Paragraph 1 : Penalties applicable to offenses relating to the

incorporation of companies

###### Article 812-5 :

In application of article 886 of the Uniform Act relating to the law of commercial companies and economic interest groups, are punished by imprisonment of three months to three years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, the founders, the chairman and chief executive officer, the chief executive officer, the general manager or the deputy general manager of a public limited company, who issue shares before registration or at any time, when registration is obtained by fraud or when the company is irregularly incorporated.

###### Article 812-6 :

In application of article 887 of the Uniform Act relating to the law of commercial companies and economic interest groups, are punished by imprisonment of three months to three years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, those who:

˗ knowingly, by establishing the notarized declaration of subscription and payment or the certificate of the depositary, will have affirmed as sincere and true subscriptions that they knew to be fictitious or will have declared that funds which have not been definitively placed at the disposal of the company have been effectively paid;

˗ will have delivered to the notary or to the depositary, a list of shareholders or subscription and payment forms mentioning fictitious subscriptions or payments of funds which have not been definitively placed at the disposal of the company;

˗ knowingly, by simulating subscription or payment or by publishing payments that do not exist or any other false facts, will have obtained or attempted to obtain subscriptions or payments;

˗ knowingly, to induce subscriptions or payments, will have published the names of persons designated contrary to the truth as being or to be attached to the company in any capacity whatsoever;

˗ fraudulently, will have caused an in-kind contribution to be assigned a valuation higher than its real value.

###### Article 812-7 :

In application of article 888 of the Uniform Act relating to the law of commercial companies and economic interest groups, are punished by imprisonment of three months to three years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, those who knowingly negotiate:

˗ registered shares which have not remained in registered form until their full payment;

˗ shares for contributions in kind before the expiration of the period during which they are not negotiable;

˗ cash shares for which the payment of one quarter of the nominal value has not been made.

###### Paragraph 2 : Penalties applicable to offenses relating to the

management, administration and direction of companies

###### Article 812-8 :

In application of article 889 of the Uniform Act relating to the law of commercial companies and economic interest groups, are punished by imprisonment of one to five years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, the corporate officers who, in the absence of inventory or by means of fraudulent inventories, knowingly distribute fictitious dividends among the shareholders or partners.

###### Article 812-9 :

In application of article 890 of the Uniform Act relating to the law of commercial companies and economic interest groups, are punished by imprisonment of one to five years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, the corporate officers who knowingly, even in the absence of any distribution of dividends, publish or present to the shareholders or partners, with a view to concealing the true situation of the company, summary financial statements that do not give, for each financial year, a true and fair view of the operations of the financial year, of the financial position and of that of the company's assets, at the expiration of this period.

###### Article 812-10 :

In application of article 891 of the Uniform Act relating to the law of commercial companies and economic interest groups, are punished by imprisonment of one to five years and a fine of two million

(2,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, the manager of the limited liability company, the directors, the chairman and chief executive officer, the general manager or the deputy general manager who, in bad faith, make use of the company's assets or credit in a way they know to be contrary to the interest of the company, for personal purposes, material or moral, or to favor another legal entity in which they have an interest, directly or indirectly.

###### Paragraph 3 : Penalties applicable to offenses relating to

general meetings

###### Article 812-11 :

In application of article 892 of the Uniform Act relating to the law of commercial companies and economic interest groups, are punished by imprisonment of three months to three years and a fine of two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, those who, knowingly, prevent a shareholder or partner from participating in a general meeting.

###### Paragraph 4 : Penalties applicable to offenses relating to

modifications of the capital of public limited companies

###### Article 812-12 :

In application of Article 893 of the Uniform Act relating to commercial company law and economic interest groups, shall be punished by imprisonment from three months to three years and a fine from one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, the directors, the chairman of the board of directors, the chairman and chief executive officer, the chief executive officer, the chief administrator or the deputy chief administrator of a public limited company who, during a capital increase, issue shares or share certificates:

˗ before the depositary's certificate is established;

˗ without the formalities prior to the capital increase being properly completed;

˗ without the company's previously subscribed capital being fully paid up;

˗ without the new contribution shares being fully paid up before the amending registration in the trade and personal property credit register;

˗ without the new shares being paid up to at least one quarter of their nominal value at the time of subscription;

˗ where applicable, without the entire share premium being paid up at the time of subscription.

Shall be punished by the same penalties, the persons referred to in this article who do not maintain cash shares in registered form until they are fully paid up.

###### Article 812-13 :

In application of Article 894 of the Uniform Act relating to commercial company law and economic interest groups, shall be punished by imprisonment from three months to three years and a fine from one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, the company directors who, during a capital increase:

˗ do not grant shareholders, in proportion to the amount of their shares, a preferential right to subscribe for cash shares when this right has not been removed by the general meeting and the shareholders have not waived it;

˗ do not reserve for shareholders a period of at least twenty days from the opening of the subscription, except when this period is closed early;

˗ do not allocate the shares made available, for lack of sufficient subscriptions on an irreducible basis, to shareholders who have subscribed on a reducible basis for a number of shares greater than that which they could subscribe for on an irreducible basis, in proportion to the rights they hold;

˗ do not reserve the rights of subscription warrant holders.

###### Article 812-14 :

In application of Article 895 of the Uniform Act relating to commercial company law and economic interest groups, shall be punished by imprisonment from three months to three years and a fine from one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, the company directors who, knowingly, give or confirm inaccurate information in the reports presented to the general meeting called to decide on the removal of the preferential subscription right.

###### Article 812-15 :

In application of Article 896 of the Uniform Act relating to commercial company law and economic interest groups, shall be punished by imprisonment from three months to three years and a fine from two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, the directors, the chairman and chief executive officer, the chief executive officer, the deputy chief administrator who, knowingly, carry out a capital reduction:

˗ without respecting the equality of shareholders;

˗ without communicating the capital reduction plan to the statutory auditors forty-five days before the holding of the general meeting called to rule on the capital reduction.

###### Paragraphe 5 : Penalties applicable to offenses relating to the

control of companies

###### Article 812-16 :

In application of Article 897 of the Uniform Act relating to commercial company law and economic interest groups, shall be punished by imprisonment from one year to five years and a fine from one million

(1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, the company directors who do not initiate the appointment of the company's statutory auditors or do not summon them to general meetings.

###### Article 812-17 :

In application of Article 898 of the Uniform Act relating to commercial company law and economic interest groups, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to five years and a fine from one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, any person who, either in their own name or as a partner in a statutory auditors' firm, knowingly accepts, exercises or retains the functions of statutory auditor, notwithstanding legal incompatibilities.

###### Article 812-18 :

In application of Article 899 of the Uniform Act relating to commercial company law and economic interest groups, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to five years and a fine from two million

(2,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, any statutory auditor who knowingly, either in their own name or as a partner in a statutory auditors' firm, gives or confirms false information about the situation of the company or who does not report to the public prosecutor's office criminal acts of which they are aware.

###### Article 812-19 :

In application of Article 900 of the Uniform Act relating to commercial company law and economic interest groups, shall be punished by imprisonment from one year to five years and a fine from two million

(2,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, the company directors or any person in the service of the company who, knowingly, obstruct the verifications or control of the statutory auditors or who refuse to communicate, on site, all documents useful for the exercise of their mission and in particular all contracts, books, accounting documents and minutes registers.

###### Paragraphe 6 : Penalties applicable to offenses relating to the

dissolution of companies

###### Article 812-20 :

In application of Article 901 of the Uniform Act relating to commercial company law and economic interest groups, shall be punished by imprisonment from one year to five years and a fine from two million

(2,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, the company directors who, knowingly, when the company's equity becomes less than half of the share capital due to losses recorded in the summary financial statements:

˗ do not convene, within four months following the approval of the financial statements showing these losses, the extraordinary general meeting for the purpose of deciding, if necessary, on the early dissolution of the company;

˗ do not file with the registry of the court in charge of commercial matters, do not register in the trade and personal property credit register and do not publish, in a newspaper authorized to receive legal notices, the early dissolution of the company.

###### Paragraphe 7 : Penalties applicable to offenses relating to the

liquidation of companies

###### Article 812-21 :

In application of Article 902 of the Uniform Act on Commercial Companies and Economic Interest Groups, shall be punished by imprisonment of three months to three years and a fine of one million (1 000 000) to five million (5 000 000) CFA francs, the liquidator of a company who, knowingly:
