˗ within one month from his appointment, does not publish in a newspaper authorized to receive legal notices at the location of the registered office, the act appointing him liquidator and does not file with the trade and movable credit register the decisions ordering the dissolution;

˗ does not convene the partners, at the end of the liquidation, to rule on the final account of the liquidation, on the approval of his management and the discharge of his mandate and to note the closure of the liquidation;

˗ in the case provided for by Article 219 of the Uniform Act on Commercial Companies and Economic Interest Groups, does not file his final accounts with the registry of the court responsible for commercial matters at the location of the registered office, nor request judicial approval thereof.

###### Article 812-22 :

In application of Article 903 of the Uniform Act on Commercial Companies and Economic Interest Groups, shall be punished by imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of one million

(1 000 000) to five million (5 000 000) CFA francs, when the liquidation occurs by court decision, the liquidator who, knowingly:

‑ within six months of his appointment, does not present a report on the assets and liabilities of the company in liquidation, and on the continuation of liquidation operations, nor request the necessary authorizations to complete them;

‑ within three months of the closing of each financial year, does not prepare the summary financial statements in view of the inventory and a written report in which he accounts for the liquidation operations during the past financial year;

‑ does not allow the partners to exercise, during the liquidation period, their right to access corporate documents under the same conditions as previously;

‑ does not convene the partners, at least once a year, to report to them on the summary financial statements in case of continuation of the company's business;

‑ does not deposit in an account opened in a bank in the name of the company in liquidation, within fifteen days from the distribution decision, the sums allocated to distributions among the partners and creditors;

‑ does not deposit, in a deposit account opened in the records of the Treasury, within one year from the closure of the liquidation, the sums attributed to creditors or partners and not claimed by them.

###### Article 812-23 :

In application of Article 904 of the Uniform Act on Commercial Companies and Economic Interest Groups, shall be punished by imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of two million

(2 000 000) to ten million (10 000 000) CFA francs, the liquidator who, in bad faith:

˗ makes use of the assets or credit of the company in liquidation in a manner that he knows to be contrary to its interest, for personal purposes or to favor another legal entity in which he has an interest, directly or indirectly;

˗ transfers all or part of the assets of the company in liquidation to a person who held in the company the capacity of general partner, limited partner, manager, member of the board of directors, general administrator or auditor, without having obtained the unanimous consent of the partners or, failing that, the authorization of the competent jurisdiction.

###### Paragraphe 8 : Des peines applicables aux infractions en cas d'appel

public à l'épargne

###### Article 812-24 :

In application of Article 905 of the Uniform Act on Commercial Companies and Economic Interest Groups, shall be punished by imprisonment of three months to three years and a fine of one million (1 000 000) to five million (5 000 000) CFA francs, the presidents, administrators or general directors of companies who issue securities offered to the public:

˗ without inserting a notice in a newspaper authorized to receive legal notices, prior to any publicity measure;

˗ without the prospectuses and circulars reproducing the statements of the notice provided for in the paragraph above and containing the mention of the insertion of this notice in the newspaper authorized to receive legal notices with reference to the issue in which it is published;

˗ without the posters and advertisements in newspapers reproducing the same statements or at least an extract of these statements with reference to said notice, and indications of the issue number of the newspaper authorized to receive legal notices in which it is published;

˗ without the posters, prospectuses and circulars mentioning the signature of the person or representative of the company from whom the offer emanates and specifying whether the securities offered are listed or not and, if so, on which stock exchange.

Shall be punished by the penalties provided for in paragraph 1, persons who serve as intermediaries on the occasion of the transfer of securities, without the requirements set forth in this article having been complied with.

###### Section 4 : Des peines applicables aux infractions prévues par l'acte

uniforme portant organisation des procédures collectives d'apurement du passif

###### Paragraphe 1 : Des peines applicables à la banqueroute simple et à la

banqueroute frauduleuse

###### Article 812-25 :

In application of Article 228 of the Uniform Act on the Organization of Collective Proceedings for Clearing Liabilities, is guilty of simple bankruptcy and punished by imprisonment of three months to three years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250 000) to one million (1 000 000) CFA francs, any natural person in a state of cessation of payments who: ˗ contracts, without receiving values in exchange, commitments deemed too significant in view of his situation when he contracts them;

˗ with the intention of delaying the finding of cessation of payments, makes purchases with a view to resale below the market price or, with the same intention, employs ruinous means to obtain funds;

˗ without legitimate excuse, does not make at the registry of the competent jurisdiction, the declaration of his state of cessation of payments within thirty days;

˗ keeps incomplete or irregular accounts or does not keep them in accordance with accounting rules and recognized professional practices having regard to the size of the business;

˗ having been declared twice in a state of cessation of payments within a period of five years, these proceedings are closed for insufficiency of assets.

###### Article 812-26:

Pursuant to Article 229 of the Uniform Act on collective insolvency proceedings, any natural person who, in the event of cessation of payments, is guilty of fraudulent bankruptcy and punished by a term of imprisonment of one to five years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs:

˗ conceals their accounting records;

˗ misappropriates or dissipates all or part of their assets;

˗ fraudulently acknowledges being a debtor of sums they do not owe, either in their books, or by published acts or private written commitments, or in their balance sheet;

˗ engages in commercial business contrary to a prohibition provided for by the Uniform Acts or by law;

˗ after cessation of payments, pays a creditor to the detriment of the collective body;

˗ stipulates with a creditor special advantages by reason of their vote in the deliberations of the collective body, or makes with a creditor a special agreement from which results for the latter an advantage at the expense of the debtor's assets as from the date of the opening decision.

Any natural person who, on the occasion of a judicial settlement procedure, is punished by the penalties provided for fraudulent bankruptcy: ˗ in bad faith, presents or causes to be presented an income statement, a balance sheet, a statement of debts or liabilities or a statement of assets and liabilities of privileges and securities, that is inaccurate or incomplete;

˗ without authorization from the president of the competent court, performs one of the acts prohibited by Article 11 of the Uniform Act on collective insolvency proceedings.

###### Article 812-27:

Pursuant to Article 227 of the Uniform Act on collective insolvency proceedings, the penalties provided for in Articles 812-25 and 812-26 above are applicable to partners of commercial companies who have the status of traders. :

###### Article 812-28:

Pursuant to Article 230 of the Uniform Act on collective insolvency proceedings, the penalties provided for in Articles 812-29, 812-30 and 812-31 below are applicable to natural persons who are directors of legal entities subject to collective proceedings and to their permanent representatives.

The directors referred to in this article mean all directors de jure or de facto and, in general, all persons who have directly or through an intermediary, administered, managed or liquidated the legal entity under cover or in place of its legal representatives.

###### Article 812-29:

Pursuant to Article 231 of the Uniform Act on collective insolvency proceedings, directors referred to in Article 812-28 above who, in that capacity and in bad faith, are punished by a term of imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs: ˗ consume sums belonging to the legal entity by engaging in purely speculative transactions or fictitious transactions;

˗ make purchases with a view to resale below market value or use ruinous means to obtain funds, with the intention of delaying the finding of cessation of payments of the legal entity;

˗ pay or cause to be paid a creditor to the detriment of the collective body after cessation of payments of the legal entity;

˗ cause the legal entity to contract on behalf of others, without receiving securities in exchange, commitments deemed too significant in view of its situation at the time those commitments were made; ˗ keep, cause to be kept or allow to be kept irregularly or incompletely the accounting records of the legal entity under the conditions provided for in Article 228-4 of the Uniform Act on collective insolvency proceedings;

˗ fail to file at the registry of the competent court, within thirty days, the declaration of the state of cessation of payments of the legal entity;

˗ misappropriate or conceal, or attempt to misappropriate or conceal part of their assets or fraudulently acknowledge being debtors of sums they do not owe, in order to shield all or part of their patrimony from the claims of the legal entity in a state of cessation of payments or from those of the partners or creditors of the legal entity.

###### Article 812-30:

Pursuant to Article 232 of the Uniform Act on collective insolvency proceedings, legal or de facto representatives of legal entities having partners who are indefinitely and jointly liable for the debts thereof, who without legitimate excuse fail to file at the registry of the competent court the declaration of the state of cessation of payments within thirty days or if such declaration does not include the list of jointly liable partners with indication of their names and addresses, are guilty of simple bankruptcy and punished by a term of imprisonment of three months to three years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Article 812-31:

Pursuant to Article 233 of the Uniform Act on collective insolvency proceedings, directors referred to in Article 812-28 who fraudulently are punished by a term of imprisonment of one to five years and a fine of two million

(2,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs:

˗ conceal the books of the legal entity;

˗ misappropriate or conceal part of its assets;

˗ acknowledge the legal entity as debtor of sums it does not owe, either in the books, or by public acts or commitments under private signature, or in the balance sheet;

˗ engage in the profession of director contrary to a prohibition provided for by the Uniform Acts or by law;

˗ stipulate with a creditor, in the name of the legal entity, special advantages by reason of their vote in the deliberations of the collective body or who conclude with a creditor a special agreement from which results for the latter an advantage at the expense of the assets of the legal entity, as from the date of the decision declaring cessation of payments. Directors referred to in Article 230 of the Uniform Act on collective insolvency proceedings who, on the occasion of a preventive settlement procedure, are punished by the penalties provided for in paragraph 1:

˗ in bad faith, present or cause to be presented an income statement, a balance sheet, a statement of debts or liabilities or a statement of assets and liabilities of privileges and securities, that is inaccurate or incomplete;

˗ without authorization from the president of the competent court, perform one of the acts prohibited by Article 11 of the Uniform Act on collective insolvency proceedings.

###### Article 812-32:

For the offenses provided for in this section, the court seized may order a prohibition from exercising the profession for a period that may not exceed five years.

###### Paragraphe 2: Penalties applicable to offenses similar to

bankruptcies

###### Article 812-33 :

In application of Article 240 of the Uniform Act on the organization of collective proceedings for the clearing of debts, are punished by imprisonment from one to five years and a fine from one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs:

˗ persons convicted of having, in the interest of the debtor, removed, concealed or hidden all or part of the movable or immovable property, without prejudice to the application of criminal provisions relating to complicity;

˗ persons convicted of having fraudulently submitted in the collective proceedings, either in their own name or through interposition or impersonation, fictitious claims;

˗ persons who, conducting business under another person's name or under a fictitious name, in bad faith, misappropriate, conceal, attempt to misappropriate or conceal part of their assets.

###### Article 812-34 :

In application of Article 241 of the Uniform Act on the organization of collective proceedings for the clearing of debts, are punished by imprisonment from six months to three years and a fine from two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to nine hundred thousand (900,000) CFA francs, the spouse, descendants, ascendants or collaterals of the debtor or his relatives by marriage up to the fourth degree who, without the debtor's knowledge, misappropriate, divert or conceal assets forming part of the debtor's estate in a state of cessation of payments, when the offenses are committed to the detriment of an incapacitated person.

###### Article 812-35 :

In application of Article 242 of the Uniform Act on the organization of collective proceedings for the clearing of debts, even if there would be acquittal in the cases provided for in Articles 240 and 241 of the said Act, the court seized rules on damages and on the reintegration, into the debtor's estate, of the property, rights or shares removed.

###### Article 812-36 :

In application of Article 243 of the Uniform Act on the organization of collective proceedings for the clearing of debts, is punished by imprisonment from one to five years and a fine from two million

(2,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, any trustee of a collective proceeding who:

˗ carries out a personal activity under cover of the debtor's business concealing his actions;

˗ disposes of the debtor's credit or assets as his own; ˗ dissipates the debtor's assets;

˗ abusively and in bad faith pursues, in his personal interest, either directly or indirectly, a loss-making operation of the debtor's business;

˗ acquires for his own account, directly or indirectly, the debtor's assets, in violation of Article 51 of the Uniform Act on the organization of collective proceedings for the clearing of debts.

###### Article 812-37 :

In application of Article 244 of the Uniform Act on the organization of collective proceedings for the clearing of debts, is punished by imprisonment from six months to three years and a fine from two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to nine hundred thousand (900,000) CFA francs, the creditor who:

˗ stipulates with the debtor or with any person, special advantages by reason of his vote in the deliberations of the body of creditors;

˗ makes a special agreement from which there results in his favor, an advantage at the expense of the debtor's assets from the day of the decision opening the collective proceeding.

###### Article 812-38 :

In application of the provisions of Article 245 of the Uniform Act on the organization of collective proceedings for the clearing of debts, the agreements provided for in Article 244 of the said Uniform Act are, moreover, declared null and void by the criminal court, with regard to all persons, including the debtor. The judgment further orders the creditor to return, to whom it may concern, the sums or values he received pursuant to the annulled agreement.

###### Article 812-39 :

In application of Article 246 of the Uniform Act on the organization of collective proceedings for the clearing of debts, without prejudice to the provisions relating to criminal records, all conviction decisions rendered by virtue of the provisions of this section are, at the expense of those convicted, posted and published in a newspaper authorized to receive legal notices as well as by summary extract, in the Official Gazette mentioning the number of the legal notices newspaper where the first insertion is published.

###### Section 5 : Des peines applicables aux infractions prévues par l'acte

uniforme portant organisation des procédures simplifiées de recouvrement et des voies d'exécution

###### Article 812-40 :

In application of Article 36 paragraph 1 of the Uniform Act on the organization of simplified recovery procedures and enforcement methods, is punished by imprisonment from one to five years and a fine from one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, the debtor seized or the third party holder in whose hands the seizure was effected who does not produce the seized objects when he is deemed custodian thereof.

###### Article 812-41 :

In application of Articles 64 paragraph 1-6e, 100-6e and 109-7e of the Uniform Act on the organization of simplified recovery procedures and enforcement methods, is punished by imprisonment from one to five years and a fine from one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, the debtor or designated third party who, except in the cases provided for in Article 97 of the Uniform Act above mentioned, moves or alienates the seized property placed under his custody.

###### Article 812-42 :

In application of Article 128 of the Uniform Act on the organization of simplified recovery procedures and enforcement methods, is punished by imprisonment from one to six months and a fine from two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs the auctioneer or any other auxiliary of justice in charge of the sale who receives a sum above the bid.

###### Article 812-43 :

In application of Article 231 paragraph 1, 5e of the Uniform Act on the organization of simplified recovery procedures and enforcement methods, is punished by imprisonment from one to five years and a fine from one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, the holder who, except in the cases provided for in Article 103 of the said Act, moves or alienates the seized property placed under his custody.

###### Section 6 : Des peines applicables aux infractions prévues par l'acte

uniforme relatif au droit comptable et à l'information financière

###### Article 812-44 :

In application of Article 111 of the Uniform Act on the organization and harmonization of company accounts are punished by imprisonment from three months to three years and a fine from one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, individual entrepreneurs and corporate officers who:

˗ for each financial year, do not draw up the inventory and do not prepare the annual financial statements as well as, where applicable, the management report and the social balance sheet;

˗ knowingly prepare and communicate financial statements that do not present a true and fair view of the assets, financial position and result of the financial year.

The court seized may, moreover, pronounce the prohibition from exercising the functions of manager of a Limited Liability Company (SARL), Director, Chairman and Chief Executive Officer, Chief Executive Officer, General Administrator or Deputy Director Administrator for a period that cannot exceed five years.

###### Section 7 : Des peines applicables aux infractions prévues par l'acte

uniforme relatif au droit des sociétés coopératives

###### Article 812-45 :

In application of Article 386 of the Uniform Act relating to cooperative society law, is punishable by imprisonment from three months to three years and a fine from two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to one million

(1,000,000) CFA francs, any person who, without being authorized in accordance with the legal and regulatory provisions applicable to this type of grouping, improperly uses the expressions cooperative societies, union of cooperative societies, federation of cooperative societies or confederation of cooperative societies, accompanied by any qualifier, as well as all designations likely to suggest that it concerns one of the groupings cited in this article.

## LIVRE IX : MISCELLANEOUS AND FINAL PROVISIONS

###### Article 900-1 :

Death penalty sentences pronounced under the previous law are automatically commuted to life imprisonment.

###### Article 900-2 :

Is repealed Law No. 043/96/ADP of 13 November 1996 establishing the criminal code. Are also repealed:

˗ Articles 736 and 737 of Ordinance 68-7 of 21 February 1968 establishing the code of criminal procedure;

˗ Articles 44 to 72, 82 of Law No. 017-99/AN of 29 April 1999 establishing the drug code;

˗ Articles 1 and 2 of Law No. 007-2004 of 06 April 2004 on the administration of community service work in Burkina Faso; repression of acts of vandalism committed during demonstrations on public roads; fight against trafficking in persons and related practices;

˗ Articles 1 to 4, 10, 16 to 22 of Law No. 17-2009/AN of 05 May 2009 on the repression of serious banditry;

˗ Articles 2 to 12, 14 of Law No. 060-2009/AN of 17 December 2009 on the repression of acts of terrorism in Burkina Faso;

˗ Articles 1 to 21 and 23 to 29 of Law No. 11-2014/AN of 17 April 2014 on the repression of the sale of children, child prostitution and child pornography;

˗ Articles 87, 116 paragraph 4 and 121 of Law No. 015-2014/AN of 13 May 2014 on the protection of children in conflict with the law or in danger;

˗ Articles 1 to 11, 13, 14 to 16, 25 and 40 to 42 of Law No. 022-2014/AN of 27 May 2014 on the prevention and repression of torture and related practices;

˗ Articles 3, 42 to 86 of Law No. 04-2015/CNT of 03 March 2015 on the prevention and repression of corruption in Burkina Faso;

˗ Articles 5, 8 to 14, 18 and 19 of Law No. 061-2015/CNT of 06 September 2015 on the prevention, repression and reparation of violence against women and girls and care for victims;

˗ Articles 2, 2 bis, 13, 15 to 15 quinquiès of Law No. 084-2015/CNT of 17 December 2015 amending Law No. 060-2009/AN of 17 December 2009 on the repression of acts of terrorism in Burkina Faso. Are finally repealed all previous contrary provisions.

###### Article 900-3 :

This law shall be executed as law of the State.

Thus done and deliberated in public session in Ouagadougou, on 31 May 2018
